IROS: A Dual-Process Architecture for Real-Time VLM-Based Indoor Navigation

Abstract (English): Indoor mobile robot navigation requires fast responsiveness and robust semantic understanding, yet existing methods struggle to provide both. Classical geometric approaches such as SLAM offer reliable localization but depend on detailed maps and cannot interpret human-targeted cues (e.g., signs, room numbers) essential for indoor reasoning. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models introduce semantic grounding but remain strictly reactive, basing decisions only on visible frames and failing to anticipate unseen intersections or reason about distant textual cues. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide richer contextual inference but suffer from high computational latency, making them unsuitable for real-time operation on embedded platforms. In this work, we present IROS, a real-time navigation framework that combines VLM-level contextual reasoning with the efficiency of lightweight perceptual modules on low-cost, on-device hardware. Inspired by Dual Process Theory, IROS separates fast reflexive decisions (System One) from slow deliberative reasoning (System Two), invoking the VLM only when necessary. Furthermore, by augmenting compact VLMs with spatial and textual cues, IROS delivers robust, human-like navigation with minimal latency. Across five real-world buildings, IROS improves decision accuracy and reduces latency by 66% compared to continuous VLM-based navigation.

摘要（中文）: 室内移动机器人导航需要快速响应和强大的语义理解能力，但现有方法难以兼顾两者。经典的几何方法，如SLAM，提供可靠的定位，但依赖于详细的地图，且无法解读室内推理所需的人工提示（如标志、房间号）。视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型引入了语义基础，但仍然是严格的反应式，决策仅基于可见帧，无法预测不可见的交叉路口或推理远处文本提示。视觉-语言模型（VLM）提供更丰富的上下文推断，但计算延迟较高，不适合在嵌入式平台上进行实时操作。在这项工作中，我们提出 IROS，一种实时导航框架，它将 VLM 级别的上下文推理与低成本、片上硬件上的轻量级感知模块的效率相结合。受到双重加工理论的启发，IROS 将快速反射性决策（系统一）与慢速审慎性推理（系统二）分开，仅在必要时调用 VLM。此外，通过利用空间和文本提示增强紧凑型 VLM，IROS 以最小的延迟提供稳健的、类人导航。在五个真实建筑中，与连续的基于 VLM 的导航相比，IROS 将决策准确性提高了，并将延迟降低了 66%。

TLDR: The paper introduces IROS, a dual-process architecture for real-time indoor robot navigation that combines the contextual reasoning of VLMs with efficient lightweight perceptual modules, achieving improved accuracy and reduced latency.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了IROS，一种双过程架构，用于实时室内机器人导航，它结合了VLM的上下文推理和高效的轻量级感知模块，从而提高了准确性并降低了延迟。

Authors: Joonhee Lee, Hyunseung Shin, Jeonggil Ko

Drive-KD: Multi-Teacher Distillation for VLMs in Autonomous Driving

Abstract (English): Autonomous driving is an important and safety-critical task, and recent advances in LLMs/VLMs have opened new possibilities for reasoning and planning in this domain. However, large models demand substantial GPU memory and exhibit high inference latency, while conventional supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often struggles to bridge the capability gaps of small models. To address these limitations, we propose Drive-KD, a framework that decomposes autonomous driving into a "perception-reasoning-planning" triad and transfers these capabilities via knowledge distillation. We identify layer-specific attention as the distillation signal to construct capability-specific single-teacher models that outperform baselines. Moreover, we unify these single-teacher settings into a multi-teacher distillation framework and introduce asymmetric gradient projection to mitigate cross-capability gradient conflicts. Extensive evaluations validate the generalization of our method across diverse model families and scales. Experiments show that our distilled InternVL3-1B model, with ~42 times less GPU memory and ~11.4 times higher throughput, achieves better overall performance than the pretrained 78B model from the same family on DriveBench, and surpasses GPT-5.1 on the planning dimension, providing insights toward efficient autonomous driving VLMs.

摘要（中文）: 自动驾驶是一项重要且对安全性至关重要的任务，LLM/VLM 的最新进展为该领域的推理和规划开辟了新的可能性。然而，大型模型需要大量的 GPU 内存并表现出高推理延迟，而传统的监督微调 (SFT) 通常难以弥补小型模型的能力差距。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了 Drive-KD，一个将自动驾驶分解为“感知-推理-规划”三元组并通过知识蒸馏传递这些能力的框架。我们确定了层特定的注意力作为蒸馏信号，以构建优于基线的特定于能力的单教师模型。此外，我们将这些单教师设置统一到一个多教师蒸馏框架中，并引入非对称梯度投影来减轻跨能力梯度冲突。广泛的评估验证了我们方法在不同模型家族和规模上的泛化能力。实验表明，我们蒸馏后的 InternVL3-1B 模型，占用 GPU 内存减少约 42 倍，吞吐量提高约 11.4 倍，在 DriveBench 上实现了比来自同一系列的预训练 78B 模型更好的整体性能，并在规划维度上超越了 GPT-5.1，为高效的自动驾驶 VLM 提供了思路。

TLDR: The paper introduces Drive-KD, a multi-teacher knowledge distillation framework for VLMs in autonomous driving, achieving significant efficiency gains without sacrificing performance by distilling knowledge from large VLMs into smaller models using a perception-reasoning-planning decomposition and asymmetric gradient projection.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了Drive-KD，一个用于自动驾驶中视觉语言模型的多教师知识蒸馏框架。该框架通过将自动驾驶分解为"感知-推理-规划"三元组并使用非对称梯度投影，在不牺牲性能的前提下，通过将大型视觉语言模型的知识蒸馏到较小模型中，实现了显著的效率提升。

Authors: Weitong Lian, Zecong Tang, Haoran Li, Tianjian Gao, Yifei Wang, Zixu Wang, Lingyi Meng, Tengju Ru, Zhejun Cui, Yichen Zhu, Hangshuo Cao, Qi Kang, Tianxing Chen, Yusen Qin, Kaixuan Wang, Yu Zhang

WorldBench: Disambiguating Physics for Diagnostic Evaluation of World Models

Abstract (English): Recent advances in generative foundational models, often termed "world models," have propelled interest in applying them to critical tasks like robotic planning and autonomous system training. For reliable deployment, these models must exhibit high physical fidelity, accurately simulating real-world dynamics. Existing physics-based video benchmarks, however, suffer from entanglement, where a single test simultaneously evaluates multiple physical laws and concepts, fundamentally limiting their diagnostic capability. We introduce WorldBench, a novel video-based benchmark specifically designed for concept-specific, disentangled evaluation, allowing us to rigorously isolate and assess understanding of a single physical concept or law at a time. To make WorldBench comprehensive, we design benchmarks at two different levels: 1) an evaluation of intuitive physical understanding with concepts such as object permanence or scale/perspective, and 2) an evaluation of low-level physical constants and material properties such as friction coefficients or fluid viscosity. When SOTA video-based world models are evaluated on WorldBench, we find specific patterns of failure in particular physics concepts, with all tested models lacking the physical consistency required to generate reliable real-world interactions. Through its concept-specific evaluation, WorldBench offers a more nuanced and scalable framework for rigorously evaluating the physical reasoning capabilities of video generation and world models, paving the way for more robust and generalizable world-model-driven learning.

摘要（中文）: 生成式基础模型，通常被称为“世界模型”，近来的进展推动了人们将其应用于机器人规划和自动驾驶系统训练等关键任务的兴趣。为了可靠部署，这些模型必须展现出高度的物理逼真度，准确模拟真实世界的动力学。然而，现有的基于物理的视频基准测试存在纠缠问题，即单个测试同时评估多个物理定律和概念，从根本上限制了它们的诊断能力。我们引入了 WorldBench，一种新型的基于视频的基准测试，专门设计用于概念特定的、解耦的评估，从而使我们能够严格地隔离和评估对单个物理概念或定律的理解。为了使 WorldBench 更具全面性，我们设计了两个不同级别的基准测试：1) 对直观物理理解的评估，涉及物体永久性或尺度/透视等概念；2) 对低级物理常数和材料属性的评估，涉及摩擦系数或流体粘度等。当在 WorldBench 上评估 SOTA 基于视频的世界模型时，我们发现特定物理概念中存在特定的失败模式，所有被测模型都缺乏生成可靠的真实世界交互所需的物理一致性。通过其概念特定的评估，WorldBench 为严格评估视频生成和世界模型的物理推理能力提供了一个更细致和可扩展的框架，为更稳健和更具泛化能力的世界模型驱动学习铺平了道路。

TLDR: The paper introduces WorldBench, a new video-based benchmark for evaluating the physical reasoning capabilities of world models, designed to disentangle physics concepts for more diagnostic evaluation. Experiments reveal current SOTA models exhibit deficiencies in physical consistency.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一个名为WorldBench的新型视频基准测试，用于评估世界模型的物理推理能力。该基准测试旨在解耦物理概念，从而实现更具诊断性的评估。实验表明，目前最先进的模型在物理一致性方面存在不足。

Authors: Rishi Upadhyay, Howard Zhang, Jim Solomon, Ayush Agrawal, Pranay Boreddy, Shruti Satya Narayana, Yunhao Ba, Alex Wong, Celso M de Melo, Achuta Kadambi

PocketDP3: Efficient Pocket-Scale 3D Visuomotor Policy

Abstract (English): Recently, 3D vision-based diffusion policies have shown strong capability in learning complex robotic manipulation skills. However, a common architectural mismatch exists in these models: a tiny yet efficient point-cloud encoder is often paired with a massive decoder. Given a compact scene representation, we argue that this may lead to substantial parameter waste in the decoder. Motivated by this observation, we propose PocketDP3, a pocket-scale 3D diffusion policy that replaces the heavy conditional U-Net decoder used in prior methods with a lightweight Diffusion Mixer (DiM) built on MLP-Mixer blocks. This architecture enables efficient fusion across temporal and channel dimensions, significantly reducing model size. Notably, without any additional consistency distillation techniques, our method supports two-step inference without sacrificing performance, improving practicality for real-time deployment. Across three simulation benchmarks--RoboTwin2.0, Adroit, and MetaWorld--PocketDP3 achieves state-of-the-art performance with fewer than 1% of the parameters of prior methods, while also accelerating inference. Real-world experiments further demonstrate the practicality and transferability of our method in real-world settings. Code will be released.

摘要（中文）: 近来，基于3D视觉的扩散策略在学习复杂的机器人操作技能上展现出强大的能力。然而，在这些模型中存在一个常见的架构不匹配问题：一个微型且高效的点云编码器通常与一个庞大的解码器配对。考虑到场景表示的紧凑性，我们认为这可能导致解码器中大量的参数浪费。受此观察的驱动，我们提出了PocketDP3，一个口袋规模的3D扩散策略，它将先前方法中使用的重型条件U-Net解码器替换为基于MLP-Mixer块构建的轻量级扩散混合器(DiM)。这种架构能够实现跨时间和通道维度的高效融合，显著减小模型尺寸。值得注意的是，在没有任何额外的自洽蒸馏技术的情况下，我们的方法支持两步推理而不会牺牲性能，从而提高了实时部署的实用性。在三个模拟基准测试——RoboTwin2.0、Adroit和MetaWorld——中，PocketDP3实现了最先进的性能，参数量不到先前方法的1%，同时也加速了推理。真实世界的实验进一步证明了我们的方法在现实环境中的实用性和可迁移性。代码将会发布。

TLDR: PocketDP3 proposes a lightweight diffusion policy for 3D visuomotor control, achieving state-of-the-art performance with significantly fewer parameters and faster inference through a Diffusion Mixer architecture. It shows strong performance both in simulation and real-world experiments.

简要说明: PocketDP3 提出了一种轻量级的3D视觉运动控制扩散策略，通过扩散混合器（Diffusion Mixer）架构，以显著更少的参数和更快的推理速度实现了最先进的性能。该方法在仿真和真实世界实验中都表现出强大的性能。

Authors: Jinhao Zhang, Zhexuan Zhou, Huizhe Li, Yichen Lai, Wenlong Xia, Haoming Song, Youmin Gong, Jie Me

Causal World Modeling for Robot Control

Abstract (English): This work highlights that video world modeling, alongside vision-language pre-training, establishes a fresh and independent foundation for robot learning. Intuitively, video world models provide the ability to imagine the near future by understanding the causality between actions and visual dynamics. Inspired by this, we introduce LingBot-VA, an autoregressive diffusion framework that learns frame prediction and policy execution simultaneously. Our model features three carefully crafted designs: (1) a shared latent space, integrating vision and action tokens, driven by a Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture, (2) a closed-loop rollout mechanism, allowing for ongoing acquisition of environmental feedback with ground-truth observations, (3) an asynchronous inference pipeline, parallelizing action prediction and motor execution to support efficient control. We evaluate our model on both simulation benchmarks and real-world scenarios, where it shows significant promise in long-horizon manipulation, data efficiency in post-training, and strong generalizability to novel configurations. The code and model are made publicly available to facilitate the community.

摘要（中文）: 本文强调，视频世界建模与视觉-语言预训练相结合，为机器人学习建立了一个全新的、独立的基石。直观地讲，视频世界模型通过理解动作和视觉动态之间的因果关系，提供了设想近期未来的能力。受此启发，我们提出了LingBot-VA，一个自回归扩散框架，该框架同时学习帧预测和策略执行。我们的模型具有三个精心设计的部分：(1)一个共享的潜在空间，集成了视觉和动作token，并由混合Transformer (MoT)架构驱动；(2)一个闭环展开机制，允许持续获取环境反馈以及真实观测；(3)一个异步推理流水线，并行化动作预测和电机执行，以支持高效控制。我们在模拟基准和真实世界场景中评估了我们的模型，结果表明其在长程操作、后训练中的数据效率以及对新颖配置的强大泛化性方面具有显著潜力。代码和模型已公开，以促进社区发展。

TLDR: The paper introduces LingBot-VA, an autoregressive diffusion framework for robot control that learns frame prediction and policy execution simultaneously using a video world model with vision-language pre-training. It demonstrates strong performance in long-horizon manipulation and generalization.

简要说明: 本文介绍了LingBot-VA，一个用于机器人控制的自回归扩散框架，它利用视频世界模型和视觉-语言预训练，同时学习帧预测和策略执行。该模型在长程操作和泛化方面表现出强大的性能。

Authors: Lin Li, Qihang Zhang, Yiming Luo, Shuai Yang, Ruilin Wang, Fei Han, Mingrui Yu, Zelin Gao, Nan Xue, Xing Zhu, Yujun Shen, Yinghao Xu

MoE-ACT: Improving Surgical Imitation Learning Policies through Supervised Mixture-of-Experts

Abstract (English): Imitation learning has achieved remarkable success in robotic manipulation, yet its application to surgical robotics remains challenging due to data scarcity, constrained workspaces, and the need for an exceptional level of safety and predictability. We present a supervised Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture designed for phase-structured surgical manipulation tasks, which can be added on top of any autonomous policy. Unlike prior surgical robot learning approaches that rely on multi-camera setups or thousands of demonstrations, we show that a lightweight action decoder policy like Action Chunking Transformer (ACT) can learn complex, long-horizon manipulation from less than 150 demonstrations using solely stereo endoscopic images, when equipped with our architecture. We evaluate our approach on the collaborative surgical task of bowel grasping and retraction, where a robot assistant interprets visual cues from a human surgeon, executes targeted grasping on deformable tissue, and performs sustained retraction. We benchmark our method against state-of-the-art Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models and the standard ACT baseline. Our results show that generalist VLAs fail to acquire the task entirely, even under standard in-distribution conditions. Furthermore, while standard ACT achieves moderate success in-distribution, adopting a supervised MoE architecture significantly boosts its performance, yielding higher success rates in-distribution and demonstrating superior robustness in out-of-distribution scenarios, including novel grasp locations, reduced illumination, and partial occlusions. Notably, it generalizes to unseen testing viewpoints and also transfers zero-shot to ex vivo porcine tissue without additional training, offering a promising pathway toward in vivo deployment. To support this, we present qualitative preliminary results of policy roll-outs during in vivo porcine surgery.

摘要（中文）: 模仿学习在机器人操控领域取得了显著成功，但由于数据稀缺、工作空间受限以及对极高安全性和可预测性的需求，其在外科机器人领域的应用仍然具有挑战性。我们提出了一种用于阶段性外科操作任务的监督式混合专家（MoE）架构，可以添加到任何自主策略之上。与先前依赖多摄像头设置或数千次演示的外科机器人学习方法不同，我们证明，当配备我们的架构时，诸如动作分块Transformer（ACT）这样的轻量级动作解码器策略仅使用立体内窥镜图像，就可以从不到150次演示中学习到复杂的、长时程操控。我们在肠道抓取和牵开的协作手术任务中评估了我们的方法，其中机器人助手解读来自人类外科医生的视觉提示，对可变形组织执行有针对性的抓取，并执行持续的牵开。我们将我们的方法与最先进的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型和标准ACT基线进行比较。我们的结果表明，即使在标准同分布条件下，通用VLA也未能完全掌握该任务。此外，虽然标准ACT在同分布条件下取得了一定的成功，但采用监督式MoE架构显著提高了其性能，在同分布条件下产生了更高的成功率，并在分布外场景（包括新的抓取位置、降低的照明和局部遮挡）中表现出卓越的鲁棒性。值得注意的是，它可以泛化到未见过的测试视角，并且无需额外训练即可零样本迁移到离体猪组织，为体内部署提供了一条有希望的途径。为了支持这一点，我们展示了在体内猪手术期间策略执行的初步定性结果。

TLDR: This paper introduces a supervised Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture to enhance the robustness and performance of imitation learning policies, specifically Action Chunking Transformer (ACT), for surgical robot tasks using limited demonstration data and stereo endoscopic images.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种监督式混合专家（MoE）架构，旨在增强模仿学习策略（尤其是 Action Chunking Transformer (ACT)）在外科机器人任务中的鲁棒性和性能，该方法使用有限的演示数据和立体内窥镜图像。

Authors: Lorenzo Mazza, Ariel Rodriguez, Rayan Younis, Martin Lelis, Ortrun Hellig, Chenpan Li, Sebastian Bodenstedt, Martin Wagner, Stefanie Speidel

Information Filtering via Variational Regularization for Robot Manipulation

Abstract (English): Diffusion-based visuomotor policies built on 3D visual representations have achieved strong performance in learning complex robotic skills. However, most existing methods employ an oversized denoising decoder. While increasing model capacity can improve denoising, empirical evidence suggests that it also introduces redundancy and noise in intermediate feature blocks. Crucially, we find that randomly masking backbone features at inference time (without changing training) can improve performance, confirming the presence of task-irrelevant noise in intermediate features. To this end, we propose Variational Regularization (VR), a lightweight module that imposes a timestep-conditioned Gaussian over backbone features and applies a KL-divergence regularizer, forming an adaptive information bottleneck. Extensive experiments on three simulation benchmarks (RoboTwin2.0, Adroit, and MetaWorld) show that, compared to the baseline DP3, our approach improves the success rate by 6.1% on RoboTwin2.0 and by 4.1% on Adroit and MetaWorld, achieving new state-of-the-art results. Real-world experiments further demonstrate that our method performs well in practical deployments. Code will released.

摘要（中文）: 基于扩散的三维视觉表征的视觉运动策略，在学习复杂的机器人技能方面取得了显著的成果。然而，大多数现有方法都采用了过大的去噪解码器。虽然增加模型容量可以提高去噪性能，但经验证据表明，这也会在中间特征块中引入冗余和噪声。关键是，我们发现，在推理时随机掩蔽骨干网络特征（无需改变训练）可以提高性能，这证实了中间特征中存在与任务无关的噪声。为此，我们提出了一种变分正则化(VR)方法，该方法是一个轻量级模块，对骨干网络特征施加了时间步条件的高斯分布，并应用了KL散度正则化项，从而形成了一个自适应的信息瓶颈。在三个模拟基准(RoboTwin2.0、Adroit和MetaWorld)上的大量实验表明，与基线DP3相比，我们的方法在RoboTwin2.0上的成功率提高了6.1%，在Adroit和MetaWorld上的成功率提高了4.1%，取得了新的当前最佳结果。真实世界的实验进一步表明，我们的方法在实际部署中表现良好。代码即将发布。

TLDR: This paper proposes a Variational Regularization (VR) module for diffusion-based visuomotor policies that adaptively filters task-irrelevant noise in intermediate features, leading to improved performance on robot manipulation tasks in simulation and real-world settings.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种用于基于扩散的视觉运动策略的变分正则化（VR）模块，该模块自适应地过滤中间特征中与任务无关的噪声，从而提高了在模拟和实际环境中机器人操作任务的性能。

Authors: Jinhao Zhang, Wenlong Xia, Yaojia Wang, Zhexuan Zhou, Huizhe Li, Yichen Lai, Haoming Song, Youmin Gong, Jie Me

LLM-Driven Scenario-Aware Planning for Autonomous Driving

Abstract (English): Hybrid planner switching framework (HPSF) for autonomous driving needs to reconcile high-speed driving efficiency with safe maneuvering in dense traffic. Existing HPSF methods often fail to make reliable mode transitions or sustain efficient driving in congested environments, owing to heuristic scene recognition and low-frequency control updates. To address the limitation, this paper proposes LAP, a large language model (LLM) driven, adaptive planning method, which switches between high-speed driving in low-complexity scenes and precise driving in high-complexity scenes, enabling high qualities of trajectory generation through confined gaps. This is achieved by leveraging LLM for scene understanding and integrating its inference into the joint optimization of mode configuration and motion planning. The joint optimization is solved using tree-search model predictive control and alternating minimization. We implement LAP by Python in Robot Operating System (ROS). High-fidelity simulation results show that the proposed LAP outperforms other benchmarks in terms of both driving time and success rate.

摘要（中文）: 用于自动驾驶的混合规划器切换框架 (HPSF) 需要在高速度驾驶效率和密集交通中的安全操控之间取得平衡。现有的 HPSF 方法通常由于启发式场景识别和低频率控制更新而无法进行可靠的模式转换或在拥堵环境中维持高效驾驶。为了解决这一局限性，本文提出了一种大型语言模型 (LLM) 驱动的自适应规划方法 LAP，该方法在高复杂度场景中切换到精确驾驶，并在低复杂度场景中切换到高速驾驶，从而能够通过狭窄间隙生成高质量轨迹。这通过利用 LLM 进行场景理解，并将其推理集成到模式配置和运动规划的联合优化中来实现。该联合优化使用树搜索模型预测控制和交替最小化方法求解。我们在机器人操作系统 (ROS) 中使用 Python 实现了 LAP。高保真仿真结果表明，所提出的 LAP 在驾驶时间和成功率方面均优于其他基准方法。

TLDR: This paper introduces LAP, an LLM-driven adaptive planning method for autonomous driving that uses LLMs for scene understanding and joint optimization of mode configuration and motion planning to improve driving efficiency and safety in complex scenarios.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了 LAP，一种由 LLM 驱动的自动驾驶自适应规划方法，该方法利用 LLM 进行场景理解并对模式配置和运动规划进行联合优化，以提高在复杂场景中的驾驶效率和安全性。

Authors: He Li, Zhaowei Chen, Rui Gao, Guoliang Li, Qi Hao, Shuai Wang, Chengzhong Xu

Disentangling perception and reasoning for improving data efficiency in learning cloth manipulation without demonstrations

Abstract (English): Cloth manipulation is a ubiquitous task in everyday life, but it remains an open challenge for robotics. The difficulties in developing cloth manipulation policies are attributed to the high-dimensional state space, complex dynamics, and high propensity to self-occlusion exhibited by fabrics. As analytical methods have not been able to provide robust and general manipulation policies, reinforcement learning (RL) is considered a promising approach to these problems. However, to address the large state space and complex dynamics, data-based methods usually rely on large models and long training times. The resulting computational cost significantly hampers the development and adoption of these methods. Additionally, due to the challenge of robust state estimation, garment manipulation policies often adopt an end-to-end learning approach with workspace images as input. While this approach enables a conceptually straightforward sim-to-real transfer via real-world fine-tuning, it also incurs a significant computational cost by training agents on a highly lossy representation of the environment state. This paper questions this common design choice by exploring an efficient and modular approach to RL for cloth manipulation. We show that, through careful design choices, model size and training time can be significantly reduced when learning in simulation. Furthermore, we demonstrate how the resulting simulation-trained model can be transferred to the real world. We evaluate our approach on the SoftGym benchmark and achieve significant performance improvements over available baselines on our task, while using a substantially smaller model.

摘要（中文）: 布料操作是日常生活中的常见任务，但对于机器人来说仍然是一个公开的挑战。布料操作策略开发上的困难归因于布料所表现出的高维状态空间、复杂动力学和高自遮挡倾向。 由于分析方法无法提供稳健且通用的操作策略，强化学习（RL）被认为是解决这些问题的一种有前景的方法。 然而，为了解决庞大的状态空间和复杂的动力学问题，基于数据的方法通常依赖于大型模型和长时间的训练。 由此产生的计算成本极大地阻碍了这些方法的开发和应用。 此外，由于鲁棒状态估计的挑战，服装操作策略通常采用以工作空间图像作为输入的端到端学习方法。 虽然这种方法可以通过真实世界的微调实现概念上直接的从仿真到真实的迁移，但它也通过在高损失环境状态表示上训练代理而产生巨大的计算成本。 本文通过探索一种高效且模块化的布料操作 RL 方法，对这种常见的设计选择提出质疑。 我们表明，通过谨慎的设计选择，可以在仿真学习中显著减少模型尺寸和训练时间。 此外，我们展示了如何将由此产生的仿真训练模型迁移到真实世界。 我们在 SoftGym 基准测试中评估了我们的方法，并在我们的任务上实现了相对于现有基线的显著性能提升，同时使用了明显更小的模型。

TLDR: This paper presents a modular reinforcement learning approach for cloth manipulation that disentangles perception and reasoning, reducing model size and training time in simulation, and demonstrating sim-to-real transfer.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种用于布料操作的模块化强化学习方法，该方法解耦了感知和推理，减少了在仿真中的模型大小和训练时间，并展示了从仿真到真实的迁移。

Authors: Donatien Delehelle, Fei Chen, Darwin Caldwell

CoFreeVLA: Collision-Free Dual-Arm Manipulation via Vision-Language-Action Model and Risk Estimation

Abstract (English): Vision Language Action (VLA) models enable instruction following manipulation, yet dualarm deployment remains unsafe due to under modeled selfcollisions between arms and grasped objects. We introduce CoFreeVLA, which augments an endtoend VLA with a short horizon selfcollision risk estimator that predicts collision likelihood from proprioception, visual embeddings, and planned actions. The estimator gates risky commands, recovers to safe states via risk-guided adjustments, and shapes policy refinement for safer rollouts. It is pre-trained with model-based collision labels and posttrained on real robot rollouts for calibration. On five bimanual tasks with the PiPER robot arm, CoFreeVLA reduces selfcollisions and improves success rates versus RDT and APEX.

摘要（中文）: 视觉语言动作（VLA）模型能够实现指令跟随操作，但由于对双臂之间及抓握物体之间的自碰撞建模不足，双臂部署仍然不安全。我们引入了CoFreeVLA，它通过一个短时程的自碰撞风险估计器来增强端到端的VLA，该估计器从本体感受、视觉嵌入和规划动作中预测碰撞可能性。该估计器门控有风险的指令，通过风险引导的调整恢复到安全状态，并塑造策略精炼以实现更安全的轨迹。它使用基于模型的碰撞标签进行预训练，并在真实机器人轨迹上进行后训练以进行校准。在使用PiPER机器人手臂的五个双手动任务中，CoFreeVLA降低了自碰撞次数，并提高了相对于RDT和APEX的成功率。

TLDR: CoFreeVLA addresses the challenge of self-collisions in dual-arm robot manipulation by augmenting a Vision-Language-Action model with a collision risk estimator, resulting in safer and more successful bimanual task completion.

简要说明: CoFreeVLA 通过使用碰撞风险评估器增强视觉-语言-动作模型来解决双臂机器人操作中的自碰撞问题，从而实现更安全和更成功的双手任务。

Authors: Xuanran Zhai, Binkai Ou, Yemin Wang, Hui Yi Leong, Qiaojun Yu, Ce Hao, Yaohua Liu

AIR-VLA: Vision-Language-Action Systems for Aerial Manipulation

Abstract (English): While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved remarkable success in ground-based embodied intelligence, their application to Aerial Manipulation Systems (AMS) remains a largely unexplored frontier. The inherent characteristics of AMS, including floating-base dynamics, strong coupling between the UAV and the manipulator, and the multi-step, long-horizon nature of operational tasks, pose severe challenges to existing VLA paradigms designed for static or 2D mobile bases. To bridge this gap, we propose AIR-VLA, the first VLA benchmark specifically tailored for aerial manipulation. We construct a physics-based simulation environment and release a high-quality multimodal dataset comprising 3000 manually teleoperated demonstrations, covering base manipulation, object & spatial understanding, semantic reasoning, and long-horizon planning. Leveraging this platform, we systematically evaluate mainstream VLA models and state-of-the-art VLM models. Our experiments not only validate the feasibility of transferring VLA paradigms to aerial systems but also, through multi-dimensional metrics tailored to aerial tasks, reveal the capabilities and boundaries of current models regarding UAV mobility, manipulator control, and high-level planning. AIR-VLA establishes a standardized testbed and data foundation for future research in general-purpose aerial robotics. The resource of AIR-VLA will be available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AIR-VLA-dataset-B5CC/.

摘要（中文）: 视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型在地面具身智能方面取得了显著成功，但其在空中操作系统(AMS)中的应用仍是一个很大程度上未被探索的前沿领域。AMS的固有特性，包括漂浮基座动力学、无人机与机械臂之间的强耦合，以及操作任务的多步骤、长时程特性，对专为静态或二维移动基座设计的现有VLA范式提出了严峻挑战。为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了AIR-VLA，这是首个专门为空中操作定制的VLA基准。我们构建了一个基于物理的仿真环境，并发布了一个高质量的多模态数据集，其中包含3000个手动遥控演示，涵盖基座操作、物体与空间理解、语义推理和长时程规划。利用该平台，我们系统地评估了主流VLA模型和最先进的VLM模型。我们的实验不仅验证了将VLA范式转移到空中系统的可行性，而且通过专为空中任务定制的多维度指标，揭示了当前模型在无人机移动性、机械臂控制和高层规划方面的能力和局限性。AIR-VLA为通用空中机器人领域的未来研究建立了标准化的测试平台和数据基础。AIR-VLA的资源将在https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AIR-VLA-dataset-B5CC/上提供。

TLDR: The paper introduces AIR-VLA, a novel benchmark and dataset for Vision-Language-Action models tailored for aerial manipulation, addressing the challenges of applying VLA models to complex aerial systems and providing a standardized testbed for future research.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了AIR-VLA，这是一个专为空中操作设计的视觉-语言-动作模型的新基准和数据集，旨在解决将VLA模型应用于复杂空中系统的挑战，并为未来研究提供标准化测试平台。

Authors: Jianli Sun, Bin Tian, Qiyao Zhang, Chengxiang Li, Zihan Song, Zhiyong Cui, Yisheng Lv, Yonglin Tian

EmboCoach-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents on Developing Embodied Robots

Abstract (English): The field of Embodied AI is witnessing a rapid evolution toward general-purpose robotic systems, fueled by high-fidelity simulation and large-scale data collection. However, this scaling capability remains severely bottlenecked by a reliance on labor-intensive manual oversight from intricate reward shaping to hyperparameter tuning across heterogeneous backends. Inspired by LLMs' success in software automation and science discovery, we introduce \textsc{EmboCoach-Bench}, a benchmark evaluating the capacity of LLM agents to autonomously engineer embodied policies. Spanning 32 expert-curated RL and IL tasks, our framework posits executable code as the universal interface. We move beyond static generation to assess a dynamic closed-loop workflow, where agents leverage environment feedback to iteratively draft, debug, and optimize solutions, spanning improvements from physics-informed reward design to policy architectures such as diffusion policies. Extensive evaluations yield three critical insights: (1) autonomous agents can qualitatively surpass human-engineered baselines by 26.5\% in average success rate; (2) agentic workflow with environment feedback effectively strengthens policy development and substantially narrows the performance gap between open-source and proprietary models; and (3) agents exhibit self-correction capabilities for pathological engineering cases, successfully resurrecting task performance from near-total failures through iterative simulation-in-the-loop debugging. Ultimately, this work establishes a foundation for self-evolving embodied intelligence, accelerating the paradigm shift from labor-intensive manual tuning to scalable, autonomous engineering in embodied AI field.

摘要（中文）: 具身智能正朝着通用型机器人系统快速演进，这得益于高保真模拟和大规模数据收集。然而，这种扩展能力仍然受到人工密集型监督的严重限制，从复杂的奖励塑造到跨异构后端的超参数调整皆需人工参与。受大型语言模型（LLM）在软件自动化和科学发现方面取得成功的启发，我们引入了 \textsc{EmboCoach-Bench}，一个评估 LLM 智能体自主设计具身策略的能力的基准。我们的框架涵盖了由专家策划的 32 个强化学习（RL）和模仿学习（IL）任务，并将可执行代码作为通用接口。我们超越了静态生成，评估了一种动态的闭环工作流程，其中智能体利用环境反馈来迭代地起草、调试和优化解决方案，涵盖了从物理信息奖励设计到扩散策略等策略架构的改进。广泛的评估产生了三个关键见解：（1）自主智能体在平均成功率方面可以定性地超过人工设计的基线 26.5%；（2）具有环境反馈的智能体工作流程有效地加强了策略开发，并显着缩小了开源模型和专有模型之间的性能差距；（3）智能体表现出自纠正病态工程案例的能力，通过迭代的模拟环调试，成功地从几乎完全失败的状态中恢复了任务性能。最终，这项工作为自我进化的具身智能奠定了基础，加速了具身智能领域从人工密集型手动调整到可扩展的自主工程的范式转变。

TLDR: The paper introduces EmboCoach-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating LLM agents' ability to autonomously develop embodied robot policies through a closed-loop workflow of code generation, debugging, and optimization, demonstrating performance exceeding human-engineered baselines.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了EmboCoach-Bench，一个用于评估LLM智能体自主开发具身机器人策略能力的基准。它采用闭环工作流程，包括代码生成、调试和优化，实验结果表明其性能超过了人工设计的基线。

Authors: Zixing Lei, Genjia Liu, Yuanshuo Zhang, Qipeng Liu, Chuan Wen, Shanghang Zhang, Wenzhao Lian, Siheng Chen

Training slow silicon neurons to control extremely fast robots with spiking reinforcement learning

Abstract (English): Air hockey demands split-second decisions at high puck velocities, a challenge we address with a compact network of spiking neurons running on a mixed-signal analog/digital neuromorphic processor. By co-designing hardware and learning algorithms, we train the system to achieve successful puck interactions through reinforcement learning in a remarkably small number of trials. The network leverages fixed random connectivity to capture the task's temporal structure and adopts a local e-prop learning rule in the readout layer to exploit event-driven activity for fast and efficient learning. The result is real-time learning with a setup comprising a computer and the neuromorphic chip in-the-loop, enabling practical training of spiking neural networks for robotic autonomous systems. This work bridges neuroscience-inspired hardware with real-world robotic control, showing that brain-inspired approaches can tackle fast-paced interaction tasks while supporting always-on learning in intelligent machines.

摘要（中文）: 空气曲棍球需要在高速冰球速度下做出瞬间决策，我们通过运行在混合信号模拟/数字神经形态处理器上的紧凑型脉冲神经元网络来应对这一挑战。通过协同设计硬件和学习算法，我们训练系统通过强化学习以极少的尝试次数成功实现冰球交互。该网络利用固定的随机连接来捕捉任务的时间结构，并在读出层采用局部e-prop学习规则来利用事件驱动的活动进行快速高效学习。结果是通过计算机和神经形态芯片在环的设置实现实时学习，从而能够对用于机器人自主系统的脉冲神经网络进行实际训练。这项工作将受神经科学启发的硬件与现实世界的机器人控制联系起来，表明受大脑启发的解决方案可以应对快节奏的交互任务，同时支持智能机器中的常时学习。

TLDR: This paper presents a spiking neural network implemented on neuromorphic hardware, trained with reinforcement learning to control an air hockey robot with fast learning and real-time performance.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种在神经形态硬件上实现的脉冲神经网络，通过强化学习进行训练，以控制气垫球机器人，具有快速学习和实时性能。

DexTac: Learning Contact-aware Visuotactile Policies via Hand-by-hand Teaching

Abstract (English): For contact-intensive tasks, the ability to generate policies that produce comprehensive tactile-aware motions is essential. However, existing data collection and skill learning systems for dexterous manipulation often suffer from low-dimensional tactile information. To address this limitation, we propose DexTac, a visuo-tactile manipulation learning framework based on kinesthetic teaching. DexTac captures multi-dimensional tactile data-including contact force distributions and spatial contact regions-directly from human demonstrations. By integrating these rich tactile modalities into a policy network, the resulting contact-aware agent enables a dexterous hand to autonomously select and maintain optimal contact regions during complex interactions. We evaluate our framework on a challenging unimanual injection task. Experimental results demonstrate that DexTac achieves a 91.67% success rate. Notably, in high-precision scenarios involving small-scale syringes, our approach outperforms force-only baselines by 31.67%. These results underscore that learning multi-dimensional tactile priors from human demonstrations is critical for achieving robust, human-like dexterous manipulation in contact-rich environments.

摘要（中文）: 对于接触密集型任务，生成能够产生全面触觉感知动作的策略至关重要。然而，目前灵巧操作的数据收集和技能学习系统往往受到低维触觉信息的限制。为了解决这一问题，我们提出DexTac，一种基于动觉示教的视觉-触觉操作学习框架。 DexTac直接从人类演示中捕获多维触觉数据——包括接触力分布和空间接触区域。 通过将这些丰富的触觉模态集成到策略网络中，由此产生的触觉感知代理能够使灵巧手在复杂交互过程中自主地选择和维持最佳接触区域。 我们在一个具有挑战性的单手注射任务上评估了我们的理论框架。 实验结果表明，DexTac的成功率达到了 91.67%。 特别是在涉及小型注射器的高精度场景中，我们的方法比仅使用力基线的性能高出 31.67%。 这些结果强调了从人类演示中学习多维触觉先验知识对于在接触丰富的环境中实现鲁棒的、类人的灵巧操作至关重要。

TLDR: DexTac uses kinesthetic teaching to learn contact-aware manipulation policies by integrating multi-dimensional tactile data from human demonstrations, achieving high success rates on a challenging injection task.

Authors: Xingyu Zhang, Chaofan Zhang, Boyue Zhang, Zhinan Peng, Shaowei Cui, Shuo Wang

Nimbus: A Unified Embodied Synthetic Data Generation Framework

Abstract (English): Scaling data volume and diversity is critical for generalizing embodied intelligence. While synthetic data generation offers a scalable alternative to expensive physical data acquisition, existing pipelines remain fragmented and task-specific. This isolation leads to significant engineering inefficiency and system instability, failing to support the sustained, high-throughput data generation required for foundation model training. To address these challenges, we present Nimbus, a unified synthetic data generation framework designed to integrate heterogeneous navigation and manipulation pipelines. Nimbus introduces a modular four-layer architecture featuring a decoupled execution model that separates trajectory planning, rendering, and storage into asynchronous stages. By implementing dynamic pipeline scheduling, global load balancing, distributed fault tolerance, and backend-specific rendering optimizations, the system maximizes resource utilization across CPU, GPU, and I/O resources. Our evaluation demonstrates that Nimbus achieves a 2-3X improvement in end-to-end throughput compared to unoptimized baselines and ensuring robust, long-term operation in large-scale distributed environments. This framework serves as the production backbone for the InternData suite, enabling seamless cross-domain data synthesis.

摘要（中文）: 扩展数据量和多样性对于通用具身智能至关重要。虽然合成数据生成为昂贵的物理数据采集提供了一种可扩展的替代方案，但现有的流程仍然是碎片化且特定于任务的。这种孤立性导致显著的工程效率低下和系统不稳定，无法支持基础模型训练所需的可持续、高吞吐量的数据生成。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了 Nimbus，一个统一的合成数据生成框架，旨在集成异构的导航和操作流程。 Nimbus 引入了一个模块化的四层架构，采用解耦执行模型，将轨迹规划、渲染和存储分离成异步阶段。通过实施动态流程调度、全局负载均衡、分布式容错和后端特定的渲染优化，该系统最大限度地利用了 CPU、GPU 和 I/O 资源。我们的评估表明，与未优化的基线相比，Nimbus 在端到端吞吐量方面实现了 2-3 倍的提升，并确保了大规模分布式环境中稳健的长期运行。此框架作为 InternData 套件的生产主干，实现了无缝的跨域数据合成。

TLDR: The paper introduces Nimbus, a unified framework for generating synthetic data for embodied AI, achieving 2-3x throughput improvement by integrating heterogeneous navigation and manipulation pipelines with a modular, distributed architecture.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了 Nimbus，一个用于生成具身人工智能合成数据的统一框架，通过集成异构导航和操作流程，并采用模块化、分布式架构，实现了 2-3 倍的吞吐量提升。

Spotlighting Task-Relevant Features: Object-Centric Representations for Better Generalization in Robotic Manipulation

Abstract (English): The generalization capabilities of robotic manipulation policies are heavily influenced by the choice of visual representations. Existing approaches typically rely on representations extracted from pre-trained encoders, using two dominant types of features: global features, which summarize an entire image via a single pooled vector, and dense features, which preserve a patch-wise embedding from the final encoder layer. While widely used, both feature types mix task-relevant and irrelevant information, leading to poor generalization under distribution shifts, such as changes in lighting, textures, or the presence of distractors. In this work, we explore an intermediate structured alternative: Slot-Based Object-Centric Representations (SBOCR), which group dense features into a finite set of object-like entities. This representation permits to naturally reduce the noise provided to the robotic manipulation policy while keeping enough information to efficiently perform the task. We benchmark a range of global and dense representations against intermediate slot-based representations, across a suite of simulated and real-world manipulation tasks ranging from simple to complex. We evaluate their generalization under diverse visual conditions, including changes in lighting, texture, and the presence of distractors. Our findings reveal that SBOCR-based policies outperform dense and global representation-based policies in generalization settings, even without task-specific pretraining. These insights suggest that SBOCR is a promising direction for designing visual systems that generalize effectively in dynamic, real-world robotic environments.

摘要（中文）: 机器人操作策略的泛化能力很大程度上受到视觉表征选择的影响。现有方法通常依赖于从预训练编码器中提取的表征，并采用两种主要的特征类型：全局特征（通过单个池化向量概括整个图像）和稠密特征（保留来自编码器最后一层的逐块嵌入）。虽然应用广泛，但两种特征类型都混合了任务相关和不相关的信息，导致在分布偏移下泛化能力较差，例如光照、纹理的变化或干扰物的存在。在这项工作中，我们探索了一种中间结构化的替代方案：基于槽位的以对象为中心的表征（SBOCR），它将稠密特征分组为有限的对象类实体集合。这种表征能够自然地减少提供给机器人操作策略的噪声，同时保持足够的信息以有效执行任务。我们在一系列从简单到复杂的模拟和真实世界操作任务中，针对中间slot-based表征，对一系列全局和稠密表征进行了基准测试。我们评估了它们在各种视觉条件下的泛化能力，包括光照、纹理的变化以及干扰物的存在。我们的研究结果表明，即使没有任务特定的预训练，基于SBOCR的策略在泛化设置中也优于基于稠密和全局表征的策略。这些见解表明，SBOCR是设计在动态、真实世界机器人环境中有效泛化的视觉系统的一个有希望的方向。

TLDR: This paper introduces Slot-Based Object-Centric Representations (SBOCR) for robotic manipulation, demonstrating improved generalization in simulated and real-world environments compared to global and dense feature representations, especially under distributional shifts.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种用于机器人操作的基于槽位的以对象为中心的表示方法（SBOCR），与全局和密集特征表示相比，它在模拟和真实环境中表现出更好的泛化能力，特别是在分布偏移的情况下。

Authors: Alexandre Chapin, Bruno Machado, Emmanuel Dellandréa, Liming Chen

DSCD-Nav: Dual-Stance Cooperative Debate for Object Navigation

Abstract (English): Adaptive navigation in unfamiliar indoor environments is crucial for household service robots. Despite advances in zero-shot perception and reasoning from vision-language models, existing navigation systems still rely on single-pass scoring at the decision layer, leading to overconfident long-horizon errors and redundant exploration. To tackle these problems, we propose Dual-Stance Cooperative Debate Navigation (DSCD-Nav), a decision mechanism that replaces one-shot scoring with stance-based cross-checking and evidence-aware arbitration to improve action reliability under partial observability. Specifically, given the same observation and candidate action set, we explicitly construct two stances by conditioning the evaluation on diverse and complementary objectives: a Task-Scene Understanding (TSU) stance that prioritizes goal progress from scene-layout cues, and a Safety-Information Balancing (SIB) stance that emphasizes risk and information value. The stances conduct a cooperative debate and make policy by cross-checking their top candidates with cue-grounded arguments. Then, a Navigation Consensus Arbitration (NCA) agent is employed to consolidate both sides' reasons and evidence, optionally triggering lightweight micro-probing to verify uncertain choices, preserving NCA's primary intent while disambiguating. Experiments on HM3Dv1, HM3Dv2, and MP3D demonstrate consistent improvements in success and path efficiency while reducing exploration redundancy.

摘要（中文）: 在陌生的室内环境中进行自适应导航对于家用服务机器人至关重要。尽管零样本感知和视觉-语言模型的推理取得了进展，但现有的导航系统仍然依赖于决策层的单次评分，导致过度自信的长程误差和冗余探索。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种双立场协同辩论导航(DSCD-Nav)的决策机制，该机制用基于立场的交叉检查和证据感知的仲裁来代替一次性评分，以提高在部分可观察性下的动作可靠性。具体而言，给定相同的观察和候选动作集，我们通过将评估条件设置为不同且互补的目标来显式地构建两个立场：一个任务-场景理解(TSU)立场，该立场优先考虑来自场景布局线索的目标进展；一个安全-信息平衡(SIB)立场，该立场强调风险和信息价值。这两个立场进行协同辩论，并通过交叉检查其选择的前列候选方式以及基于线索的论证来制定策略。然后，采用导航共识仲裁(NCA)代理来整合双方的理由和证据，并可选择触发轻量级的微探测来验证不确定的选择，从而在消除歧义的同时，保留NCA的主要意图。在HM3Dv1、HM3Dv2和MP3D上的实验表明，在提高成功率和路径效率的同时，持续减少了探索冗余。

TLDR: DSCD-Nav introduces a dual-stance cooperative debate mechanism for object navigation, improving success rate and path efficiency by using two perspectives (Task-Scene Understanding and Safety-Information Balancing) to cross-check and arbitrate action selection.

简要说明: DSCD-Nav 提出了一种双立场合作辩论机制，用于物体导航，通过利用任务场景理解和安全信息平衡两种视角进行交叉检查和仲裁动作选择，从而提高成功率和路径效率。

Authors: Weitao An, Qi Liu, Chenghao Xu, Jiayi Chai, Xu Yang, Kun Wei, Cheng Deng

Towards Bridging the Gap between Large-Scale Pretraining and Efficient Finetuning for Humanoid Control

Abstract (English): Reinforcement learning (RL) is widely used for humanoid control, with on-policy methods such as Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) enabling robust training via large-scale parallel simulation and, in some cases, zero-shot deployment to real robots. However, the low sample efficiency of on-policy algorithms limits safe adaptation to new environments. Although off-policy RL and model-based RL have shown improved sample efficiency, the gap between large-scale pretraining and efficient finetuning on humanoids still exists. In this paper, we find that off-policy Soft Actor-Critic (SAC), with large-batch update and a high Update-To-Data (UTD) ratio, reliably supports large-scale pretraining of humanoid locomotion policies, achieving zero-shot deployment on real robots. For adaptation, we demonstrate that these SAC-pretrained policies can be finetuned in new environments and out-of-distribution tasks using model-based methods. Data collection in the new environment executes a deterministic policy while stochastic exploration is instead confined to a physics-informed world model. This separation mitigates the risks of random exploration during adaptation while preserving exploratory coverage for improvement. Overall, the approach couples the wall-clock efficiency of large-scale simulation during pretraining with the sample efficiency of model-based learning during fine-tuning.

摘要（中文）: 强化学习 (RL) 广泛应用于人形机器人控制，其中近端策略优化 (PPO) 等在策略方法通过大规模并行仿真实现了稳健的训练，并在某些情况下实现了对真实机器人的零样本部署。然而，在策略算法的低样本效率限制了对新环境的安全适应。尽管离策略强化学习和基于模型的强化学习展示了更高的样本效率，但人形机器人大规模预训练和高效微调之间仍然存在差距。本文发现，具有大批量更新和高更新-数据 (UTD) 比率的离策略软演员-评论家 (SAC) 可以可靠地支持人形机器人运动策略的大规模预训练，从而实现真实机器人的零样本部署。对于适应性，我们证明了这些 SAC 预训练的策略可以使用基于模型的方法在新环境和超出分布的任务中进行微调。新环境中的数据收集执行确定性策略，而随机探索则被限制在物理信息丰富的世界模型中。这种分离缓解了适应过程中随机探索的风险，同时保留了用于改进的探索覆盖范围。总的来说，该方法将预训练期间大规模仿真的时钟效率与微调期间基于模型的学习的样本效率相结合。

TLDR: This paper proposes a method to bridge the gap between large-scale pretraining and efficient finetuning for humanoid control by combining off-policy SAC for pretraining with model-based RL for finetuning, achieving zero-shot deployment and efficient adaptation.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种方法，通过结合off-policy SAC进行预训练和基于模型的强化学习进行微调，来弥合人形机器人控制中大规模预训练和高效微调之间的差距，实现了零样本部署和高效适应。

Authors: Weidong Huang, Zhehan Li, Hangxin Liu, Biao Hou, Yao Su, Jingwen Zhang

Deep QP Safety Filter: Model-free Learning for Reachability-based Safety Filter

Abstract (English): We introduce Deep QP Safety Filter, a fully data-driven safety layer for black-box dynamical systems. Our method learns a Quadratic-Program (QP) safety filter without model knowledge by combining Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability with model-free learning. We construct contraction-based losses for both the safety value and its derivatives, and train two neural networks accordingly. In the exact setting, the learned critic converges to the viscosity solution (and its derivative), even for non-smooth values. Across diverse dynamical systems -- even including a hybrid system -- and multiple RL tasks, Deep QP Safety Filter substantially reduces pre-convergence failures while accelerating learning toward higher returns than strong baselines, offering a principled and practical route to safe, model-free control.

摘要（中文）: 我们引入了深度二次规划安全滤波器（Deep QP Safety Filter），这是一种完全数据驱动的黑盒动力系统的安全层。我们的方法通过将 Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) 可达性与无模型学习相结合，在没有模型知识的情况下学习二次规划 (QP) 安全滤波器。我们为安全值及其导数构建了基于收缩的损失函数，并相应地训练了两个神经网络。在精确设置下，即使对于非光滑的值，所学习的评论家也能收敛到粘性解（及其导数）。在不同的动力系统（甚至包括混合系统）和多个强化学习任务中，深度二次规划安全滤波器显著减少了预收敛失败，同时加速了学习过程，使其能够获得比强大基线更高的回报，从而为安全、无模型控制提供了一条有原则且实用的途径。

TLDR: The paper introduces a data-driven safety filter, Deep QP Safety Filter, using Hamilton-Jacobi reachability and model-free learning to improve safety and accelerate learning in dynamical systems including hybrid systems, applied to RL tasks.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种数据驱动的安全过滤器 Deep QP Safety Filter，它使用 Hamilton-Jacobi 可达性分析和模型无关学习来提高动态系统的安全性并加速学习，包括混合系统，并应用于强化学习任务。

Authors: Byeongjun Kim, H. Jin Kim

Abstracting Robot Manipulation Skills via Mixture-of-Experts Diffusion Policies

Abstract (English): Diffusion-based policies have recently shown strong results in robot manipulation, but their extension to multi-task scenarios is hindered by the high cost of scaling model size and demonstrations. We introduce Skill Mixture-of-Experts Policy (SMP), a diffusion-based mixture-of-experts policy that learns a compact orthogonal skill basis and uses sticky routing to compose actions from a small, task-relevant subset of experts at each step. A variational training objective supports this design, and adaptive expert activation at inference yields fast sampling without oversized backbones. We validate SMP in simulation and on a real dual-arm platform with multi-task learning and transfer learning tasks, where SMP achieves higher success rates and markedly lower inference cost than large diffusion baselines. These results indicate a practical path toward scalable, transferable multi-task manipulation: learn reusable skills once, activate only what is needed, and adapt quickly when tasks change.

摘要（中文）: 基于扩散的策略最近在机器人操作中表现出强大的性能，但将其扩展到多任务场景受到模型尺寸和演示数据成本升高的阻碍。我们提出了技能混合专家策略（SMP），这是一种基于扩散的混合专家策略，能够学习紧凑的正交技能基，并使用粘性路由从每个步骤的一小部分与任务相关的专家中组合动作。一个变分训练目标支持这一设计，并且在推理阶段的自适应专家激活能够在不使用过大骨干网络的情况下实现快速采样。我们在模拟和真实的双臂平台上对 SMP 进行了多任务学习和迁移学习任务的验证，结果表明 SMP 实现了比大型扩散基线更高的成功率和明显更低的推理成本。这些结果表明了一种可扩展、可迁移的多任务操作的实际路径：一次性学习可重用的技能，仅激活所需技能，并在任务变化时快速适应。

TLDR: This paper introduces Skill Mixture-of-Experts Policy (SMP), a diffusion-based mixture-of-experts policy for robot manipulation that aims to improve scalability and transferability in multi-task scenarios by learning a compact skill basis and activating only task-relevant experts. SMP achieves higher success rates and lower inference costs compared to large diffusion baselines in simulation and real-world experiments.

简要说明: 本文介绍了技能混合专家策略（SMP），这是一种基于扩散的机器人操作混合专家策略，旨在通过学习紧凑的技能基础并仅激活与任务相关的专家来提高多任务场景中的可扩展性和可转移性。在模拟和现实世界的实验中，与大型扩散基线相比，SMP实现了更高的成功率和更低的推理成本。

Authors: Ce Hao, Xuanran Zhai, Yaohua Liu, Harold Soh

InspecSafe-V1: A Multimodal Benchmark for Safety Assessment in Industrial Inspection Scenarios

Abstract (English): With the rapid development of industrial intelligence and unmanned inspection, reliable perception and safety assessment for AI systems in complex and dynamic industrial sites has become a key bottleneck for deploying predictive maintenance and autonomous inspection. Most public datasets remain limited by simulated data sources, single-modality sensing, or the absence of fine-grained object-level annotations, which prevents robust scene understanding and multimodal safety reasoning for industrial foundation models. To address these limitations, InspecSafe-V1 is released as the first multimodal benchmark dataset for industrial inspection safety assessment that is collected from routine operations of real inspection robots in real-world environments. InspecSafe-V1 covers five representative industrial scenarios, including tunnels, power facilities, sintering equipment, oil and gas petrochemical plants, and coal conveyor trestles. The dataset is constructed from 41 wheeled and rail-mounted inspection robots operating at 2,239 valid inspection sites, yielding 5,013 inspection instances. For each instance, pixel-level segmentation annotations are provided for key objects in visible-spectrum images. In addition, a semantic scene description and a corresponding safety level label are provided according to practical inspection tasks. Seven synchronized sensing modalities are further included, including infrared video, audio, depth point clouds, radar point clouds, gas measurements, temperature, and humidity, to support multimodal anomaly recognition, cross-modal fusion, and comprehensive safety assessment in industrial environments.

摘要（中文）: 随着工业智能化和无人巡检的快速发展，在复杂和动态的工业场所中，人工智能系统的可靠感知和安全评估已成为部署预测性维护和自主巡检的关键瓶颈。 大多数公共数据集仍受限于模拟数据源、单模态感知或缺乏细粒度的对象级标注，这阻碍了工业基础模型的鲁棒场景理解和多模态安全推理。 为了解决这些局限性，InspecSafe-V1 作为首个用于工业巡检安全评估的多模态基准数据集发布，该数据集是从真实环境中真实巡检机器人的日常操作中收集的。 InspecSafe-V1 涵盖了五个具有代表性的工业场景，包括隧道、电力设施、烧结设备、油气石化工厂和煤炭输送栈桥。 该数据集由在 2,239 个有效巡检站点运行的 41 个轮式和轨道式巡检机器人构建，产生了 5,013 个巡检实例。 对于每个实例，都提供了可见光谱图像中关键对象的像素级分割标注。 此外，根据实际巡检任务，提供了语义场景描述和相应的安全等级标签。 进一步包括七种同步感知模态，包括红外视频、音频、深度点云、雷达点云、气体测量、温度和湿度，以支持工业环境中的多模态异常识别、跨模态融合和综合安全评估。

TLDR: InspecSafe-V1 is a new multimodal benchmark dataset for safety assessment in industrial inspection scenarios, collected from real-world robot deployments and featuring extensive annotations across seven modalities.

简要说明: InspecSafe-V1是一个用于工业检测场景中安全评估的新型多模态基准数据集，它来源于真实机器人部署并具有七种模态的广泛标注。

Authors: Zeyi Liu, Shuang Liu, Jihai Min, Zhaoheng Zhang, Jun Cen, Pengyu Han, Songqiao Hu, Zihan Meng, Xiao He, Donghua Zhou

Meta-ROS: A Next-Generation Middleware Architecture for Adaptive and Scalable Robotic Systems

Abstract (English): The field of robotics faces significant challenges related to the complexity and interoperability of existing middleware frameworks, like ROS2, which can be difficult for new developers to adopt. To address these issues, we propose Meta-ROS, a novel middleware solution designed to streamline robotics development by simplifying integration, enhancing performance, and ensuring cross-platform compatibility. Meta-ROS leverages modern communication protocols, such as Zenoh and ZeroMQ, to enable efficient and low-latency communication across diverse hardware platforms, while also supporting various data types like audio, images, and video. We evaluated Meta-ROS's performance through comprehensive testing, comparing it with existing middleware frameworks like ROS1 and ROS2. The results demonstrated that Meta-ROS outperforms ROS2, achieving up to 30% higher throughput, significantly reducing message latency, and optimizing resource usage. Additionally, its robust hardware support and developer-centric design facilitate seamless integration and ease of use, positioning Meta-ROS as an ideal solution for modern, real-time robotics AI applications.

摘要（中文）: 机器人领域面临着与现有中间件框架（如 ROS2）的复杂性和互操作性相关的重大挑战，这使得新开发人员难以采用。为了解决这些问题，我们提出 Meta-ROS，一种新型中间件解决方案，旨在通过简化集成、提升性能和确保跨平台兼容性来简化机器人开发。 Meta-ROS 利用现代通信协议，如 Zenoh 和 ZeroMQ，以在各种硬件平台上实现高效和低延迟的通信，同时还支持各种数据类型，如音频、图像和视频。我们通过全面的测试评估了 Meta-ROS 的性能，并将其与现有的中间件框架（如 ROS1 和 ROS2）进行了比较。结果表明，Meta-ROS 优于 ROS2，吞吐量提高了 30%，显著降低了消息延迟，并优化了资源使用。此外，其强大的硬件支持和以开发者为中心的设计促进了无缝集成和易用性，使 Meta-ROS 成为现代实时机器人 AI 应用的理想解决方案。

TLDR: The paper introduces Meta-ROS, a novel robotics middleware architecture designed to improve upon ROS2 in terms of performance, integration, and cross-platform compatibility by leveraging technologies like Zenoh and ZeroMQ.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了Meta-ROS，一种新型机器人中间件架构，旨在通过利用Zenoh和ZeroMQ等技术，在性能、集成和跨平台兼容性方面改进ROS2。

Authors: Anshul Ranjan, Anoosh Damodar, Neha Chougule, Dhruva S Nayak, Anantharaman P. N, Shylaja S S

End-to-end example-based sim-to-real RL policy transfer based on neural stylisation with application to robotic cutting

Abstract (English): Whereas reinforcement learning has been applied with success to a range of robotic control problems in complex, uncertain environments, reliance on extensive data - typically sourced from simulation environments - limits real-world deployment due to the domain gap between simulated and physical systems, coupled with limited real-world sample availability. We propose a novel method for sim-to-real transfer of reinforcement learning policies, based on a reinterpretation of neural style transfer from image processing to synthesise novel training data from unpaired unlabelled real world datasets. We employ a variational autoencoder to jointly learn self-supervised feature representations for style transfer and generate weakly paired source-target trajectories to improve physical realism of synthesised trajectories. We demonstrate the application of our approach based on the case study of robot cutting of unknown materials. Compared to baseline methods, including our previous work, CycleGAN, and conditional variational autoencoder-based time series translation, our approach achieves improved task completion time and behavioural stability with minimal real-world data. Our framework demonstrates robustness to geometric and material variation, and highlights the feasibility of policy adaptation in challenging contact-rich tasks where real-world reward information is unavailable.

摘要（中文）: 强化学习已成功应用于复杂、不确定环境中一系列机器人控制问题，但对大量数据（通常来自仿真环境）的依赖限制了其在现实世界中的部署，原因在于模拟系统和物理系统之间的领域差异，以及现实世界样本的有限可用性。我们提出了一种新颖的强化学习策略从仿真到现实的迁移方法，该方法基于对图像处理中神经风格迁移的重新诠释，以从非配对、无标签的真实世界数据集中合成新的训练数据。我们采用变分自编码器来联合学习用于风格迁移的自监督特征表示，并生成弱配对的源-目标轨迹，以提高合成轨迹的物理真实感。我们通过机器人切割未知材料的案例研究来展示我们方法的应用。与包括我们之前的CycleGAN工作，以及基于条件变分自编码器的时间序列转换等基线方法相比，我们的方法以最少的真实世界数据实现了更短的任务完成时间和更高的行为稳定性。我们的框架展示了对几何和材料变化的鲁棒性，并突出了在缺乏真实世界奖励信息的挑战性接触密集型任务中进行策略适应的可行性。

TLDR: This paper proposes a novel sim-to-real transfer method using neural style transfer and variational autoencoders to generate more realistic training data for robotic cutting, achieving improved performance compared to baselines with minimal real-world data.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种新的sim-to-real迁移方法，该方法使用神经风格迁移和变分自动编码器来生成更逼真的机器人切割训练数据，与基线相比，在最少的真实世界数据下实现了更好的性能。

Authors: Jamie Hathaway, Alireza Rastegarpanah, Rustam Stolkin

MemCtrl: Using MLLMs as Active Memory Controllers on Embodied Agents

Abstract (English): Foundation models rely on in-context learning for personalized decision making. The limited size of this context window necessitates memory compression and retrieval systems like RAG. These systems however often treat memory as large offline storage spaces, which is unfavorable for embodied agents that are expected to operate under strict memory and compute constraints, online. In this work, we propose MemCtrl, a novel framework that uses Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) for pruning memory online. MemCtrl augments MLLMs with a trainable memory head μthat acts as a gate to determine which observations or reflections to retain, update, or discard during exploration. We evaluate with training two types of μ, 1) via an offline expert, and 2) via online RL, and observe significant improvement in overall embodied task completion ability on μ-augmented MLLMs. In particular, on augmenting two low performing MLLMs with MemCtrl on multiple subsets of the EmbodiedBench benchmark, we observe that μ-augmented MLLMs show an improvement of around 16% on average, with over 20% on specific instruction subsets. Finally, we present a qualitative analysis on the memory fragments collected by μ, noting the superior performance of μaugmented MLLMs on long and complex instruction types.

摘要（中文）: 基础模型依赖于上下文学习进行个性化决策。这种上下文窗口大小的限制，需要类似RAG的记忆压缩和检索系统。然而，这些系统通常将记忆视为大型离线存储空间，这对需要在严格的记忆和计算约束下在线运行的具身智能体是不利的。 在这项工作中，我们提出了MemCtrl，一种使用多模态大型语言模型(MLLM)进行在线记忆修剪的新框架。MemCtrl使用一个可训练的记忆头μ来增强MLLM，该记忆头μ充当门控，以确定在探索期间保留、更新或丢弃哪些观察或反思。我们通过训练两种类型的μ来评估，1)通过离线专家，以及2)通过在线强化学习，并观察到μ增强的MLLM在整体具身任务完成能力方面有显著提高。 特别是，在EmbodiedBench基准测试的多个子集上，用MemCtrl增强两个性能较低的MLLM后，我们观察到μ增强的MLLM平均提高了约16%，在特定的指令子集上提高了超过20%。最后，我们对μ收集的记忆片段进行了定性分析，注意到μ增强的MLLM在长而复杂的指令类型上表现出卓越的性能。

TLDR: The paper introduces MemCtrl, a framework using MLLMs with a trainable memory head to selectively prune memory for embodied agents, showing improved task completion in benchmark environments.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了 MemCtrl，一个使用 MLLM 和可训练的记忆头来选择性修剪具身代理内存的框架，并在基准环境中展现出改进的任务完成能力。

Authors: Vishnu Sashank Dorbala, Dinesh Manocha

MetricAnything: Scaling Metric Depth Pretraining with Noisy Heterogeneous Sources

Abstract (English): Scaling has powered recent advances in vision foundation models, yet extending this paradigm to metric depth estimation remains challenging due to heterogeneous sensor noise, camera-dependent biases, and metric ambiguity in noisy cross-source 3D data. We introduce Metric Anything, a simple and scalable pretraining framework that learns metric depth from noisy, diverse 3D sources without manually engineered prompts, camera-specific modeling, or task-specific architectures. Central to our approach is the Sparse Metric Prompt, created by randomly masking depth maps, which serves as a universal interface that decouples spatial reasoning from sensor and camera biases. Using about 20M image-depth pairs spanning reconstructed, captured, and rendered 3D data across 10000 camera models, we demonstrate-for the first time-a clear scaling trend in the metric depth track. The pretrained model excels at prompt-driven tasks such as depth completion, super-resolution and Radar-camera fusion, while its distilled prompt-free student achieves state-of-the-art results on monocular depth estimation, camera intrinsics recovery, single/multi-view metric 3D reconstruction, and VLA planning. We also show that using pretrained ViT of Metric Anything as a visual encoder significantly boosts Multimodal Large Language Model capabilities in spatial intelligence. These results show that metric depth estimation can benefit from the same scaling laws that drive modern foundation models, establishing a new path toward scalable and efficient real-world metric perception. We open-source MetricAnything at http://metric-anything.github.io/metric-anything-io/ to support community research.

摘要（中文）: 尺度扩展驱动了视觉基础模型领域的近期进展，然而，由于异构传感器噪声、相机相关的偏差以及嘈杂的跨源3D数据中的尺度模糊性，将这种范式扩展到尺度深度估计仍然具有挑战性。我们提出了Metric Anything，一个简单且可扩展的预训练框架，它可以从嘈杂、多样的3D源学习尺度深度，而无需人工设计的提示、相机特定的建模或任务特定的架构。我们方法的核心是稀疏尺度提示，它通过随机掩码深度图创建，作为一种通用接口，将空间推理与传感器和相机偏差解耦。我们使用约2000万个图像-深度对，涵盖跨越10000个相机模型的重建、采集和渲染的3D数据，首次展示了尺度深度追踪中的明显尺度扩展趋势。 预训练模型擅长提示驱动的任务，如深度补全、超分辨率和雷达-相机融合，而其蒸馏的无提示学生模型在单目深度估计、相机内参恢复、单/多视图尺度3D重建以及VLA规划方面取得了最先进的结果。 我们还表明，使用Metric Anything的预训练ViT作为视觉编码器能够显著提升多模态大型语言模型在空间智能方面的能力。 这些结果表明，尺度深度估计可以受益于驱动现代基础模型的相同尺度扩展定律，从而为可扩展和高效的真实世界尺度感知建立了一条新路径。 我们开源了MetricAnything，网址为http://metric-anything.github.io/metric-anything-io/，以支持社区研究。

TLDR: MetricAnything pretrains a model on noisy, diverse 3D data using a sparse metric prompt, achieving scaling benefits for metric depth estimation and improving performance on various downstream tasks, including enhancing spatial reasoning in VLMs.

简要说明: MetricAnything利用稀疏度量提示在噪声和多样化的3D数据上预训练模型，实现了度量深度估计的缩放优势，并提高了各种下游任务的性能，包括增强视觉语言模型中的空间推理。

Authors: Baorui Ma, Jiahui Yang, Donglin Di, Xuancheng Zhang, Jianxun Cui, Hao Li, Yan Xie, Wei Chen

Optimizing Agentic Workflows using Meta-tools

Abstract (English): Agentic AI enables LLM to dynamically reason, plan, and interact with tools to solve complex tasks. However, agentic workflows often require many iterative reasoning steps and tool invocations, leading to significant operational expense, end-to-end latency and failures due to hallucinations. This work introduces Agent Workflow Optimization (AWO), a framework that identifies and optimizes redundant tool execution patterns to improve the efficiency and robustness of agentic workflows. AWO analyzes existing workflow traces to discover recurring sequences of tool calls and transforms them into meta-tools, which are deterministic, composite tools that bundle multiple agent actions into a single invocation. Meta-tools bypass unnecessary intermediate LLM reasoning steps and reduce operational cost while also shortening execution paths, leading to fewer failures. Experiments on two agentic AI benchmarks show that AWO reduces the number of LLM calls up to 11.9% while also increasing the task success rate by up to 4.2 percent points.

摘要（中文）: 自主智能 (Agentic AI) 使得大语言模型 (LLM) 能够动态地推理、计划和与工具交互以解决复杂任务。然而，自主工作流通常需要许多迭代推理步骤和工具调用，导致显著的运营费用、端到端延迟以及由于幻觉导致的失败。 本文介绍了自主工作流优化 (AWO)，一种识别和优化冗余工具执行模式的框架，以提高自主工作流的效率和鲁棒性。 AWO 分析现有的工作流踪迹，发现重复出现的工具调用序列，并将它们转换为元工具，即确定性的、组合工具，将多个自主智能体的动作捆绑到单个调用中。 元工具绕过不必要的中间 LLM 推理步骤，降低运营成本，同时缩短执行路径，从而减少失败。 在两个自主智能基准测试上的实验表明，AWO 可将 LLM 调用次数减少高达 11.9%，同时将任务成功率提高高达 4.2 个百分点。

TLDR: This paper introduces Agent Workflow Optimization (AWO), a framework that creates meta-tools from redundant tool execution patterns in agentic AI workflows, improving efficiency and robustness by reducing LLM calls and increasing task success rates.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为Agent Workflow Optimization (AWO) 的框架，该框架通过从智能体AI工作流程中冗余的工具执行模式创建元工具，从而提高效率和鲁棒性，减少 LLM 调用并提高任务成功率。

Authors: Sami Abuzakuk, Anne-Marie Kermarrec, Rishi Sharma, Rasmus Moorits Veski, Martijn de Vos

CAR-bench: Evaluating the Consistency and Limit-Awareness of LLM Agents under Real-World Uncertainty

Abstract (English): Existing benchmarks for Large Language Model (LLM) agents focus on task completion under idealistic settings but overlook reliability in real-world, user-facing applications. In domains, such as in-car voice assistants, users often issue incomplete or ambiguous requests, creating intrinsic uncertainty that agents must manage through dialogue, tool use, and policy adherence. We introduce CAR-bench, a benchmark for evaluating consistency, uncertainty handling, and capability awareness in multi-turn, tool-using LLM agents in an in-car assistant domain. The environment features an LLM-simulated user, domain policies, and 58 interconnected tools spanning navigation, productivity, charging, and vehicle control. Beyond standard task completion, CAR-bench introduces Hallucination tasks that test agents' limit-awareness under missing tools or information, and Disambiguation tasks that require resolving uncertainty through clarification or internal information gathering. Baseline results reveal large gaps between occasional and consistent success on all task types. Even frontier reasoning LLMs achieve less than 50% consistent pass rate on Disambiguation tasks due to premature actions, and frequently violate policies or fabricate information to satisfy user requests in Hallucination tasks, underscoring the need for more reliable and self-aware LLM agents in real-world settings.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型 (LLM) 代理的现有基准侧重于理想条件下的任务完成，但忽视了在面向用户的实际应用中的可靠性。 在诸如车载语音助手等领域，用户常常发出不完整或含糊不清的请求，这产生了固有的不确定性，代理必须通过对话、工具使用和策略遵守来管理。 我们推出了 CAR-bench，这是一个评估车载助手领域中多轮、工具使用型 LLM 代理在一致性、不确定性处理和能力感知方面的基准。 该环境以 LLM 模拟用户、领域策略以及涵盖导航、效率、充电和车辆控制的 58 个相互关联的工具为特色。 除了标准的任务完成之外，CAR-bench 还引入了幻觉任务，以测试代理在缺少工具或信息时的极限感知能力，以及消歧任务，要求通过澄清或内部信息收集来解决不确定性。 基线结果表明，在所有任务类型中，偶然成功和持续成功之间存在巨大差距。 即使是最先进的推理 LLM 在消歧任务上的持续通过率也低于 50%，原因是过早采取行动，并且在幻觉任务中，它们经常违反策略或捏造信息以满足用户请求，这突显了在实际环境中对更可靠和具有自我意识的 LLM 代理的需求。

TLDR: CAR-bench is introduced as a new benchmark to evaluate the consistency and limit-awareness of LLM agents in real-world scenarios, specifically within an in-car assistant domain, revealing shortcomings in current models' ability to handle uncertainty and adhere to constraints.

简要说明: CAR-bench是一个新的基准测试，用于评估LLM Agent在真实世界场景中的一致性和极限感知能力，特别是在车载助理领域。该基准揭示了当前模型在处理不确定性和遵守约束方面的不足。

Authors: Johannes Kirmayr, Lukas Stappen, Elisabeth André

Learning Decentralized LLM Collaboration with Multi-Agent Actor Critic

Abstract (English): Recent work has explored optimizing LLM collaboration through Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). However, most MARL fine-tuning approaches rely on predefined execution protocols, which often require centralized execution. Decentralized LLM collaboration is more appealing in practice, as agents can run inference in parallel with flexible deployments. Also, current approaches use Monte Carlo methods for fine-tuning, which suffer from high variance and thus require more samples to train effectively. Actor-critic methods are prevalent in MARL for dealing with these issues, so we developed Multi-Agent Actor-Critic (MAAC) methods to optimize decentralized LLM collaboration. In this paper, we analyze when and why these MAAC methods are beneficial. We propose 2 MAAC approaches, \textbf{CoLLM-CC} with a \textbf{C}entralized \textbf{C}ritic and \textbf{CoLLM-DC} with \textbf{D}ecentralized \textbf{C}ritics. Our experiments across writing, coding, and game-playing domains show that Monte Carlo methods and CoLLM-DC can achieve performance comparable to CoLLM-CC in short-horizon and dense-reward settings. However, they both underperform CoLLM-CC on long-horizon or sparse-reward tasks, where Monte Carlo methods require substantially more samples and CoLLM-DC struggles to converge. Our code is available at https://github.com/OpenMLRL/CoMLRL/releases/tag/v1.3.2.

摘要（中文）: 最近的研究探索了通过多智能体强化学习（MARL）优化大型语言模型（LLM）协作。然而，大多数 MARL 微调方法依赖于预定义的执行协议，这些协议通常需要集中式执行。去中心化 LLM 协作在实践中更具吸引力，因为智能体可以并行运行推理并进行灵活部署。此外，目前的方法使用蒙特卡洛方法进行微调，这会受到高方差的影响，因此需要更多的样本才能有效地进行训练。Actor-critic 方法在 MARL 中普遍用于解决这些问题，因此我们开发了多智能体 Actor-Critic（MAAC）方法来优化去中心化 LLM 协作。在本文中，我们分析了这些 MAAC 方法在何时以及为何有益。我们提出了两种 MAAC 方法，分别是具有集中式评论家（\textbf{C}entralized \textbf{C}ritic）的 \textbf{CoLLM-CC} 和具有去中心化评论家（\textbf{D}ecentralized \textbf{C}ritics）的 \textbf{CoLLM-DC}。我们在写作、编码和游戏领域进行的一系列实验表明，在短视界和稠密奖励设置下，蒙特卡洛方法和 CoLLM-DC 可以实现与 CoLLM-CC 相媲美的性能。然而，在长视界或稀疏奖励任务上，它们的表现均不如 CoLLM-CC，蒙特卡洛方法需要明显更多的样本，而 CoLLM-DC 则难以收敛。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/OpenMLRL/CoMLRL/releases/tag/v1.3.2 获取。

TLDR: This paper introduces Multi-Agent Actor-Critic methods (CoLLM-CC and CoLLM-DC) for decentralized LLM collaboration, demonstrating their benefits over Monte Carlo methods, particularly in long-horizon and sparse-reward settings.

简要说明: 本文介绍了用于去中心化LLM协作的多智能体Actor-Critic方法（CoLLM-CC和CoLLM-DC），证明了其优于蒙特卡罗方法，尤其是在长程和稀疏奖励设置中。

Authors: Shuo Liu, Tianle Chen, Ryan Amiri, Christopher Amato

Self-Compression of Chain-of-Thought via Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): The inference overhead induced by redundant reasoning undermines the interactive experience and severely bottlenecks the deployment of Large Reasoning Models. Existing reinforcement learning (RL)-based solutions tackle this problem by coupling a length penalty with outcome-based rewards. This simplistic reward weighting struggles to reconcile brevity with accuracy, as enforcing brevity may compromise critical reasoning logic. In this work, we address this limitation by proposing a multi-agent RL framework that selectively penalizes redundant chunks, while preserving essential reasoning logic. Our framework, Self-Compression via MARL (SCMA), instantiates redundancy detection and evaluation through two specialized agents: \textbf{a Segmentation Agent} for decomposing the reasoning process into logical chunks, and \textbf{a Scoring Agent} for quantifying the significance of each chunk. The Segmentation and Scoring agents collaboratively define an importance-weighted length penalty during training, incentivizing \textbf{a Reasoning Agent} to prioritize essential logic without introducing inference overhead during deployment. Empirical evaluations across model scales demonstrate that SCMA reduces response length by 11.1\% to 39.0\% while boosting accuracy by 4.33\% to 10.02\%. Furthermore, ablation studies and qualitative analysis validate that the synergistic optimization within the MARL framework fosters emergent behaviors, yielding more powerful LRMs compared to vanilla RL paradigms.

摘要（中文）: 冗余推理带来的推理开销削弱了交互体验，并严重阻碍了大型推理模型的部署。现有的基于强化学习（RL）的解决方案通过将长度惩罚与基于结果的奖励相结合来解决这个问题。这种简单的奖励加权难以兼顾简洁性和准确性，因为强制追求简洁性可能会损害关键的推理逻辑。在本文中，我们提出了一种多智能体RL框架来解决这一局限性，该框架选择性地惩罚冗余块，同时保留必要的推理逻辑。我们的框架，通过MARL进行自压缩（SCMA），通过两个专门的智能体来实例化冗余检测和评估：一个 \textbf{分割智能体}，用于将推理过程分解为逻辑块；以及一个 \textbf{评分智能体}，用于量化每个块的重要性。分割和评分智能体在训练过程中协同定义一个重要性加权的长度惩罚，激励 \textbf{推理智能体} 优先考虑必要的逻辑，而不会在部署期间引入推理开销。跨模型规模的实证评估表明，SCMA将响应长度缩短了11.1％至39.0％，同时将准确性提高了4.33％至10.02％。此外，消融研究和定性分析验证了MARL框架内的协同优化促进了涌现行为，从而产生了比原始RL范式更强大的LRM。

TLDR: This paper presents SCMA, a multi-agent reinforcement learning framework to compress Chain-of-Thought reasoning by selectively penalizing redundant information, improving both length and accuracy.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种多智能体强化学习框架SCMA，通过选择性地惩罚冗余信息来压缩Chain-of-Thought推理，从而提高推理长度和准确性。

Authors: Yiqun Chen, Jinyuan Feng, Wei Yang, Meizhi Zhong, Zhengliang Shi, Rui Li, Xiaochi Wei, Yan Gao, Yi Wu, Yao Hu, Zhiqiang Pu, Jiaxin Mao

ProRAG: Process-Supervised Reinforcement Learning for Retrieval-Augmented Generation

Abstract (English): Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a promising paradigm for optimizing Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) in complex reasoning tasks. However, traditional outcome-based RL approaches often suffer from reward sparsity and inefficient credit assignment, as coarse-grained scalar rewards fail to identify specific erroneous steps within long-horizon trajectories. This ambiguity frequently leads to "process hallucinations", where models reach correct answers through flawed logic or redundant retrieval steps. Although recent process-aware approaches attempt to mitigate this via static preference learning or heuristic reward shaping, they often lack the on-policy exploration capabilities required to decouple step-level credit from global outcomes. To address these challenges, we propose ProRAG, a process-supervised reinforcement learning framework designed to integrate learned step-level supervision into the online optimization loop. Our framework consists of four stages: (1) Supervised Policy Warmup to initialize the model with a structured reasoning format; (2) construction of an MCTS-based Process Reward Model (PRM) to quantify intermediate reasoning quality; (3) PRM-Guided Reasoning Refinement to align the policy with fine-grained process preferences; and (4) Process-Supervised Reinforcement Learning with a dual-granularity advantage mechanism. By aggregating step-level process rewards with global outcome signals, ProRAG provides precise feedback for every action. Extensive experiments on five multi-hop reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that ProRAG achieves superior overall performance compared to strong outcome-based and process-aware RL baselines, particularly on complex long-horizon tasks, validating the effectiveness of fine-grained process supervision. The code and model are available at https://github.com/lilinwz/ProRAG.

摘要（中文）: 强化学习(RL)已成为复杂推理任务中优化检索增强生成(RAG)的一种有前景的范式。然而，传统的基于结果的强化学习方法常常受到奖励稀疏性和低效信用分配的影响，因为粗粒度的标量奖励无法识别长时程轨迹中的特定错误步骤。这种模糊性经常导致“过程幻觉”，即模型通过有缺陷的逻辑或冗余的检索步骤得到正确的答案。尽管最近的过程感知方法试图通过静态偏好学习或启发式奖励塑造来缓解这种情况，但它们通常缺乏将步骤级信用与全局结果解耦所需的在线探索能力。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了ProRAG，一种过程监督的强化学习框架，旨在将学习到的步骤级监督集成到在线优化循环中。我们的框架包含四个阶段：（1）有监督的策略预热，以使用结构化的推理格式初始化模型；(2) 构建基于蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)的过程奖励模型(PRM)来量化中间推理质量；(3) PRM引导的推理细化，以使策略与细粒度的过程偏好对齐；(4) 采用双粒度优势机制的过程监督强化学习。通过将步骤级的过程奖励与全局结果信号相结合，ProRAG为每个动作提供精确的反馈。在五个多跳推理基准上进行的大量实验表明，与强大的基于结果和过程感知的强化学习基线相比，ProRAG实现了卓越的整体性能，尤其是在复杂的长时程任务上，验证了细粒度过程监督的有效性。代码和模型可在https://github.com/lilinwz/ProRAG获取。

TLDR: ProRAG introduces a process-supervised reinforcement learning framework for RAG that leverages step-level supervision to address reward sparsity and process hallucinations, demonstrating improved performance on multi-hop reasoning tasks.

简要说明: ProRAG 提出了一种过程监督的强化学习框架，用于检索增强生成，该框架利用步骤级别的监督来解决奖励稀疏性和过程幻觉问题，并在多跳推理任务中表现出改进的性能。

Authors: Zhao Wang, Ziliang Zhao, Zhicheng Dou

WebArbiter: A Principle-Guided Reasoning Process Reward Model for Web Agents

Abstract (English): Web agents hold great potential for automating complex computer tasks, yet their interactions involve long-horizon, sequential decision-making with irreversible actions. In such settings, outcome-based supervision is sparse and delayed, often rewarding incorrect trajectories and failing to support inference-time scaling. This motivates the use of Process Reward Models (WebPRMs) for web navigation, but existing approaches remain limited: scalar WebPRMs collapse progress into coarse, weakly grounded signals, while checklist-based WebPRMs rely on brittle template matching that fails under layout or semantic changes and often mislabels superficially correct actions as successful, providing little insight or interpretability. To address these challenges, we introduce WebArbiter, a reasoning-first, principle-inducing WebPRM that formulates reward modeling as text generation, producing structured justifications that conclude with a preference verdict and identify the action most conducive to task completion under the current context. Training follows a two-stage pipeline: reasoning distillation equips the model with coherent principle-guided reasoning, and reinforcement learning corrects teacher biases by directly aligning verdicts with correctness, enabling stronger generalization. To support systematic evaluation, we release WebPRMBench, a comprehensive benchmark spanning four diverse web environments with rich tasks and high-quality preference annotations. On WebPRMBench, WebArbiter-7B outperforms the strongest baseline, GPT-5, by 9.1 points. In reward-guided trajectory search on WebArena-Lite, it surpasses the best prior WebPRM by up to 7.2 points, underscoring its robustness and practical value in real-world complex web tasks.

摘要（中文）: Web代理在自动化复杂的计算机任务方面具有巨大潜力，但它们的交互涉及不可逆操作的长期、序列决策。在这样的环境中，基于结果的监督是稀疏且延迟的，通常会奖励不正确的轨迹，并且无法支持推理时扩展。这促使人们使用流程奖励模型（WebPRM）进行网页导航，但现有方法仍然存在局限性：标量WebPRM将进展信息压缩为粗略、弱接地的信号，而基于检查表的WebPRM则依赖于脆弱的模板匹配，这些模板匹配在布局或语义发生变化时会失效，并且经常将表面上正确的动作错误地标记为成功，几乎没有提供洞察力或可解释性。为了解决这些挑战，我们引入了WebArbiter，这是一种推理优先、原则归纳的WebPRM，它将奖励建模表述为文本生成，生成结构化论证，最终得出偏好结论，并识别在当前上下文中最有益于任务完成的动作。训练遵循一个两阶段的流程：推理蒸馏为模型配备了连贯的、原则指导的推理能力，强化学习通过将结论与正确性直接对齐来纠正教师偏差，从而实现更强的泛化能力。为了支持系统评估，我们发布了WebPRMBench，这是一个全面的基准测试，涵盖四个具有丰富任务和高质量偏好标注的多样化Web环境。在WebPRMBench上，WebArbiter-7B的性能优于最强的基线GPT-5，高出9.1个百分点。在WebArena-Lite上使用奖励引导的轨迹搜索中，它超越了之前最好的WebPRM最多7.2个百分点，突显了其在真实世界复杂Web任务中的稳健性和实际价值。

TLDR: The paper introduces WebArbiter, a reasoning-first Process Reward Model (PRM) for web agents that uses text generation to provide structured justifications for action selection, achieving state-of-the-art performance on a newly released benchmark.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了WebArbiter，一种基于推理的Web代理过程奖励模型(PRM)，它使用文本生成来提供结构化的行动选择理由，并在新发布 的基准测试中实现了最先进的性能。

Authors: Yao Zhang, Shijie Tang, Zeyu Li, Zhen Han, Volker Tresp

A Unified XAI-LLM Approach for EndotrachealSuctioning Activity Recognition

Abstract (English): Endotracheal suctioning (ES) is an invasive yet essential clinical procedure that requires a high degree of skill to minimize patient risk - particularly in home care and educational settings, where consistent supervision may be limited. Despite its critical importance, automated recognition and feedback systems for ES training remain underexplored. To address this gap, this study proposes a unified, LLM-centered framework for video-based activity recognition benchmarked against conventional machine learning and deep learning approaches, and a pilot study on feedback generation. Within this framework, the Large Language Model (LLM) serves as the central reasoning module, performing both spatiotemporal activity recognition and explainable decision analysis from video data. Furthermore, the LLM is capable of verbalizing feedback in natural language, thereby translating complex technical insights into accessible, human-understandable guidance for trainees. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed LLM-based approach outperforms baseline models, achieving an improvement of approximately 15-20\% in both accuracy and F1 score. Beyond recognition, the framework incorporates a pilot student-support module built upon anomaly detection and explainable AI (XAI) principles, which provides automated, interpretable feedback highlighting correct actions and suggesting targeted improvements. Collectively, these contributions establish a scalable, interpretable, and data-driven foundation for advancing nursing education, enhancing training efficiency, and ultimately improving patient safety.

摘要（中文）: 气管内吸痰 (ES) 是一项侵入性但至关重要的临床操作，需要高度的技能才能最大限度地降低患者风险，尤其是在家庭护理和教育环境中，在这些环境中，持续的监督可能受到限制。 尽管其至关重要，但用于 ES 培训的自动识别和反馈系统仍未得到充分探索。 为了弥补这一差距，本研究提出了一种统一的、以 LLM 为中心的框架，用于基于视频的活动识别，并以传统的机器学习和深度学习方法为基准，以及针对反馈生成的一项试点研究。 在此框架内，大型语言模型 (LLM) 充当中央推理模块，从视频数据中执行时空活动识别和可解释的决策分析。 此外，LLM 能够以自然语言表达反馈，从而将复杂的技术见解转化为易于理解的人工指导，供学员使用。 实验结果表明，所提出的基于 LLM 的方法优于基线模型，在准确率和 F1 分数方面均提高了约 15-20%。 除了识别之外，该框架还包含一个基于异常检测和可解释 AI (XAI) 原则的试点学生支持模块，该模块提供自动的、可解释的反馈，突出显示正确的操作并提供有针对性的改进建议。 总之，这些贡献为推进护理教育、提高培训效率并最终提高患者安全性奠定了可扩展、可解释和数据驱动的基础。

TLDR: This paper introduces a LLM-centered framework for video-based activity recognition of endotracheal suctioning (ES) with explainable feedback generation, outperforming traditional methods by 15-20% in accuracy and F1 score, aiming to improve nursing education and patient safety.

Authors: Hoang Khang Phan, Quang Vinh Dang, Noriyo Colley, Christina Garcia, Nhat Tan Le

Language-based Trial and Error Falls Behind in the Era of Experience

Abstract (English): While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel in language-based agentic tasks, their applicability to unseen, nonlinguistic environments (e.g., symbolic or spatial tasks) remains limited. Previous work attributes this performance gap to the mismatch between the pretraining distribution and the testing distribution. In this work, we demonstrate the primary bottleneck is the prohibitive cost of exploration: mastering these tasks requires extensive trial-and-error, which is computationally unsustainable for parameter-heavy LLMs operating in a high dimensional semantic space. To address this, we propose SCOUT (Sub-Scale Collaboration On Unseen Tasks), a novel framework that decouples exploration from exploitation. We employ lightweight "scouts" (e.g., small MLPs) to probe environmental dynamics at a speed and scale far exceeding LLMs. The collected trajectories are utilized to bootstrap the LLM via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), followed by multi-turn Reinforcement Learning (RL) to activate its latent world knowledge. Empirically, SCOUT enables a Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct model to achieve an average score of 0.86, significantly outperforming proprietary models, including Gemini-2.5-Pro (0.60), while saving about 60% GPU hours consumption.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型 (LLM) 在基于语言的智能体任务中表现出色，但它们在未见过的、非语言环境（例如，符号或空间任务）中的适用性仍然有限。此前的工作将这种性能差距归因于预训练分布和测试分布之间的不匹配。在这项工作中，我们证明，主要的瓶颈是探索的巨大成本：掌握这些任务需要大量的试错，这对于在高维语义空间中运行的参数庞大的 LLM 来说，在计算上是不可持续的。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了 SCOUT（ Sub-Scale Collaboration On Unseen Tasks，在未见过的任务上的子规模协作）， 这是一个将探索与利用解耦的新颖框架。我们采用轻量级的 “侦察兵”（例如，小型MLP）以远超LLM的速度和规模探测环境动态。收集到的轨迹被用于通过监督微调 (SFT) 来引导 LLM，然后通过多轮强化学习 (RL) 来激活其潜在的世界知识。 在实验上，SCOUT 使 Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct 模型能够达到 0.86 的平均得分，显著优于包括 Gemini-2.5-Pro (0.60) 在内的专有模型，同时节省了约 60% 的 GPU 使用时间。

TLDR: The paper introduces SCOUT, a framework that uses lightweight models to explore nonlinguistic environments and then bootstraps LLMs via SFT and RL, achieving better performance than larger proprietary models with less computation.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了 SCOUT 框架，该框架使用轻量级模型探索非语言环境，然后通过 SFT 和 RL 引导 LLM，从而以更少的计算量实现了优于更大的专有模型的性能。
