Authors: Haoyu Wang, Guozheng Ma, Shugang Cui, Yilun Kong, Haotian Luo, Li Shen, Mengya Gao, Yichao Wu, Xiaogang Wang, Dacheng Tao

E-mem: Multi-agent based Episodic Context Reconstruction for LLM Agent Memory

Abstract (English): The evolution of Large Language Model (LLM) agents towards System~2 reasoning, characterized by deliberative, high-precision problem-solving, requires maintaining rigorous logical integrity over extended horizons. However, prevalent memory preprocessing paradigms suffer from destructive de-contextualization. By compressing complex sequential dependencies into pre-defined structures (e.g., embeddings or graphs), these methods sever the contextual integrity essential for deep reasoning. To address this, we propose E-mem, a framework shifting from Memory Preprocessing to Episodic Context Reconstruction. Inspired by biological engrams, E-mem employs a heterogeneous hierarchical architecture where multiple assistant agents maintain uncompressed memory contexts, while a central master agent orchestrates global planning. Unlike passive retrieval, our mechanism empowers assistants to locally reason within activated segments, extracting context-aware evidence before aggregation. Evaluations on the LoCoMo benchmark demonstrate that E-mem achieves over 54\% F1, surpassing the state-of-the-art GAM by 7.75\%, while reducing token cost by over 70\%.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型(LLM)智能体向System~2推理的演进，其特点是深思熟虑、高精度的问题解决，需要在较长的时间跨度内保持严格的逻辑完整性。然而，目前流行的记忆预处理范式遭受了破坏性的去情境化。通过将复杂的序列依赖关系压缩到预定义的结构中（例如，嵌入或图），这些方法切断了对于深度推理至关重要的情境完整性。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了E-mem，一个从记忆预处理转向情景上下文重建的框架。受生物印迹的启发，E-mem采用异构分层架构，其中多个辅助智能体维护未压缩的记忆上下文，而一个中央主智能体协调全局规划。与被动检索不同，我们的机制使辅助智能体能够在激活的片段内进行本地推理，在聚合之前提取与上下文相关的证据。在LoCoMo基准测试中的评估表明，E-mem实现了超过54%的F1得分，超过了最先进的GAM模型7.75%，同时降低了70%以上的token成本。

TLDR: This paper introduces E-mem, a multi-agent framework for LLM agent memory that reconstructs episodic contexts by maintaining uncompressed memory segments and allowing local reasoning to improve performance and reduce token cost, showing improvements on the LoCoMo benchmark.

简要说明: 这篇论文介绍了E-mem，一个用于LLM代理记忆的多代理框架，通过维护未压缩的记忆片段并允许本地推理来重构情景上下文，从而提高性能并降低token成本，并在LoCoMo基准测试中显示出改进。

Authors: Kaixiang Wang, Yidan Lin, Jiong Lou, Zhaojiacheng Zhou, Bunyod Suvonov, Jie Li

TACLer: Tailored Curriculum Reinforcement Learning for Efficient Reasoning

Abstract (English): Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable performance on complex reasoning tasks, especially when equipped with long chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning. However, eliciting long CoT typically requires large-scale reinforcement learning (RL) training, while often leading to overthinking with redundant intermediate steps. To improve learning and reasoning efficiency, while preserving or even enhancing performance, we propose TACLer, a model-tailored curriculum reinforcement learning framework that gradually increases the complexity of the data based on the model's proficiency in multi-stage RL training. TACLer features two core components: (i) tailored curriculum learning that determines what knowledge the model lacks and needs to learn in progressive stages; (ii) a hybrid Thinking/NoThinking reasoning paradigm that balances accuracy and efficiency by enabling or disabling the Thinking mode. Our experiments show that TACLer yields a twofold advantage in learning and reasoning: (i) it reduces computational cost, cutting training compute by over 50% compared to long thinking models and reducing inference token usage by over 42% relative to the base model; and (ii) it improves accuracy by over 9% on the base model, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art Nothinking and Thinking baselines across four math datasets with complex problems.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型（LLM）在复杂的推理任务中表现出卓越的性能，尤其是在配备长链式思维（CoT）推理时。然而，诱导长CoT通常需要大规模的强化学习（RL）训练，同时常常导致过度思考，产生冗余的中间步骤。为了提高学习和推理效率，同时保持甚至增强性能，我们提出了TACLer，一种模型定制的课程强化学习框架，该框架基于模型在多阶段RL训练中的熟练程度，逐步增加数据的复杂性。TACLer具有两个核心组成部分：（i）定制的课程学习，可确定模型缺乏哪些知识以及需要在渐进阶段学习哪些知识；(ii) 一种混合的思考/非思考推理范式，通过启用或禁用思考模式来平衡准确性和效率。我们的实验表明，TACLer在学习和推理方面产生了双重优势：（i）它降低了计算成本，与长思考模型相比，训练计算量减少了50%以上，与基准模型相比，推理token使用量减少了42%以上；(ii)在基准模型的基础上，准确率提高了9%以上，在四个具有复杂问题的数学数据集上，始终优于最先进的非思考和思考基线模型。

TLDR: TACLer is a curriculum reinforcement learning framework that improves LLM reasoning efficiency and accuracy by tailoring data complexity to the model's proficiency and using a hybrid Thinking/NoThinking paradigm. It reduces computational cost and improves accuracy compared to baselines on math datasets.

简要说明: TACLer 是一种课程强化学习框架，通过根据模型的能力定制数据复杂性以及使用混合的思考/不思考范式，来提高 LLM 的推理效率和准确性。 与在数学数据集上的基线模型相比，它降低了计算成本并提高了准确性。

Authors: Huiyuan Lai, Malvina Nissim

ShardMemo: Masked MoE Routing for Sharded Agentic LLM Memory

Abstract (English): Agentic large language model (LLM) systems rely on external memory for long-horizon state and concurrent multi-agent execution, but centralized indexes and heuristic partitions become bottlenecks as memory volume and parallel access grow. We present ShardMemo, a budgeted tiered memory service with Tier A per-agent working state, Tier B sharded evidence with shard-local approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) indexes, and Tier C, a versioned skill library. Tier B enforces scope-before-routing: structured eligibility constraints mask ineligible shards before routing or ANN search. We cast shard probing as masked mixture-of-experts (MoE) routing over eligible shards, probing up to $B_{\mathrm{probe}}$ shards via Top-$B_{\mathrm{probe}}$ or adaptive Top-$P$, and use cost-aware gating over profile/observation/session shard families; the router is trained from evidence-to-shard supervision. On LoCoMo, ShardMemo improves over the strongest baseline (GAM) by +5.11 to +6.82 F1 across question categories. Under a fixed-budget routing setting ($B_{\mathrm{probe}}=3$), ShardMemo improves over cosine-to-prototype shard routing by +6.87 F1 while reducing retrieval work (VecScan 521->414, -20.5%) and p95 latency (95->76 ms). On long-context HotpotQA, ShardMemo achieves 63.41/61.88/57.95 F1 at 56K/224K/448K tokens. On ToolBench, Tier C reaches 0.97 Precision@3 and 1.94 StepRed (+10.2% and +7.2% over embedding-similarity retrieval).

摘要（中文）: 智能体化大型语言模型（LLM）系统依赖于外部记忆来实现长程状态和并发多智能体执行，但随着记忆容量和并行访问的增长，中心化索引和启发式分区会成为瓶颈。我们提出了ShardMemo，一种预算受限的分层记忆服务，具有A层：用于每个智能体的工作状态；B层：分片证据，带有分片局部的近似最近邻（ANN）索引；以及C层：版本化的技能库。B层强制执行范围优先于路由：结构化的资格约束在路由或ANN搜索之前屏蔽掉不符合条件的分片。我们将分片查询建模为在符合条件的分片上的屏蔽式混合专家（MoE）路由，通过Top-$B_{\mathrm{probe}}$或自适应Top-$P$探测最多$B_{\mathrm{probe}}$个分片，并对配置文件/观察/会话分片族使用成本敏感的门控；路由器通过证据到分片的监督进行训练。在LoCoMo上，ShardMemo在各个问题类别上比最强的基线（GAM）提高了+5.11至+6.82 F1。在固定预算的路由设置下（$B_{\mathrm{probe}}=3$），ShardMemo比余弦相似度到原型分片路由提高了+6.87 F1，同时减少了检索工作（VecScan 521->414，-20.5%）和p95延迟（95->76 ms）。在长上下文HotpotQA上，ShardMemo在56K/224K/448K tokens下实现了63.41/61.88/57.95 F1。在ToolBench上，C层达到了0.97的Precision@3和1.94的StepRed（比基于嵌入相似度的检索分别提高了+10.2%和+7.2%）。

TLDR: ShardMemo is a tiered memory system for agentic LLMs that uses masked Mixture-of-Experts routing to improve retrieval performance, reduce latency, and scale to larger memory volumes.

简要说明: ShardMemo是一个面向Agentic LLM的分层记忆系统，它采用masked MoE路由来提升检索性能，降低延迟，并扩展到更大的记忆容量。

Authors: Yang Zhao, Chengxiao Dai, Yue Xiu, Mengying Kou, Yuliang Zheng, Dusit Niyato

On the Adversarial Robustness of Large Vision-Language Models under Visual Token Compression

Abstract (English): Visual token compression is widely used to accelerate large vision-language models (LVLMs) by pruning or merging visual tokens, yet its adversarial robustness remains unexplored. We show that existing encoder-based attacks can substantially overestimate the robustness of compressed LVLMs, due to an optimization-inference mismatch: perturbations are optimized on the full-token representation, while inference is performed through a token-compression bottleneck. To address this gap, we propose the Compression-AliGnEd attack (CAGE), which aligns perturbation optimization with compression inference without assuming access to the deployed compression mechanism or its token budget. CAGE combines (i) expected feature disruption, which concentrates distortion on tokens likely to survive across plausible budgets, and (ii) rank distortion alignment, which actively aligns token distortions with rank scores to promote the retention of highly distorted evidence. Across diverse representative plug-and-play compression mechanisms and datasets, our results show that CAGE consistently achieves lower robust accuracy than the baseline. This work highlights that robustness assessments ignoring compression can be overly optimistic, calling for compression-aware security evaluation and defenses for efficient LVLMs.

摘要（中文）: 视觉令牌压缩通过剪枝或合并视觉令牌来加速大型视觉语言模型(LVLM)，并被广泛应用，但其对抗鲁棒性尚未被充分探索。我们发现，由于优化-推理不匹配，现有的基于编码器的攻击可能会大幅高估压缩后LVLM的鲁棒性：扰动是在完整令牌表示上进行优化的，而推理是通过令牌压缩瓶颈进行的。为了解决这一差距，我们提出了压缩对齐攻击(CAGE)，该攻击使扰动优化与压缩推理对齐，而无需访问已部署的压缩机制或其令牌预算。CAGE结合了(i)期望特征扰乱，将失真集中在可能在合理预算范围内幸存的令牌上，以及(ii)排名失真对齐，主动将令牌失真与排名分数对齐，以促进高度失真证据的保留。在各种具有代表性的即插即用压缩机制和数据集上，我们的结果表明，CAGE始终比基线攻击取得更低的鲁棒性准确率。这项工作强调，忽略压缩的鲁棒性评估可能过于乐观，需要对高效LVLM进行压缩感知的安全性评估和防御。

TLDR: This paper identifies a vulnerability in compressed vision-language models where existing adversarial attacks overestimate robustness. They propose a new attack, CAGE, that accounts for compression, demonstrating decreased robustness compared to baseline attacks.

简要说明: 该论文指出，压缩的视觉-语言模型存在漏洞，现有对抗攻击高估了其鲁棒性。他们提出了一种新的攻击方法CAGE，该方法考虑了压缩，并证明与基线攻击相比，鲁棒性降低。

Authors: Xinwei Zhang, Hangcheng Liu, Li Bai, Hao Wang, Qingqing Ye, Tianwei Zhang, Haibo Hu

MemOCR: Layout-Aware Visual Memory for Efficient Long-Horizon Reasoning

Abstract (English): Long-horizon agentic reasoning necessitates effectively compressing growing interaction histories into a limited context window. Most existing memory systems serialize history as text, where token-level cost is uniform and scales linearly with length, often spending scarce budget on low-value details. To this end, we introduce MemOCR, a multimodal memory agent that improves long-horizon reasoning under tight context budgets by allocating memory space with adaptive information density through visual layout. Concretely, MemOCR maintains a structured rich-text memory (e.g., headings, highlights) and renders it into an image that the agent consults for memory access, visually prioritizing crucial evidence while aggressively compressing auxiliary details. To ensure robustness across varying memory budgets, we train MemOCR with reinforcement learning under budget-aware objectives that expose the agent to diverse compression levels. Across long-context multi-hop and single-hop question-answering benchmarks, MemOCR outperforms strong text-based baselines and achieves more effective context utilization under extreme budgets.

摘要（中文）: 长程智能体推理需要将不断增长的交互历史有效压缩到有限的上下文窗口中。大多数现有的记忆系统将历史记录序列化为文本，其中token级别的成本相同，并随长度线性增长，常常将稀缺的预算花费在低价值的细节上。为此，我们引入 MemOCR，一种多模态记忆智能体，通过利用视觉布局以自适应信息密度分配记忆空间，在严格的上下文预算下改进长程推理。具体而言，MemOCR 维护一个结构化的富文本记忆（例如，标题、高亮），并将其渲染成图像，智能体通过访问该图像来获取记忆，在视觉上优先处理关键证据，同时积极压缩辅助细节。为了确保在不同的记忆预算下的稳健性，我们采用强化学习在预算感知的目标下训练 MemOCR，从而使智能体能够适应不同的压缩级别。在长上下文多跳和单跳问答基准测试中，MemOCR 优于强大的基于文本的基线，并在极端预算下实现了更有效的上下文利用。

TLDR: MemOCR uses a visually-represented, structured memory with adaptive information density for long-horizon reasoning, achieving better context utilization under tight budgets compared to text-based methods.

简要说明: MemOCR使用具有自适应信息密度的视觉表示结构化记忆进行长程推理，与基于文本的方法相比，在严格预算下实现了更好的上下文利用率。

Authors: Yaorui Shi, Shugui Liu, Yu Yang, Wenyu Mao, Yuxin Chen, Qi GU, Hui Su, Xunliang Cai, Xiang Wang, An Zhang

Mitigating Overthinking in Large Reasoning Models via Difficulty-aware Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve explicit chain-of-thought expansion by imitating deep thinking behaviors of humans, demonstrating excellent performance in complex task scenarios. However, the deep-thinking mode often leads to unnecessarily lengthy reasoning and resource inefficiency when handling simple tasks. This overthinking phenomenon may arise from the generation preference triggered by the reward function during post-training. Existing research attempts to mitigate overthinking from the perspective of prompt design or model training, but generally underestimates the importance of task difficulty awareness, which makes it difficult for LRMs to effectively allocate reasoning resources. In this paper, we propose Difficulty-aware Policy Optimization (DiPO), a reinforcement learning-based LRM training framework. DiPO encourages LRM to spontaneously model task complexity, and integrates them into reinforcement learning framework to adjust the generation preferences introduced by post-training. A difficulty modeling method based on model self-reasoning is proposed, which significantly reduces the dependence on manual annotation and formalize task complexity. We further develop a difficulty-signal-enhanced reward function that incorporates a penalty for lengthy reasoning while considering reasoning performance and output format. Experimental results indicate that DiPO enables the model to spontaneously adjust inference overhead, significantly reducing redundant tokens without losing performance due to thought compression.

摘要（中文）: 大型推理模型 (LRMs) 通过模仿人类的深度思考行为来实现显式的思维链扩展，在复杂的任务场景中表现出卓越的性能。然而，这种深度思考模式在处理简单任务时常常导致不必要的冗長推理和资源效率低下。这种过度思考现象可能源于后训练阶段奖励函数所触发的生成偏好。现有研究尝试从提示设计或模型训练的角度来缓解过度思考，但通常低估了任务难度感知的重要性，这使得LRMs难以有效地分配推理资源。本文提出了一种基于强化学习的LRM训练框架 —— 难度感知策略优化 (DiPO)。DiPO 鼓励LRM自发地对任务复杂度进行建模，并将其集成到强化学习框架中，以调整后训练引入的生成偏好。我们提出了一种基于模型自推理的难度建模方法，显著降低了对人工标注的依赖，并将任务复杂度形式化。此外，我们还开发了一种难度信号增强的奖励函数，该函数在考虑推理性能和输出格式的同时，对冗长的推理进行惩罚。实验结果表明，DiPO 能够使模型自发地调整推理开销，在不损失性能（由于思维压缩）的情况下，显著减少冗余 tokens。

TLDR: This paper introduces Difficulty-aware Policy Optimization (DiPO), a reinforcement learning method to address overthinking in Large Reasoning Models by dynamically adjusting reasoning effort based on task difficulty.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为难度感知策略优化 (DiPO) 的强化学习方法，旨在通过根据任务难度动态调整推理工作量来解决大型推理模型中的过度思考问题。

Authors: Qian Wan, Ziao Xu, Luona Wei, Xiaoxuan Shen, Jianwen Sun

Intrinsic Reward Policy Optimization for Sparse-Reward Environments

Abstract (English): Exploration is essential in reinforcement learning as an agent relies on trial and error to learn an optimal policy. However, when rewards are sparse, naive exploration strategies, like noise injection, are often insufficient. Intrinsic rewards can also provide principled guidance for exploration by, for example, combining them with extrinsic rewards to optimize a policy or using them to train subpolicies for hierarchical learning. However, the former approach suffers from unstable credit assignment, while the latter exhibits sample inefficiency and sub-optimality. We propose a policy optimization framework that leverages multiple intrinsic rewards to directly optimize a policy for an extrinsic reward without pretraining subpolicies. Our algorithm -- intrinsic reward policy optimization (IRPO) -- achieves this by using a surrogate policy gradient that provides a more informative learning signal than the true gradient in sparse-reward environments. We demonstrate that IRPO improves performance and sample efficiency relative to baselines in discrete and continuous environments, and formally analyze the optimization problem solved by IRPO. Our code is available at https://github.com/Mgineer117/IRPO.

摘要（中文）: 探索在强化学习中至关重要，因为智能体依赖于试错来学习最优策略。然而，当奖励稀疏时，简单的探索策略（如噪声注入）通常是不够的。内在奖励可以通过有原则的指导来进行探索，例如，将它们与外在奖励结合起来优化策略，或者使用它们来训练分层学习的子策略。然而，前一种方法会遭受不稳定的信用分配问题，而后一种方法会表现出样本效率低下和次优性。我们提出了一种策略优化框架，该框架利用多个内在奖励来直接优化针对外在奖励的策略，而无需预训练子策略。我们的算法——内在奖励策略优化 (IRPO)——通过使用代理策略梯度来实现这一点，该梯度在稀疏奖励环境中提供了比真实梯度更具信息性的学习信号。我们证明了 IRPO 在离散和连续环境中相对于基线算法提高了性能和样本效率，并正式分析了 IRPO 解决的优化问题。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/Mgineer117/IRPO 获取。

TLDR: This paper introduces Intrinsic Reward Policy Optimization (IRPO), a policy optimization framework that directly optimizes a policy for extrinsic rewards in sparse-reward environments by leveraging multiple intrinsic rewards and a surrogate policy gradient, demonstrating improved performance and sample efficiency compared to baselines.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了内在奖励策略优化(IRPO)，一个策略优化框架，通过利用多个内在奖励和一个替代策略梯度直接优化稀疏奖励环境中的外部奖励策略，与基线相比，展示了改进的性能和样本效率。

Authors: Minjae Cho, Huy Trong Tran

Ostrakon-VL: Towards Domain-Expert MLLM for Food-Service and Retail Stores

Abstract (English): Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have recently achieved substantial progress in general-purpose perception and reasoning. Nevertheless, their deployment in Food-Service and Retail Stores (FSRS) scenarios encounters two major obstacles: (i) real-world FSRS data, collected from heterogeneous acquisition devices, are highly noisy and lack auditable, closed-loop data curation, which impedes the construction of high-quality, controllable, and reproducible training corpora; and (ii) existing evaluation protocols do not offer a unified, fine-grained and standardized benchmark spanning single-image, multi-image, and video inputs, making it challenging to objectively gauge model robustness. To address these challenges, we first develop Ostrakon-VL, an FSRS-oriented MLLM based on Qwen3-VL-8B. Second, we introduce ShopBench, the first public benchmark for FSRS. Third, we propose QUAD (Quality-aware Unbiased Automated Data-curation), a multi-stage multimodal instruction data curation pipeline. Leveraging a multi-stage training strategy, Ostrakon-VL achieves an average score of 60.1 on ShopBench, establishing a new state of the art among open-source MLLMs with comparable parameter scales and diverse architectures. Notably, it surpasses the substantially larger Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B (59.4) by +0.7, and exceeds the same-scale Qwen3-VL-8B (55.3) by +4.8, demonstrating significantly improved parameter efficiency. These results indicate that Ostrakon-VL delivers more robust and reliable FSRS-centric perception and decision-making capabilities. To facilitate reproducible research, we will publicly release Ostrakon-VL and the ShopBench benchmark.

摘要（中文）: 多模态大型语言模型（MLLM）最近在通用感知和推理方面取得了显著进展。然而，它们在餐饮服务和零售商店（FSRS）场景中的部署面临两个主要障碍：（i）从异构采集设备收集的真实世界FSRS数据具有高度噪声，并且缺乏可审计的闭环数据管理，从而阻碍了高质量、可控和可复现的训练语料库的构建；以及（ii）现有的评估协议不提供统一的、细粒度和标准化的基准，涵盖单图像、多图像和视频输入，这使得客观地衡量模型鲁棒性具有挑战性。为了应对这些挑战，我们首先开发了Ostrakon-VL，一个基于Qwen3-VL-8B的面向FSRS的MLLM。其次，我们推出了ShopBench，第一个用于FSRS的公共基准。第三，我们提出了QUAD（质量感知的无偏自动数据管理），一个多阶段多模态指令数据管理流程。利用多阶段训练策略，Ostrakon-VL在ShopBench上取得了平均60.1分的成绩，在参数规模相当且架构各异的开源MLLM中建立了新的技术水平。值得注意的是，它超过了规模更大的Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B（59.4）+0.7分，并且超过了同等规模的Qwen3-VL-8B（55.3）+4.8分，表明参数效率显著提高。这些结果表明Ostrakon-VL提供了更强大和可靠的以FSRS为中心的感知和决策能力。为了促进可复现的研究，我们将公开发布Ostrakon-VL和ShopBench基准。

TLDR: The paper introduces Ostrakon-VL, an FSRS-oriented MLLM, the ShopBench benchmark for food-service and retail store applications, and a data curation pipeline, achieving state-of-the-art results compared to other open-source MLLMs.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了Ostrakon-VL，一个面向食品服务和零售商店的MLLM；ShopBench，一个用于食品服务和零售商店的基准测试； 以及一个数据管理流程，与其他的开源MLLM相比，取得了state-of-the-art的结果。

Authors: Zhiyong Shen, Gongpeng Zhao, Jun Zhou, Li Yu, Guandong Kou, Jichen Li, Chuanlei Dong, Zuncheng Li, Kaimao Li, Bingkun Wei, Shicheng Hu, Wei Xia, Wenguo Duan

The Surprising Difficulty of Search in Model-Based Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): This paper investigates search in model-based reinforcement learning (RL). Conventional wisdom holds that long-term predictions and compounding errors are the primary obstacles for model-based RL. We challenge this view, showing that search is not a plug-and-play replacement for a learned policy. Surprisingly, we find that search can harm performance even when the model is highly accurate. Instead, we show that mitigating distribution shift matters more than improving model or value function accuracy. Building on this insight, we identify key techniques for enabling effective search, achieving state-of-the-art performance across multiple popular benchmark domains.

摘要（中文）: 本文研究了基于模型的强化学习（RL）中的搜索。传统观点认为，长期预测和累积误差是基于模型的RL的主要障碍。我们挑战这一观点，表明搜索并非只是已学习策略的即插即用型替代方案。出人意料的是，我们发现即使模型非常准确，搜索也可能损害性能。相反，我们表明，缓解分布偏移比提高模型或价值函数的准确性更重要。在此基础上，我们确定了实现有效搜索的关键技术，并在多个流行的基准领域中实现了最先进的性能。

TLDR: This paper challenges the common belief that prediction errors are the biggest problem in model-based RL. It argues that search within accurate models can still harm performance due to distribution shift and identifies techniques to enable effective search, resulting in state-of-the-art performance.

简要说明: 本文挑战了模型强化学习中预测误差是最大问题的普遍观点。 它认为，由于分布偏移，即使在精确的模型中进行搜索仍然会损害性能，并提出了实现有效搜索的技术，从而达到最先进的性能。

Authors: Wei-Di Chang, Mikael Henaff, Brandon Amos, Gregory Dudek, Scott Fujimoto

Less Noise, More Voice: Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning via Instruction Purification

Abstract (English): Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has advanced LLM reasoning, but remains constrained by inefficient exploration under limited rollout budgets, leading to low sampling success and unstable training in complex tasks. We find that many exploration failures arise not from problem difficulty, but from a small number of prompt tokens that introduce interference. Building on this insight, we propose the Less Noise Sampling Framework (LENS), which first prompts by identifying and removing interference tokens. then transfers successful rollouts from the purification process to supervise policy optimization on the original noisy prompts, enabling the model to learn to ignore interference in the real-world, noisy prompting settings. Experimental results show that LENS significantly outperforms GRPO, delivering higher performance and faster convergence, with a 3.88% average gain and over 1.6$\times$ speedup. Our work highlights the critical role of pruning interference tokens in improving rollout efficiency, offering a new perspective for RLVR research.

摘要（中文）: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在提升大型语言模型（LLM）推理能力方面取得进展，但仍受限于有限的展开预算下低效的探索，导致在复杂任务中采样成功率低且训练不稳定。我们发现诸多探索失败并非源于问题难度，而是源于少量引入干扰的提示词 tokens。基于此洞察，我们提出一种更少噪音采样框架（LENS），该框架首先通过识别和移除干扰 tokens 来进行提示净化，然后将净化过程中成功的展开转移到原始含噪提示上的监督策略优化，使模型能够在真实的含噪声提示环境中学会忽略干扰。实验结果表明，LENS 比 GRPO 显著更优，实现了更高的性能和更快的收敛速度，平均提升 3.88%，加速超过 1.6 倍。我们的工作强调了剪除干扰 tokens 在提高展开效率方面的关键作用，为 RLVR 研究提供了一个新的视角。

TLDR: This paper introduces LENS, a reinforcement learning framework that improves LLM reasoning by identifying and removing interfering tokens in prompts, resulting in higher performance and faster convergence compared to GRPO.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为LENS的强化学习框架，通过识别和移除提示中的干扰 tokens 来提高 LLM 的推理能力，与 GRPO 相比，性能更高，收敛速度更快。

Authors: Yiju Guo, Tianyi Hu, Zexu Sun, Yankai Lin

Intelli-Planner: Towards Customized Urban Planning via Large Language Model Empowered Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): Effective urban planning is crucial for enhancing residents' quality of life and ensuring societal stability, playing a pivotal role in the sustainable development of cities. Current planning methods heavily rely on human experts, which are time-consuming and labor-intensive, or utilize deep learning algorithms, often limiting stakeholder involvement. To bridge these gaps, we propose Intelli-Planner, a novel framework integrating Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) with large language models (LLMs) to facilitate participatory and customized planning scheme generation. Intelli-Planner utilizes demographic, geographic data, and planning preferences to determine high-level planning requirements and demands for each functional type. During training, a knowledge enhancement module is employed to enhance the decision-making capability of the policy network. Additionally, we establish a multi-dimensional evaluation system and employ LLM-based stakeholders for satisfaction scoring. Experimental validation across diverse urban settings shows that Intelli-Planner surpasses traditional baselines and achieves comparable performance to state-of-the-art DRL-based methods in objective metrics, while enhancing stakeholder satisfaction and convergence speed. These findings underscore the effectiveness and superiority of our framework, highlighting the potential for integrating the latest advancements in LLMs with DRL approaches to revolutionize tasks related to functional areas planning.

摘要（中文）: 有效的城市规划对于提升居民生活质量、确保社会稳定至关重要，在城市可持续发展中发挥着关键作用。当前的规划方法严重依赖于人类专家，耗时费力；或者采用深度学习算法，但往往限制了利益相关者的参与。为弥合这些差距，我们提出Intelli-Planner，一种将深度强化学习(DRL)与大型语言模型(LLM)相结合的新型框架，以促进参与式和定制化的规划方案生成。Intelli-Planner利用人口统计学、地理数据和规划偏好来确定每种功能类型的高级规划需求。在训练过程中，采用知识增强模块来增强策略网络的决策能力。此外，我们建立了一个多维评估系统，并采用基于LLM的利益相关者进行满意度评分。在不同城市环境下的实验验证表明，Intelli-Planner超越了传统基线，并在客观指标上取得了与最先进的基于DRL的方法相当的性能，同时提高了利益相关者的满意度和收敛速度。这些发现强调了我们框架的有效性和优越性，突出了将LLM的最新进展与DRL方法相结合，从而彻底改变与功能区规划相关的任务的潜力。

TLDR: The paper introduces Intelli-Planner, a DRL and LLM integrated framework for urban planning that incorporates stakeholder preferences and outperforms traditional and DRL-based methods.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了Intelli-Planner，一个结合深度强化学习和大型语言模型的城市规划框架，该框架整合了利益相关者的偏好，并且优于传统的和基于深度强化学习的方法。

Authors: Xixian Yong, Peilin Sun, Zihe Wang, Xiao Zhou

Thinker: A vision-language foundation model for embodied intelligence

Abstract (English): When large vision-language models are applied to the field of robotics, they encounter problems that are simple for humans yet error-prone for models. Such issues include confusion between third-person and first-person perspectives and a tendency to overlook information in video endings during temporal reasoning. To address these challenges, we propose Thinker, a large vision-language foundation model designed for embodied intelligence. We tackle the aforementioned issues from two perspectives. Firstly, we construct a large-scale dataset tailored for robotic perception and reasoning, encompassing ego-view videos, visual grounding, spatial understanding, and chain-of-thought data. Secondly, we introduce a simple yet effective approach that substantially enhances the model's capacity for video comprehension by jointly incorporating key frames and full video sequences as inputs. Our model achieves state-of-the-art results on two of the most commonly used benchmark datasets in the field of task planning.

摘要（中文）: 当大型视觉语言模型应用于机器人领域时，它们会遇到对于人类来说很简单但对于模型来说容易出错的问题。这些问题包括第三人称和第一人称视角的混淆，以及在时间推理过程中忽略视频结尾信息的倾向。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了Thinker，一个为具身智能设计的大型视觉语言基础模型。我们从两个角度解决上述问题。首先，我们构建了一个针对机器人感知和推理的大规模数据集，涵盖第一人称视角视频、视觉定位、空间理解以及思维链数据。其次，我们引入了一种简单而有效的方法，通过联合引入关键帧和完整视频序列作为输入，来显著增强模型对视频的理解能力。我们的模型在任务规划领域最常用的两个基准数据集上取得了最先进的结果。

TLDR: The paper introduces Thinker, a vision-language foundation model for embodied intelligence, trained on a large-scale robotics dataset and a novel video comprehension approach, achieving state-of-the-art results in task planning.

Authors: Baiyu Pan, Daqin Luo, Junpeng Yang, Jiyuan Wang, Yixuan Zhang, Hailin Shi, Jichao Jiao

PhaseCoder: Microphone Geometry-Agnostic Spatial Audio Understanding for Multimodal LLMs

Abstract (English): Current multimodal LLMs process audio as a mono stream, ignoring the rich spatial information essential for embodied AI. Existing spatial audio models, conversely, are constrained to fixed microphone geometries, preventing deployment across diverse devices. We present PhaseCoder, a transformer-only spatial audio encoder that is agnostic to microphone geometry. PhaseCoder takes raw multichannel audio and microphone coordinates as inputs to perform localization and produces robust spatial embeddings. We demonstrate that Gemma 3n LLM can be fine-tuned to reason over "Spatial Audio Tokens" produced by PhaseCoder. We show our encoder achieves state-of-the-art results on microphone-invariant localization benchmarks and, for the first time, enables an LLM to perform complex spatial reasoning and targeted transcription tasks from an arbitrary microphone array.

摘要（中文）: 当前的多模态大型语言模型将音频处理为单声道流，忽略了对于具身人工智能至关重要的丰富空间信息。相反，现有的空间音频模型仅限于固定的麦克风几何结构，阻碍了其在各种设备上的部署。我们提出了PhaseCoder，一种仅使用Transformer的空间音频编码器，它与麦克风几何结构无关。PhaseCoder以原始多通道音频和麦克风坐标为输入，执行定位并生成鲁棒的空间嵌入。我们证明了可以对Gemma 3n LLM进行微调，使其能够对PhaseCoder生成的“空间音频令牌”进行推理。我们展示了我们的编码器在麦克风不变定位基准测试中实现了最先进的结果，并且首次使大型语言模型能够从任意麦克风阵列执行复杂的空间推理和目标转录任务。

TLDR: The paper introduces PhaseCoder, a microphone geometry-agnostic spatial audio encoder that enables multimodal LLMs to process spatial audio information from arbitrary microphone arrays for tasks like localization and targeted transcription.

Authors: Artem Dementyev, Wazeer Zulfikar, Sinan Hersek, Pascal Getreuer, Anurag Kumar, Vivek Kumar

CUA-Skill: Develop Skills for Computer Using Agent

Abstract (English): Computer-Using Agents (CUAs) aim to autonomously operate computer systems to complete real-world tasks. However, existing agentic systems remain difficult to scale and lag behind human performance. A key limitation is the absence of reusable and structured skill abstractions that capture how humans interact with graphical user interfaces and how to leverage these skills. We introduce CUA-Skill, a computer-using agentic skill base that encodes human computer-use knowledge as skills coupled with parameterized execution and composition graphs. CUA-Skill is a large-scale library of carefully engineered skills spanning common Windows applications, serving as a practical infrastructure and tool substrate for scalable, reliable agent development. Built upon this skill base, we construct CUA-Skill Agent, an end-to-end computer-using agent that supports dynamic skill retrieval, argument instantiation, and memory-aware failure recovery. Our results demonstrate that CUA-Skill substantially improves execution success rates and robustness on challenging end-to-end agent benchmarks, establishing a strong foundation for future computer-using agent development. On WindowsAgentArena, CUA-Skill Agent achieves state-of-the-art 57.5% (best of three) successful rate while being significantly more efficient than prior and concurrent approaches. The project page is available at https://microsoft.github.io/cua_skill/.

摘要（中文）: 计算机使用代理 (CUAs) 旨在自主操作计算机系统以完成现实世界的任务。然而，现有的代理系统仍然难以扩展，并且落后于人类的性能。一个关键的限制是缺乏可重用且结构化的技能抽象，这些抽象能够捕捉人类与图形用户界面交互的方式以及如何利用这些技能。我们引入了 CUA-Skill，一个计算机使用代理技能库，它将人类计算机使用知识编码为技能，并结合参数化的执行和组合图。CUA-Skill 是一个大规模、精心设计的技能库，涵盖了常见的 Windows 应用程序，为可扩展、可靠的代理开发提供了一个实用的基础设施和工具基础。基于这个技能库，我们构建了 CUA-Skill Agent，一个端到端的计算机使用代理，支持动态技能检索、参数实例化和具有记忆功能的故障恢复。我们的结果表明，CUA-Skill 显著提高了在具有挑战性的端到端代理基准测试中的执行成功率和鲁棒性，为未来的计算机使用代理开发奠定了坚实的基础。在 WindowsAgentArena 上，CUA-Skill Agent 取得了最先进的 57.5% (三选一) 成功率，同时比以前和同时期的方法效率更高。项目主页位于 https://microsoft.github.io/cua_skill/。

TLDR: The paper introduces CUA-Skill, a large-scale library of skills for computer-using agents, which significantly improves performance on end-to-end agent benchmarks compared to existing approaches.

Authors: Tianyi Chen, Yinheng Li, Michael Solodko, Sen Wang, Nan Jiang, Tingyuan Cui, Junheng Hao, Jongwoo Ko, Sara Abdali, Suzhen Zheng, Leon Xu, Hao Fan, Pashmina Cameron, Justin Wagle, Kazuhito Koishida

OpenSec: Measuring Incident Response Agent Calibration Under Adversarial Evidence

Abstract (English): As large language models improve, so do their offensive applications: frontier agents now generate working exploits for under $50 in compute (Heelan, 2026). Defensive incident response (IR) agents must keep pace, but existing benchmarks conflate action execution with correct execution, hiding calibration failures when agents process adversarial evidence. We introduce OpenSec, a dual-control reinforcement learning environment that evaluates IR agents under realistic prompt injection scenarios. Unlike static capability benchmarks, OpenSec scores world-state-changing containment actions under adversarial evidence via execution-based metrics: time-to-first-containment (TTFC), blast radius (false positives per episode), and injection violation rates. Evaluating four frontier models on 40 standard-tier episodes, we find consistent over-triggering in this setting: GPT-5.2, Gemini 3, and DeepSeek execute containment in 100% of episodes with 90-97% false positive rates. Claude Sonnet 4.5 shows partial calibration (85% containment, 72% FP), demonstrating that OpenSec surfaces a calibration failure mode hidden by aggregate success metrics. Code available at https://github.com/jbarnes850/opensec-env.

摘要（中文）: 随着大型语言模型的进步，其攻击性应用也在发展：前沿智能体现在能以低于50美元的计算成本生成可用的漏洞利用程序（Heelan, 2026）。防御性的事件响应(IR)智能体必须跟上步伐，但现有的基准测试会将行动执行与正确执行混淆，在智能体处理对抗性证据时掩盖校准失败。我们引入OpenSec，一个双重控制强化学习环境，用于评估实际提示注入场景下的IR智能体。与静态能力基准测试不同，OpenSec通过基于执行的指标，评估对抗性证据下更改世界状态的遏制行动：首次遏制时间(TTFC)、爆炸半径（每个情节的假阳性数量）和注入违规率。我们评估了40个标准级别的场景中的四个前沿模型，发现该设置中存在一致的过度触发：GPT-5.2、Gemini 3和DeepSeek在100%的情节中执行遏制，假阳性率高达90-97%。Claude Sonnet 4.5表现出部分校准（85%的遏制，72%的假阳性），表明OpenSec揭示了一种被聚合成功指标隐藏的校准失败模式。代码可从https://github.com/jbarnes850/opensec-env获取。

TLDR: The paper introduces OpenSec, a reinforcement learning environment for evaluating incident response agents under adversarial attacks, revealing calibration failures (over-triggering) in frontier models hidden by standard benchmarks.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了OpenSec，一个用于评估事件响应代理在对抗性攻击下的强化学习环境，揭示了前沿模型中被标准基准隐藏的校准失败（过度触发）。

Authors: Jarrod Barnes

Textual Equilibrium Propagation for Deep Compound AI Systems

Abstract (English): Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as part of compound AI systems that coordinate multiple modules (e.g., retrievers, tools, verifiers) over long-horizon workflows. Recent approaches that propagate textual feedback globally (e.g., TextGrad) make it feasible to optimize such pipelines, but we find that performance degrades as system depth grows. In particular, long-horizon agentic workflows exhibit two depth-scaling failure modes: 1) exploding textual gradient, where textual feedback grows exponentially with depth, leading to prohibitively long message and amplifies evaluation biases; and 2) vanishing textual gradient, where limited long-context ability causes models overemphasize partial feedback and compression of lengthy feedback causes downstream messages to lose specificity gradually as they propagate many hops upstream. To mitigate these issues, we introduce Textual Equilibrium Propagation (TEP), a local learning principle inspired by Equilibrium Propagation in energy-based models. TEP includes two phases: 1) a free phase where a local LLM critics iteratively refine prompts until reaching equilibrium (no further improvements are suggested); and 2) a nudged phase which applies proximal prompt edits with bounded modification intensity, using task-level objectives that propagate via forward signaling rather than backward feedback chains. This design supports local prompt optimization followed by controlled adaptation toward global goals without the computational burden and signal degradation of global textual backpropagation. Across long-horizon QA benchmarks and multi-agent tool-use dataset, TEP consistently improves accuracy and efficiency over global propagation methods such as TextGrad. The gains grows with depth, while preserving the practicality of black-box LLM components in deep compound AI system.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地被部署为复合AI系统的一部分，这些系统在长时程工作流中协调多个模块（例如，检索器、工具、验证器）。最近提出的全局传播文本反馈的方法（例如，TextGrad）使得优化此类流水线成为可能，但我们发现随着系统深度的增加，性能会下降。 具体而言，长时程自主工作流表现出两种深度伸缩失效模式：1）文本梯度爆炸，即文本反馈随着深度呈指数增长，导致消息过长并放大评估偏差；以及 2）文本梯度消失，即有限的长上下文能力导致模型过度强调局部反馈，并且冗长反馈的压缩导致下游消息在向上游传播许多跳后逐渐失去特异性。 为了缓解这些问题，我们引入了文本均衡传播（TEP），这是一种受能量模型中的均衡传播启发的局部学习原则。 TEP 包括两个阶段：1）自由阶段，其中局部 LLM 评论家迭代地细化提示，直到达到均衡（不再提出进一步的改进建议）；以及 2）微调阶段，该阶段应用具有有界修改强度的近端提示编辑，使用通过前向信号而不是后向反馈链传播的任务级目标。 这种设计支持局部提示优化，然后是朝着全局目标的可控适应，而无需全局文本反向传播的计算负担和信号退化。 在长时程QA基准测试和多智能体工具使用数据集上，TEP 始终优于全局传播方法（如 TextGrad），从而提高了准确性和效率。 收益随着深度的增加而增长，同时保持了深度复合AI系统中黑盒 LLM 组件的实用性。

TLDR: The paper introduces Textual Equilibrium Propagation (TEP) to address exploding/vanishing textual gradient issues in deep compound AI systems, improving accuracy and efficiency over global backpropagation methods like TextGrad, especially in long-horizon tasks.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了文本均衡传播（TEP），旨在解决深度复合AI系统中出现的文本梯度爆炸/消失问题。相对于全局反向传播方法（如TextGrad），TEP提高了准确性和效率，尤其是在长时程任务中。

Authors: Minghui Chen, Wenlong Deng, James Zou, Han Yu, Xiaoxiao Li

Thinking in Frames: How Visual Context and Test-Time Scaling Empower Video Reasoning

Abstract (English): Vision-Language Models have excelled at textual reasoning, but they often struggle with fine-grained spatial understanding and continuous action planning, failing to simulate the dynamics required for complex visual reasoning. In this work, we formulate visual reasoning by means of video generation models, positing that generated frames can act as intermediate reasoning steps between initial states and solutions. We evaluate their capacity in two distinct regimes: Maze Navigation for sequential discrete planning with low visual change and Tangram Puzzle for continuous manipulation with high visual change. Our experiments reveal three critical insights: (1) Robust Zero-Shot Generalization: In both tasks, the model demonstrates strong performance on unseen data distributions without specific finetuning. (2) Visual Context: The model effectively uses visual context as explicit control, such as agent icons and tangram shapes, enabling it to maintain high visual consistency and adapt its planning capability robustly to unseen patterns. (3) Visual Test-Time Scaling: We observe a test-time scaling law in sequential planning; increasing the generated video length (visual inference budget) empowers better zero-shot generalization to spatially and temporally complex paths. These findings suggest that video generation is not merely a media tool, but a scalable, generalizable paradigm for visual reasoning.

摘要（中文）: 以下是翻译结果： 视觉-语言模型在文本推理方面表现出色，但它们通常难以进行精细的空间理解和连续的动作规划，无法模拟复杂视觉推理所需的动态特性。 在这项工作中，我们通过视频生成模型来构建视觉推理，假定生成的帧可以充当初始状态和解决方案之间的中间推理步骤。 我们在两个不同的场景中评估了它们的能力：用于具有低视觉变化的序列离散规划的迷宫导航，以及用于具有高视觉变化的连续操作的七巧板拼图。 我们的实验揭示了三个关键见解：（1）强大的零样本泛化能力： 在两个任务中，该模型在没有特定微调的情况下，对未见过的数据分布表现出强大的性能。（2）视觉上下文： 该模型有效地利用视觉上下文作为显式控制，例如代理图标和七巧板形状，使其能够保持高的视觉一致性，并使其规划能力能够可靠地适应未见过的模式。（3）视觉测试时缩放： 我们在序列规划中观察到一种测试时缩放规律； 增加生成的视频长度（视觉推理预算）能够更好地零样本泛化到空间和时间上复杂的路径。 这些发现表明，视频生成不仅仅是一种媒体工具，而且是一种可扩展的、可泛化的视觉推理范式。

TLDR: This paper explores using video generation models as intermediate reasoning steps for visual reasoning tasks like maze navigation and tangram puzzles, demonstrating strong zero-shot generalization and the benefits of visual context and test-time scaling.

简要说明: 本文探讨了使用视频生成模型作为视觉推理任务（如迷宫导航和七巧板拼图）的中间推理步骤，展示了强大的零样本泛化能力以及视觉上下文和测试时缩放的优势。

Authors: Chengzu Li, Zanyi Wang, Jiaang Li, Yi Xu, Han Zhou, Huanyu Zhang, Ruichuan An, Dengyang Jiang, Zhaochong An, Ivan Vulić, Serge Belongie, Anna Korhonen

SokoBench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Planning and Reasoning in Large Language Models

Abstract (English): Although the capabilities of large language models have been increasingly tested on complex reasoning tasks, their long-horizon planning abilities have not yet been extensively investigated. In this work, we provide a systematic assessment of the planning and long-horizon reasoning capabilities of state-of-the-art Large Reasoning Models (LRMs). We propose a novel benchmark based on Sokoban puzzles, intentionally simplified to isolate long-horizon planning from state persistence. Our findings reveal a consistent degradation in planning performance when more than 25 moves are required to reach the solution, suggesting a fundamental constraint on forward planning capacity. We show that equipping LRMs with Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL) parsing, validation, and solving tools allows for modest improvements, suggesting inherent architectural limitations which might not be overcome by test-time scaling approaches alone.

摘要（中文）: 尽管大型语言模型的能力已在复杂的推理任务中得到越来越多的测试，但其长期规划能力尚未得到广泛研究。在这项工作中，我们对最先进的大型推理模型（LRM）的规划和长期推理能力进行了系统评估。我们提出了一个基于仓库番谜题的新基准，该谜题经过有意简化，以将长期规划与状态持久性隔离开来。我们的研究结果表明，当需要超过25步才能达到解决方案时，规划性能会持续下降，这表明前向规划能力存在根本性限制。我们表明，为LRM配备规划领域定义语言（PDDL）解析、验证和求解工具可以实现适度的改进，这表明存在固有的架构限制，可能无法仅通过测试时缩放方法来克服。

TLDR: This paper introduces SokoBench, a new benchmark based on simplified Sokoban puzzles, to evaluate the long-horizon planning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), revealing performance degradation beyond 25 moves and inherent architectural limitations.

简要说明: 该论文提出了SokoBench，一个基于简化版推箱子游戏的基准，旨在评估大型语言模型（LLMs）的长期规划能力。研究结果表明，当规划步数超过25步时，性能会下降，暗示了LLMs在长期规划方面的结构性限制。

Authors: Sebastiano Monti, Carlo Nicolini, Gianni Pellegrini, Jacopo Staiano, Bruno Lepri

Open-Vocabulary Functional 3D Human-Scene Interaction Generation

Abstract (English): Generating 3D humans that functionally interact with 3D scenes remains an open problem with applications in embodied AI, robotics, and interactive content creation. The key challenge involves reasoning about both the semantics of functional elements in 3D scenes and the 3D human poses required to achieve functionality-aware interaction. Unfortunately, existing methods typically lack explicit reasoning over object functionality and the corresponding human-scene contact, resulting in implausible or functionally incorrect interactions. In this work, we propose FunHSI, a training-free, functionality-driven framework that enables functionally correct human-scene interactions from open-vocabulary task prompts. Given a task prompt, FunHSI performs functionality-aware contact reasoning to identify functional scene elements, reconstruct their 3D geometry, and model high-level interactions via a contact graph. We then leverage vision-language models to synthesize a human performing the task in the image and estimate proposed 3D body and hand poses. Finally, the proposed 3D body configuration is refined via stage-wise optimization to ensure physical plausibility and functional correctness. In contrast to existing methods, FunHSI not only synthesizes more plausible general 3D interactions, such as "sitting on a sofa'', while supporting fine-grained functional human-scene interactions, e.g., "increasing the room temperature''. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FunHSI consistently generates functionally correct and physically plausible human-scene interactions across diverse indoor and outdoor scenes.

摘要（中文）: 生成能与3D场景进行功能性交互的3D人体仍然是一个开放性问题，它在具身人工智能、机器人技术和交互式内容创作领域有着广泛的应用。其关键挑战在于对3D场景中功能元素的语义，以及实现功能感知交互所需的3D人体姿态进行推理。不幸的是，现有方法通常缺乏对对象功能和相应人-场景接触的显式推理，从而导致不合理或功能上不正确的交互。本文中，我们提出 FunHSI，一个无需训练、由功能驱动的框架，该框架能够从开放词汇的任务提示中实现功能正确的场景交互。给定一个任务提示，FunHSI执行功能感知的接触推理，以识别场景的功能元素，重建其3D几何结构，并通过接触图建模高层交互。然后，我们利用视觉-语言模型来合成图像中执行任务的人体，并估计提出的3D身体和手部姿态。最后，通过阶段式优化来细化提出的3D身体配置，以确保物理上的合理性和功能上的正确性。与现有方法相比，FunHSI不仅合成了更合理的通用3D交互，如“坐在沙发上”，同时还支持细粒度的功能性人-场景交互，例如“升高室内温度”。 广泛的实验表明，FunHSI能够在各种室内和室外场景中始终如一地生成功能正确且物理上合理的人-场景交互。

TLDR: The paper introduces FunHSI, a training-free framework for generating functionally correct 3D human-scene interactions from open-vocabulary prompts, leveraging vision-language models and staged optimization to improve physical plausibility and functional correctness.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为FunHSI的免训练框架，用于从开放词汇提示中生成功能正确的3D人-场景交互。它利用视觉-语言模型和阶段性优化来提高物理合理性和功能正确性。

Authors: Jie Liu, Yu Sun, Alpar Cseke, Yao Feng, Nicolas Heron, Michael J. Black, Yan Zhang

Training Reasoning Models on Saturated Problems via Failure-Prefix Conditioning

Abstract (English): Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs), yet training often stalls as problems become saturated. We identify the core challenge as the poor accessibility of informative failures: learning signals exist but are rarely encountered during standard rollouts. To address this, we propose failure-prefix conditioning, a simple and effective method for learning from saturated problems. Rather than starting from the original question, our approach reallocates exploration by conditioning training on prefixes derived from rare incorrect reasoning trajectories, thereby exposing the model to failure-prone states. We observe that failure-prefix conditioning yields performance gains matching those of training on medium-difficulty problems, while preserving token efficiency. Furthermore, we analyze the model's robustness, finding that our method reduces performance degradation under misleading failure prefixes, albeit with a mild trade-off in adherence to correct early reasoning. Finally, we demonstrate that an iterative approach, which refreshes failure prefixes during training, unlocks additional gains after performance plateaus. Overall, our results suggest that failure-prefix conditioning offers an effective pathway to extend RLVR training on saturated problems.

摘要（中文）: 可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）已显著提升大型语言模型（LLM）的推理能力，但训练经常在问题饱和时停滞。我们认为核心挑战是信息性失败的可访问性差：学习信号存在，但在标准rollout中很少遇到。为了解决这个问题，我们提出失败前缀条件化，这是一种从饱和问题中学习的简单有效的方法。我们的方法并非从原始问题开始，而是通过将训练条件化于源自罕见错误推理轨迹的前缀上，来重新分配探索，从而使模型暴露于易发生失败的状态。我们观察到，失败前缀条件化所产生的性能提升与训练于中等难度问题上的性能提升相当，同时保持了token效率。此外，我们分析了模型的鲁棒性，发现我们的方法降低了在误导性失败前缀下的性能退化，尽管在早期正确推理的坚持度方面存在轻微的权衡。最后，我们证明了迭代方法，即在训练期间刷新失败前缀，可以在性能达到稳定期后解锁额外的收益。总的来说，我们的结果表明，失败前缀条件化为扩展饱和问题上的RLVR训练提供了一条有效的途径。

TLDR: The paper introduces failure-prefix conditioning, a method to improve Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) for LLMs on saturated reasoning problems by conditioning training on prefixes derived from incorrect reasoning trajectories, ultimately leading to performance gains and improved robustness.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种失败前缀条件化方法，通过在不正确的推理轨迹导出的前缀上进行条件训练，改进了大型语言模型在饱和推理问题上的可验证奖励强化学习(RLVR)效果，最终实现了性能提升和鲁棒性提高。

Authors: Minwu Kim, Safal Shrestha, Keith Ross

Drive-JEPA: Video JEPA Meets Multimodal Trajectory Distillation for End-to-End Driving

Abstract (English): End-to-end autonomous driving increasingly leverages self-supervised video pretraining to learn transferable planning representations. However, pretraining video world models for scene understanding has so far brought only limited improvements. This limitation is compounded by the inherent ambiguity of driving: each scene typically provides only a single human trajectory, making it difficult to learn multimodal behaviors. In this work, we propose Drive-JEPA, a framework that integrates Video Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (V-JEPA) with multimodal trajectory distillation for end-to-end driving. First, we adapt V-JEPA for end-to-end driving, pretraining a ViT encoder on large-scale driving videos to produce predictive representations aligned with trajectory planning. Second, we introduce a proposal-centric planner that distills diverse simulator-generated trajectories alongside human trajectories, with a momentum-aware selection mechanism to promote stable and safe behavior. When evaluated on NAVSIM, the V-JEPA representation combined with a simple transformer-based decoder outperforms prior methods by 3 PDMS in the perception-free setting. The complete Drive-JEPA framework achieves 93.3 PDMS on v1 and 87.8 EPDMS on v2, setting a new state-of-the-art.

摘要（中文）: 端到端自动驾驶越来越多地利用自监督视频预训练来学习可迁移的规划表征。然而，至今为止，用于场景理解的预训练视频世界模型带来的提升有限。驾驶的固有歧义性加剧了这种局限性：每个场景通常仅提供单一的人类轨迹，从而难以学习多模态行为。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种名为Drive-JEPA的框架，该框架将视频联合嵌入预测架构（V-JEPA）与用于端到端驾驶的多模态轨迹蒸馏相结合。首先，我们针对端到端驾驶调整V-JEPA，在大规模驾驶视频上预训练一个ViT编码器，以生成与轨迹规划对齐的预测表征。其次，我们引入了一种以提议为中心的规划器，该规划器蒸馏了由模拟器生成的多样性轨迹以及人类轨迹，并采用了一种具有动量感知的选择机制来促进稳定且安全的行为。在NAVSIM上的评估结果表明，结合一个简单的基于Transformer的解码器的V-JEPA表征在无感知设置下比现有方法提高了3个PDMS。完整的Drive-JEPA框架在v1上实现了93.3 PDMS，在v2上实现了87.8 EPDMS，创造了新的最先进水平。

TLDR: The paper introduces Drive-JEPA, which combines V-JEPA with multimodal trajectory distillation for end-to-end autonomous driving, achieving state-of-the-art results on NAVSIM.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了Drive-JEPA，它将V-JEPA与多模态轨迹蒸馏相结合，用于端到端自动驾驶，并在NAVSIM上取得了最先进的结果。

Authors: Linhan Wang, Zichong Yang, Chen Bai, Guoxiang Zhang, Xiaotong Liu, Xiaoyin Zheng, Xiao-Xiao Long, Chang-Tien Lu, Cheng Lu

Visual-Guided Key-Token Regularization for Multimodal Large Language Model Unlearning

Abstract (English): Unlearning in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) prevents the model from revealing private information when queried about target images. Existing MLLM unlearning methods largely adopt approaches developed for LLMs. They treat all answer tokens uniformly, disregarding their varying importance in the unlearning process. Moreover, these methods focus exclusively on the language modality, disregarding visual cues that indicate key tokens in answers. In this paper, after formulating the problem of unlearning in multimodal question answering for MLLMs, we propose Visual-Guided Key-Token Regularization (ViKeR). We leverage irrelevant visual inputs to predict ideal post-unlearning token-level distributions and use these distributions to regularize the unlearning process, thereby prioritizing key tokens. Further, we define key tokens in unlearning via information entropy and discuss ViKeR's effectiveness through token-level gradient reweighting, which amplifies updates on key tokens. Experiments on MLLMU and CLEAR benchmarks demonstrate that our method effectively performs unlearning while mitigating forgetting and maintaining response coherence.

摘要（中文）: 多模态大型语言模型（MLLM）中的遗忘学习旨在防止模型在被询问关于目标图像时泄露私人信息。现有的MLLM遗忘学习方法主要采用为LLM开发的方法。它们统一对待所有答案标记，忽略了它们在遗忘学习过程中的不同重要性。此外，这些方法只关注语言模态，忽略了指示答案中关键token的视觉线索。在本文中，在构建MLLM多模态问题回答中的遗忘学习问题后，我们提出了一种视觉引导的关键Token正则化方法（ViKeR）。我们利用不相关的视觉输入来预测理想的后遗忘学习Token级别分布，并使用这些分布来正则化遗忘学习过程，从而优先考虑关键Token。此外，我们通过信息熵定义遗忘学习中的关键Token，并通过Token级别梯度重加权讨论ViKeR的有效性，该方法放大了关键Token的更新。在MLLMU和CLEAR基准上的实验表明，我们的方法有效地执行了遗忘学习，同时减轻了遗忘并保持了响应的连贯性。

TLDR: This paper introduces Visual-Guided Key-Token Regularization (ViKeR), a novel MLLM unlearning method that leverages visual cues and token-level entropy to prioritize key tokens during unlearning, improving performance on MLLMU and CLEAR benchmarks.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种新的多模态大型语言模型（MLLM）的遗忘方法，即视觉引导的关键令牌正则化（ViKeR）。该方法利用视觉线索和令牌级熵来优先处理遗忘过程中的关键令牌，从而在 MLLMU 和 CLEAR 基准测试中提高了性能。

Authors: Chengyi Cai, Zesheng Ye, Peike Li, Bo Han, Jianzhong Qi, Feng Liu

CG-MLLM: Captioning and Generating 3D content via Multi-modal Large Language Models

Abstract (English): Large Language Models(LLMs) have revolutionized text generation and multimodal perception, but their capabilities in 3D content generation remain underexplored. Existing methods compromise by producing either low-resolution meshes or coarse structural proxies, failing to capture fine-grained geometry natively. In this paper, we propose CG-MLLM, a novel Multi-modal Large Language Model (MLLM) capable of 3D captioning and high-resolution 3D generation in a single framework. Leveraging the Mixture-of-Transformer architecture, CG-MLLM decouples disparate modeling needs, where the Token-level Autoregressive (TokenAR) Transformer handles token-level content, and the Block-level Autoregressive (BlockAR) Transformer handles block-level content. By integrating a pre-trained vision-language backbone with a specialized 3D VAE latent space, CG-MLLM facilitates long-context interactions between standard tokens and spatial blocks within a single integrated architecture. Experimental results show that CG-MLLM significantly outperforms existing MLLMs in generating high-fidelity 3D objects, effectively bringing high-resolution 3D content creation into the mainstream LLM paradigm.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型（LLMs）已经革新了文本生成和多模态感知，但它们在三维内容生成方面的能力仍然未被充分探索。现有方法折衷地生成低分辨率网格或粗略的结构代理，未能原生捕捉精细的几何结构。在本文中，我们提出CG-MLLM，一种新颖的多模态大型语言模型（MLLM），能够在单一框架下实现三维描述和高分辨率三维生成。通过利用混合 Transformer 架构，CG-MLLM 解耦了不同的建模需求，其中令牌级自回归（TokenAR）Transformer 处理令牌级内容，而块级自回归（BlockAR）Transformer 处理块级内容。通过将预训练的视觉-语言骨干网络与专门设计的三维 VAE 潜在空间集成，CG-MLLM 促进了标准令牌和空间块在单一集成架构内的长上下文交互。实验结果表明，CG-MLLM 在生成高保真度三维对象方面显著优于现有的 MLLM，有效地将高分辨率三维内容创建引入到主流 LLM 范式中。

TLDR: CG-MLLM introduces a novel Multi-modal Large Language Model (MLLM) for high-resolution 3D content generation by integrating a vision-language backbone with a specialized 3D VAE latent space, outperforming existing MLLMs.

简要说明: CG-MLLM 提出了一种新颖的多模态大型语言模型（MLLM），通过将视觉语言骨干与专门的 3D VAE 潜在空间集成，用于高分辨率 3D 内容生成，优于现有的 MLLM。

Authors: Junming Huang, Weiwei Xu

Dynamic Topology Awareness: Breaking the Granularity Rigidity in Vision-Language Navigation

Abstract (English): Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) presents a core challenge: grounding high-level linguistic instructions into precise, safe, and long-horizon spatial actions. Explicit topological maps have proven to be a vital solution for providing robust spatial memory in such tasks. However, existing topological planning methods suffer from a "Granularity Rigidity" problem. Specifically, these methods typically rely on fixed geometric thresholds to sample nodes, which fails to adapt to varying environmental complexities. This rigidity leads to a critical mismatch: the model tends to over-sample in simple areas, causing computational redundancy, while under-sampling in high-uncertainty regions, increasing collision risks and compromising precision. To address this, we propose DGNav, a framework for Dynamic Topological Navigation, introducing a context-aware mechanism to modulate map density and connectivity on-the-fly. Our approach comprises two core innovations: (1) A Scene-Aware Adaptive Strategy that dynamically modulates graph construction thresholds based on the dispersion of predicted waypoints, enabling "densification on demand" in challenging environments; (2) A Dynamic Graph Transformer that reconstructs graph connectivity by fusing visual, linguistic, and geometric cues into dynamic edge weights, enabling the agent to filter out topological noise and enhancing instruction adherence. Extensive experiments on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE benchmarks demonstrate DGNav exhibits superior navigation performance and strong generalization capabilities. Furthermore, ablation studies confirm that our framework achieves an optimal trade-off between navigation efficiency and safe exploration. The code is available at https://github.com/shannanshouyin/DGNav.

摘要（中文）: 连续环境下视觉-语言导航（VLN-CE）面临一个核心挑战：将高层次的语言指令转化为精确、安全、且长时程的空间动作。显式的拓扑地图已被证明是提供此类任务中鲁棒空间记忆的关键解决方案。然而，现有的拓扑规划方法存在一个“粒度刚性”问题。具体而言，这些方法通常依赖于固定的几何阈值来采样节点，这无法适应不同的环境复杂度。这种刚性导致一个关键的不匹配：模型倾向于在简单区域过度采样，导致计算冗余，而在高不确定性区域欠采样，增加碰撞风险并损害精度。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了DGNav，一种动态拓扑导航框架，引入了一种上下文感知机制来动态调节地图密度和连通性。我们的方法包含两个核心创新：（1）一种场景感知自适应策略，能够基于预测航路点的离散程度动态调节图构建阈值，在具有挑战性的环境中实现“按需密集化”；（2）一种动态图Transformer，通过将视觉、语言和几何线索融合到动态边权重中来重建图的连通性，使智能体能够过滤掉拓扑噪声并增强指令遵从性。在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上的大量实验表明，DGNav表现出卓越的导航性能和强大的泛化能力。此外，消融研究证实我们的框架在导航效率和安全探索之间实现了最佳权衡。代码可在https://github.com/shannanshouyin/DGNav上获取。

TLDR: The paper introduces DGNav, a dynamic topological navigation framework for VLN-CE that addresses the "Granularity Rigidity" problem by adaptively modulating map density and connectivity based on environmental complexity and fusing multimodal cues for robust navigation.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了DGNav，一个用于VLN-CE的动态拓扑导航框架，通过根据环境复杂性自适应地调整地图密度和连通性，并融合多模态线索以实现稳健的导航，从而解决了“粒度刚性”问题。

Authors: Jiankun Peng, Jianyuan Guo, Ying Xu, Yue Liu, Jiashuang Yan, Xuanwei Ye, Houhua Li, Xiaoming Wang

PathReasoner-R1: Instilling Structured Reasoning into Pathology Vision-Language Model via Knowledge-Guided Policy Optimization

Abstract (English): Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are advancing computational pathology with superior visual understanding capabilities. However, current systems often reduce diagnosis to directly output conclusions without verifiable evidence-linked reasoning, which severely limits clinical trust and hinders expert error rectification. To address these barriers, we construct PathReasoner, the first large-scale dataset of whole-slide image (WSI) reasoning. Unlike previous work reliant on unverified distillation, we develop a rigorous knowledge-guided generation pipeline. By leveraging medical knowledge graphs, we explicitly align structured pathological findings and clinical reasoning with diagnoses, generating over 20K high-quality instructional samples. Based on the database, we propose PathReasoner-R1, which synergizes trajectory-masked supervised fine-tuning with reasoning-oriented reinforcement learning to instill structured chain-of-thought capabilities. To ensure medical rigor, we engineer a knowledge-aware multi-granular reward function incorporating an Entity Reward mechanism strictly aligned with knowledge graphs. This effectively guides the model to optimize for logical consistency rather than mere outcome matching, thereby enhancing robustness. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PathReasoner-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance on both PathReasoner and public benchmarks across various image scales, equipping pathology models with transparent, clinically grounded reasoning capabilities. Dataset and code are available at https://github.com/cyclexfy/PathReasoner-R1.

摘要（中文）: 视觉-语言模型（VLMs）凭借其卓越的视觉理解能力，正在推动计算病理学的发展。然而，当前的系统通常会将诊断简化为直接输出结论，而缺乏可验证的证据关联推理，这严重限制了临床信任，并阻碍了专家进行错误纠正。为了解决这些障碍，我们构建了PathReasoner，这是首个大规模的整张切片图像（WSI）推理数据集。与先前依赖未经验证的知识蒸馏的工作不同，我们开发了一个严谨的知识引导生成流程。通过利用医学知识图谱，我们将结构化的病理学发现和临床推理与诊断进行显式对齐，从而生成超过2万个高质量的指令样本。基于该数据库，我们提出了PathReasoner-R1，它将轨迹掩码的监督微调与面向推理的强化学习相结合，从而赋予模型结构化思维链能力。为了确保医学的严谨性，我们设计了一种知识感知的多粒度奖励函数，其中包含一个严格与知识图谱对齐的实体奖励机制。这有效地引导模型优化逻辑一致性，而非仅仅匹配结果，从而增强鲁棒性。广泛的实验表明，PathReasoner-R1在PathReasoner和公开基准数据集上都实现了最先进的性能，并且涵盖了多种图像尺度，从而使病理学模型具备了透明的、具有临床依据的推理能力。数据集和代码可在https://github.com/cyclexfy/PathReasoner-R1获取。

TLDR: The paper introduces PathReasoner-R1, a novel vision-language model for computational pathology that instills structured reasoning via knowledge-guided policy optimization using a newly constructed dataset of whole-slide image reasoning. It achieves strong performance by promoting logical consistency aligned with medical knowledge graphs.

简要说明: 本文介绍了PathReasoner-R1，一种用于计算病理学的新型视觉-语言模型，通过知识引导的策略优化，利用新构建的全切片图像推理数据集来灌输结构化推理。通过促进与医学知识图谱对齐的逻辑一致性，该模型取得了优异的性能。

Authors: Songhan Jiang, Fengchun Liu, Ziyue Wang, Linghan Cai, Yongbing Zhang

Towards Mitigating Modality Bias in Vision-Language Models for Temporal Action Localization

Abstract (English): Temporal Action Localization (TAL) requires identifying both the boundaries and categories of actions in untrimmed videos. While vision-language models (VLMs) offer rich semantics to complement visual evidence, existing approaches tend to overemphasize linguistic priors at the expense of visual performance, leading to a pronounced modality bias. We propose ActionVLM, a vision-language aggregation framework that systematically mitigates modality bias in TAL. Our key insight is to preserve vision as the dominant signal while adaptively exploiting language only when beneficial. To this end, we introduce (i) a debiasing reweighting module that estimates the language advantage-the incremental benefit of language over vision-only predictions-and dynamically reweights language modality accordingly, and (ii) a residual aggregation strategy that treats language as a complementary refinement rather than the primary driver. This combination alleviates modality bias, reduces overconfidence from linguistic priors, and strengthens temporal reasoning. Experiments on THUMOS14 show that our model outperforms state-of-the-art by up to 3.2% mAP.

摘要（中文）: 时间动作定位 (TAL) 需要识别未裁剪视频中动作的边界和类别。虽然视觉-语言模型 (VLM) 提供了丰富的语义来补充视觉证据，但现有方法往往过度强调语言先验，牺牲了视觉性能，导致显著的模态偏见。我们提出 ActionVLM，一个视觉-语言聚合框架，系统地缓解 TAL 中的模态偏见。我们的关键洞察在于保持视觉作为主导信号，并且仅在有益时自适应地利用语言。为此，我们引入了 (i) 一个去偏重加权模块，用于估计语言优势——语言相对于仅视觉预测的增量收益——并相应地动态重加权语言模态，以及 (ii) 一个残差聚合策略，将语言视为互补的精细化，而不是主要驱动因素。这种结合减轻了模态偏见，减少了来自语言先验的过度自信，并加强了时间推理。在 THUMOS14 上的实验表明，我们的模型优于最先进的方法，mAP 提升高达 3.2%。

TLDR: This paper introduces ActionVLM, a vision-language framework for temporal action localization that mitigates modality bias by prioritizing visual information and adaptively integrating language, achieving state-of-the-art results on THUMOS14.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种名为ActionVLM的视觉-语言框架，用于时序动作定位，通过优先考虑视觉信息并自适应地整合语言来减轻模态偏差，并在THUMOS14上取得了最先进的结果。

Authors: Jiaqi Li, Guangming Wang, Shuntian Zheng, Minzhe Ni, Xiaoman Lu, Guanghui Ye, Yu Guan

Constrained Meta Reinforcement Learning with Provable Test-Time Safety

Abstract (English): Meta reinforcement learning (RL) allows agents to leverage experience across a distribution of tasks on which the agent can train at will, enabling faster learning of optimal policies on new test tasks. Despite its success in improving sample complexity on test tasks, many real-world applications, such as robotics and healthcare, impose safety constraints during testing. Constrained meta RL provides a promising framework for integrating safety into meta RL. An open question in constrained meta RL is how to ensure the safety of the policy on the real-world test task, while reducing the sample complexity and thus, enabling faster learning of optimal policies. To address this gap, we propose an algorithm that refines policies learned during training, with provable safety and sample complexity guarantees for learning a near optimal policy on the test tasks. We further derive a matching lower bound, showing that this sample complexity is tight.

摘要（中文）: 元强化学习（RL）允许智能体利用其在可随意训练的任务分布上的经验，从而能够更快地学习新测试任务下的最优策略。尽管其在提高测试任务上的样本复杂度方面取得了成功，但许多现实世界的应用，如机器人和医疗保健，在测试期间都施加了安全约束。约束元强化学习为将安全性集成到元强化学习中提供了一个有前景的框架。约束元强化学习中的一个开放性问题是如何确保在实际测试任务中策略的安全性，同时降低样本复杂度，从而更快地学习最优策略。为了解决这一差距，我们提出了一种算法，该算法优化了训练期间学习的策略，并具有在测试任务上学习近最优策略的可证明的安全性和样本复杂度保证。我们进一步导出了一个匹配的下界，表明该样本复杂度是紧的。

TLDR: This paper proposes a constrained meta-RL algorithm with provable safety guarantees and tight sample complexity bounds, addressing safety concerns in real-world applications during test time.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种约束元强化学习算法，具有可证明的安全保证和紧密的样本复杂度界限，解决了现实世界应用测试期间的安全问题。

Authors: Tingting Ni, Maryam Kamgarpour

Generalized Information Gathering Under Dynamics Uncertainty

Abstract (English): An agent operating in an unknown dynamical system must learn its dynamics from observations. Active information gathering accelerates this learning, but existing methods derive bespoke costs for specific modeling choices: dynamics models, belief update procedures, observation models, and planners. We present a unifying framework that decouples these choices from the information-gathering cost by explicitly exposing the causal dependencies between parameters, beliefs, and controls. Using this framework, we derive a general information-gathering cost based on Massey's directed information that assumes only Markov dynamics with additive noise and is otherwise agnostic to modeling choices. We prove that the mutual information cost used in existing literature is a special case of our cost. Then, we leverage our framework to establish an explicit connection between the mutual information cost and information gain in linearized Bayesian estimation, thereby providing theoretical justification for mutual information-based active learning approaches. Finally, we illustrate the practical utility of our framework through experiments spanning linear, nonlinear, and multi-agent systems.

摘要（中文）: 摘要： 在未知动态系统中运行的智能体必须从观测中学习其动态特性。主动信息收集能够加速这一学习过程，但现有方法针对特定的建模选择（动力学模型、置信度更新过程、观测模型和规划器）导出定制化的代价函数。我们提出了一种统一框架，通过显式地揭示参数、置信度和控制之间的因果依赖关系，从而将这些选择与信息收集代价解耦。利用这个框架，我们推导出一种基于梅西有向信息的通用信息收集代价，该代价仅假设具有加性噪声的马尔可夫动态系统，而对其他的建模选择不可知。我们证明了现有文献中使用的互信息代价是我们的代价的一个特例。然后，我们利用我们的框架在线性化贝叶斯估计中建立了互信息代价和信息增益之间的显式联系，从而为基于互信息的主动学习方法提供了理论依据。最后，我们通过涵盖线性、非线性和多智能体系统的实验，展示了我们框架的实际效用。

TLDR: The paper presents a generalized framework for active information gathering in unknown dynamical systems, decoupling modeling choices from the information-gathering cost and establishing a connection between mutual information and information gain in linearized Bayesian estimation.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一个用于在未知动力系统中主动信息收集的通用框架，将建模选择与信息收集成本分离，并在线性化贝叶斯估计中建立了互信息和信息增益之间的联系。

From Instruction to Event: Sound-Triggered Mobile Manipulation

Abstract (English): Current mobile manipulation research predominantly follows an instruction-driven paradigm, where agents rely on predefined textual commands to execute tasks. However, this setting confines agents to a passive role, limiting their autonomy and ability to react to dynamic environmental events. To address these limitations, we introduce sound-triggered mobile manipulation, where agents must actively perceive and interact with sound-emitting objects without explicit action instructions. To support these tasks, we develop Habitat-Echo, a data platform that integrates acoustic rendering with physical interaction. We further propose a baseline comprising a high-level task planner and low-level policy models to complete these tasks. Extensive experiments show that the proposed baseline empowers agents to actively detect and respond to auditory events, eliminating the need for case-by-case instructions. Notably, in the challenging dual-source scenario, the agent successfully isolates the primary source from overlapping acoustic interference to execute the first interaction, and subsequently proceeds to manipulate the secondary object, verifying the robustness of the baseline.

摘要（中文）: 当前移动操作研究主要遵循指令驱动范式，其中智能体依赖预定义的文本命令来执行任务。然而，这种设置将智能体限制在一个被动的角色，限制了它们的自主性和对动态环境事件的反应能力。为了解决这些局限性，我们引入了声音触发的移动操作，其中智能体必须主动感知并与发声物体交互，而无需明确的动作指令。为了支持这些任务，我们开发了 Habitat-Echo，一个集成了声学渲染和物理交互的数据平台。我们进一步提出了一个包含高层任务规划器和低层策略模型的基线，以完成这些任务。大量的实验表明，所提出的基线能够使智能体主动检测和响应听觉事件，无需针对具体案例给出指令。值得注意的是，在具有挑战性的双源场景中，智能体成功地将主要声源从重叠的声学干扰中分离出来以执行首次交互，并随后继续操作次要物体，验证了该基线的鲁棒性。

Authors: Hao Ju, Shaofei Huang, Hongyu Li, Zihan Ding, Si Liu, Meng Wang, Zhedong Zheng

Towards Space-Based Environmentally-Adaptive Grasping

Abstract (English): Robotic manipulation in unstructured environments requires reliable execution under diverse conditions, yet many state-of-the-art systems still struggle with high-dimensional action spaces, sparse rewards, and slow generalization beyond carefully curated training scenarios. We study these limitations through the example of grasping in space environments. We learn control policies directly in a learned latent manifold that fuses (grammarizes) multiple modalities into a structured representation for policy decision-making. Building on GPU-accelerated physics simulation, we instantiate a set of single-shot manipulation tasks and achieve over 95% task success with Soft Actor-Critic (SAC)-based reinforcement learning in less than 1M environment steps, under continuously varying grasping conditions from step 1. This empirically shows faster convergence than representative state-of-the-art visual baselines under the same open-loop single-shot conditions. Our analysis indicates that explicitly reasoning in latent space yields more sample-efficient learning and improved robustness to novel object and gripper geometries, environmental clutter, and sensor configurations compared to standard baselines. We identify remaining limitations and outline directions toward fully adaptive and generalizable grasping in the extreme conditions of space.

摘要（中文）: 在非结构化环境中进行机器人操控需要在各种条件下可靠执行，然而许多最先进的系统仍然在高维动作空间、稀疏奖励以及无法推广到精心设计的训练场景之外的问题上挣扎。我们以空间环境中的抓取为例来研究这些局限性。我们直接在学习到的潜在流形中学习控制策略，该潜在流形将多种模态融合（语法化）成结构化的表示用于策略决策。基于GPU加速的物理仿真，我们实例化了一组单次操作任务，并在连续变化抓取条件的情况下，从第一步起，利用基于软演员-评论家（SAC）的强化学习，在不到100万个环境步数内实现了超过95%的任务成功率。这经验性地表明，在相同的开环、单次操作条件下，收敛速度比具有代表性的最先进视觉基线更快。我们的分析表明，与标准基线相比，在潜在空间中进行显式推理可以产生更高效的样本学习，并提高对新对象和夹具几何形状、环境杂物以及传感器配置的鲁棒性。我们识别了剩余的局限性，并概述了在空间极端条件下实现完全自适应和可泛化的抓取的发展方向。

TLDR: The paper explores reinforcement learning for robotic grasping in simulated space environments using a learned latent manifold, achieving high success rates and faster convergence compared to visual baselines, with improved robustness to varying conditions.

Abstract (English): Wheelchairs and robotic arms enhance independent living by assisting individuals with upper-body and mobility limitations in their activities of daily living (ADLs). Although recent advancements in assistive robotics have focused on Wheelchair-Mounted Robotic Arms (WMRAs) and wheelchairs separately, integrated and unified control of the combination using machine learning models remains largely underexplored. To fill this gap, we introduce the concept of WheelArm, an integrated cyber-physical system (CPS) that combines wheelchair and robotic arm controls. Data collection is the first step toward developing WheelArm models. In this paper, we present WheelArm-Sim, a simulation framework developed in Isaac Sim for synthetic data collection. Authors: Guangping Liu, Tipu Sultan, Vittorio Di Giorgio, Nick Hawkins, Flavio Esposito, Madi Babaiasl

AI-Augmented Density-Driven Optimal Control (D2OC) for Decentralized Environmental Mapping

Abstract (English): This paper presents an AI-augmented decentralized framework for multi-agent (multi-robot) environmental mapping under limited sensing and communication. While conventional coverage formulations achieve effective spatial allocation when an accurate reference map is available, their performance deteriorates under uncertain or biased priors. The proposed method introduces an adaptive and self-correcting mechanism that enables agents to iteratively refine local density estimates within an optimal transport-based framework, ensuring theoretical consistency and scalability. A dual multilayer perceptron (MLP) module enhances adaptivity by inferring local mean-variance statistics and regulating virtual uncertainty for long-unvisited regions, mitigating stagnation around local minima. Theoretical analysis rigorously proves convergence under the Wasserstein metric, while simulation results demonstrate that the proposed AI-augmented Density-Driven Optimal Control consistently achieves robust and precise alignment with the ground-truth density, yielding substantially higher-fidelity reconstruction of complex multi-modal spatial distributions compared with conventional decentralized baselines.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出了一种AI增强的去中心化框架，用于在有限感知和通信条件下进行多智能体（多机器人）环境地图构建。传统的覆盖范围公式在有精确参考地图时能实现有效的空间分配，但在不确定或有偏先验条件下，其性能会下降。本文提出的方法引入了一种自适应和自校正机制，使智能体能够在基于最优传输的框架内迭代地精炼局部密度估计，从而确保理论一致性和可扩展性。一个双重多层感知机（MLP）模块通过推断局部均值-方差统计量并调节长期未访问区域的虚拟不确定性来增强自适应性，从而减轻围绕局部最小值停滞的现象。理论分析严格证明了在Wasserstein度量下的收敛性，仿真结果表明，所提出的AI增强密度驱动最优控制能够持续实现与真实密度稳健而精确的对齐，与传统的去中心化基线方法相比，显著提高了对复杂多模态空间分布的重建保真度。

TLDR: This paper introduces an AI-augmented decentralized control framework for multi-agent environmental mapping, utilizing density-driven optimal transport and MLPs to improve the accuracy and robustness of map reconstruction in uncertain environments.

Multi-Robot Decentralized Collaborative SLAM in Planetary Analogue Environments: Dataset, Challenges, and Lessons Learned

Abstract (English): Decentralized collaborative simultaneous localization and mapping (C-SLAM) is essential to enable multirobot missions in unknown environments without relying on preexisting localization and communication infrastructure. This technology is anticipated to play a key role in the exploration of the Moon, Mars, and other planets. In this article, we share insights and lessons learned from C-SLAM experiments involving three robots operating on a Mars analogue terrain and communicating over an ad hoc network. We examine the impact of limited and intermittent communication on C-SLAM performance, as well as the unique localization challenges posed by planetary-like environments. Additionally, we introduce a novel dataset collected during our experiments, which includes real-time peer-to-peer inter-robot throughput and latency measurements. This dataset aims to support future research on communication-constrained, decentralized multirobot operations.

Authors: Pierre-Yves Lajoie, Karthik Soma, Haechan Mark Bong, Alice Lemieux-Bourque, Rongge Zhang, Vivek Shankar Varadharajan, Giovanni Beltrame

Track-centric Iterative Learning for Global Trajectory Optimization in Autonomous Racing

Abstract (English): This paper presents a global trajectory optimization framework for minimizing lap time in autonomous racing under uncertain vehicle dynamics. Optimizing the trajectory over the full racing horizon is computationally expensive, and tracking such a trajectory in the real world hardly assures global optimality due to uncertain dynamics. Yet, existing work mostly focuses on dynamics learning at the tracking level, without updating the trajectory itself to account for the learned dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose a track-centric approach that directly learns and optimizes the full-horizon trajectory. We first represent trajectories through a track-agnostic parametric space in light of the wavelet transform. This space is then efficiently explored using Bayesian optimization, where the lap time of each candidate is evaluated by running simulations with the learned dynamics. This optimization is embedded in an iterative learning framework, where the optimized trajectory is deployed to collect real-world data for updating the dynamics, progressively refining the trajectory over the iterations. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is validated through simulations and real-world experiments, demonstrating lap time improvement of up to 20.7% over a nominal baseline and consistently outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出了一种全局轨迹优化框架，用于在不确定的车辆动力学条件下最小化自动驾驶赛车的单圈时间。优化整个赛道的轨迹计算成本很高，并且由于动力学的不确定性，在现实世界中跟踪这样的轨迹很难保证全局最优性。然而，现有的工作主要集中在跟踪层面的动力学学习，而没有更新轨迹本身以考虑学习到的动力学。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了一种以赛道为中心的方法，该方法直接学习和优化整个赛道的轨迹。我们首先借助小波变换，通过一个与赛道无关的参数化空间来表示轨迹。然后使用贝叶斯优化有效地探索该空间，其中通过运行使用学习到的动力学进行的仿真来评估每个候选轨迹的单圈时间。这种优化嵌入在一个迭代学习框架中，其中部署优化的轨迹以收集真实世界的数据，用于更新动力学，从而在迭代中逐步完善轨迹。通过仿真和真实世界的实验验证了所提出的框架的有效性，结果表明，与标称基线相比，单圈时间最多提高了20.7%，并且始终优于最先进的方法。

TLDR: This paper presents an iterative learning framework for global trajectory optimization in autonomous racing, using Bayesian optimization over a wavelet-transformed trajectory space and real-world data to refine dynamics and improve lap times.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种用于自主赛车全局轨迹优化的迭代学习框架，该框架使用贝叶斯优化在小波变换的轨迹空间中进行优化，并利用真实世界的数据来改进动力学模型并缩短单圈时间。

Authors: Youngim Nam, Jungbin Kim, Kyungtae Kang, Cheolhyeon Kwon

Unsupervised Decomposition and Recombination with Discriminator-Driven Diffusion Models

Abstract (English): Decomposing complex data into factorized representations can reveal reusable components and enable synthesizing new samples via component recombination. We investigate this in the context of diffusion-based models that learn factorized latent spaces without factor-level supervision. In images, factors can capture background, illumination, and object attributes; in robotic videos, they can capture reusable motion components. To improve both latent factor discovery and quality of compositional generation, we introduce an adversarial training signal via a discriminator trained to distinguish between single-source samples and those generated by recombining factors across sources. By optimizing the generator to fool this discriminator, we encourage physical and semantic consistency in the resulting recombinations. Our method outperforms implementations of prior baselines on CelebA-HQ, Virtual KITTI, CLEVR, and Falcor3D, achieving lower FID scores and better disentanglement as measured by MIG and MCC. Furthermore, we demonstrate a novel application to robotic video trajectories: by recombining learned action components, we generate diverse sequences that significantly increase state-space coverage for exploration on the LIBERO benchmark.

摘要（中文）: 将复杂数据分解为因子化表示可以揭示可重用的组件，并通过组件重组合成新的样本。我们研究了基于扩散模型的上下文，该模型学习无需因子级别监督的因子化潜在空间。 在图像中，因子可以捕获背景、光照和对象属性；在机器人视频中，它们可以捕获可重用的运动组件。为了提高隐变量因子发现和组合生成质量，我们引入了一种对抗性训练信号，通过训练一个判别器来区分单源样本和通过跨源重组因子生成的样本。通过优化生成器来欺骗这个判别器，我们鼓励在结果重组中保持物理和语义一致性。我们的方法优于先前基线在CelebA-HQ、Virtual KITTI、CLEVR和Falcor3D上的实现，实现了更低的FID分数，以及更好的解耦性，如MIG和MCC所衡量。此外，我们展示了一种机器人视频轨迹的新颖应用：通过重组学习到的动作组件，我们生成了多样的序列，显著提高了LIBERO基准上探索的状态空间覆盖率。

TLDR: The paper presents an unsupervised method for decomposing data into factorized representations using diffusion models and an adversarial discriminator, enabling compositional generation and improving performance on image and robotic video datasets.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型和对抗判别器的无监督方法，将数据分解为分解表示，从而实现组合生成，并提高图像和机器人视频数据集的性能。

SIA: Symbolic Interpretability for Anticipatory Deep Reinforcement Learning in Network Control

Abstract (English): Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) promises adaptive control for future mobile networks but conventional agents remain reactive: they act on past and current measurements and cannot leverage short-term forecasts of exogenous KPIs such as bandwidth. Augmenting agents with predictions can overcome this temporal myopia, yet uptake in networking is scarce because forecast-aware agents act as closed-boxes; operators cannot tell whether predictions guide decisions or justify the added complexity. We propose SIA, the first interpreter that exposes in real time how forecast-augmented DRL agents operate. SIA fuses Symbolic AI abstractions with per-KPI Knowledge Graphs to produce explanations, and includes a new Influence Score metric. SIA achieves sub-millisecond speed, over 200x faster than existing XAI methods. We evaluate SIA on three diverse networking use cases, uncovering hidden issues, including temporal misalignment in forecast integration and reward-design biases that trigger counter-productive policies. These insights enable targeted fixes: a redesigned agent achieves a 9% higher average bitrate in video streaming, and SIA's online Action-Refinement module improves RAN-slicing reward by 25% without retraining. By making anticipatory DRL transparent and tunable, SIA lowers the barrier to proactive control in next-generation mobile networks.

摘要（中文）: 深度强化学习（DRL）有望为未来移动网络提供自适应控制，但传统智能体仍保持反应性：它们基于过去和当前的测量数据进行动作，无法利用诸如带宽等外生KPI的短期预测。用预测增强智能体可以克服这种时间短视性，但在网络领域的应用仍然稀少，因为预测感知智能体以封闭盒子的形式存在；运营商无法判断预测是否指导了决策，也无法证明增加复杂性的合理性。我们提出了SIA，这是第一个实时揭示预测增强型DRL智能体如何运作的解释器。SIA融合了符号人工智能抽象和按KPI的知识图谱来生成解释，并包含一个新的影响评分指标。SIA实现了亚毫秒级的速度，比现有的可解释人工智能（XAI）方法快200多倍。我们在三种不同的网络用例中评估了SIA，揭示了隐藏的问题，包括预测整合中的时间不对齐以及触发反生产策略的奖励设计偏差。这些见解促成了有针对性的修复：重新设计的智能体在视频流中实现了平均比特率提高9%，并且SIA的在线动作优化模块在不进行重新训练的情况下将RAN切片奖励提高了25%。通过使预测性DRL透明且可调，SIA降低了下一代移动网络中主动控制的门槛。

Learning to Communicate Across Modalities: Perceptual Heterogeneity in Multi-Agent Systems

Abstract (English): Emergent communication offers insight into how agents develop shared structured representations, yet most research assumes homogeneous modalities or aligned representational spaces, overlooking the perceptual heterogeneity of real-world settings. We study a heterogeneous multi-step binary communication game where agents differ in modality and lack perceptual grounding. Despite perceptual misalignment, multimodal systems converge to class-consistent messages grounded in perceptual input. Unimodal systems communicate more efficiently, using fewer bits and achieving lower classification entropy, while multimodal agents require greater information exchange and exhibit higher uncertainty. Bit perturbation experiments provide strong evidence that meaning is encoded in a distributional rather than compositional manner, as each bit's contribution depends on its surrounding pattern. Finally, interoperability analyses show that systems trained in different perceptual worlds fail to directly communicate, but limited fine-tuning enables successful cross-system communication. This work positions emergent communication as a framework for studying how agents adapt and transfer representations across heterogeneous modalities, opening new directions for both theory and experimentation.

摘要（中文）: 涌现通信为研究智能体如何发展共享的结构化表示提供了一种视角，然而，大多数研究都假设同质模态或对齐的表征空间，忽略了现实世界环境中感知异质性的问题。我们研究了一个异质多步二进制通信博弈，其中智能体在模态上存在差异，且缺乏感知基础。尽管存在感知错位，多模态系统仍能收敛到基于感知输入且类别一致的消息。单模态系统通信效率更高，使用的比特更少，并且分类熵更低，而多模态智能体需要更大的信息交换量，并表现出更高的不确定性。比特扰动实验提供了强有力的证据，表明意义是以分布式的而非组合式的方式编码的，因为每个比特的贡献取决于其周围的模式。最后，互操作性分析表明，在不同感知世界中训练的系统无法直接通信，但有限的微调能够实现成功的跨系统通信。这项工作将涌现通信定位为一个研究智能体如何在异质模态间进行表征适应和迁移的框架，为理论和实验开辟了新的方向。

TLDR: This paper explores emergent communication in multi-agent systems with heterogeneous modalities and perceptual misalignment, finding that while unimodal systems are more efficient, multimodal systems can learn to communicate with fine-tuning even across different perceptual worlds.
