SymbXRL: Symbolic Explainable Deep Reinforcement Learning for Mobile Networks

Abstract (English): The operation of future 6th-generation (6G) mobile networks will increasingly rely on the ability of deep reinforcement learning (DRL) to optimize network decisions in real-time. DRL yields demonstrated efficacy in various resource allocation problems, such as joint decisions on user scheduling and antenna allocation or simultaneous control of computing resources and modulation. However, trained DRL agents are closed-boxes and inherently difficult to explain, which hinders their adoption in production settings. In this paper, we make a step towards removing this critical barrier by presenting SymbXRL, a novel technique for explainable reinforcement learning (XRL) that synthesizes human-interpretable explanations for DRL agents. SymbXRL leverages symbolic AI to produce explanations where key concepts and their relationships are described via intuitive symbols and rules; coupling such a representation with logical reasoning exposes the decision process of DRL agents and offers more comprehensible descriptions of their behaviors compared to existing approaches. We validate SymbXRL in practical network management use cases supported by DRL, proving that it not only improves the semantics of the explanations but also paves the way for explicit agent control: for instance, it enables intent-based programmatic action steering that improves by 12% the median cumulative reward over a pure DRL solution.

Authors: Abhishek Duttagupta, MohammadErfan Jabbari, Claudio Fiandrino, Marco Fiore, Joerg Widmer

Geometry of Drifting MDPs with Path-Integral Stability Certificates

Abstract (English): Real-world reinforcement learning is often \emph{nonstationary}: rewards and dynamics drift, accelerate, oscillate, and trigger abrupt switches in the optimal action. Existing theory often represents nonstationarity with coarse-scale models that measure \emph{how much} the environment changes, not \emph{how} it changes locally -- even though acceleration and near-ties drive tracking error and policy chattering. We take a geometric view of nonstationary discounted Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) by modeling the environment as a differentiable homotopy path and tracking the induced motion of the optimal Bellman fixed point. This yields a length-curvature-kink signature of intrinsic complexity: cumulative drift, acceleration/oscillation, and action-gap-induced nonsmoothness. We prove a solver-agnostic path-integral stability bound and derive gap-safe feasible regions that certify local stability away from switch regimes. Building on these results, we introduce \textit{Homotopy-Tracking RL (HT-RL)} and \textit{HT-MCTS}, lightweight wrappers that estimate replay-based proxies of length, curvature, and near-tie proximity online and adapt learning or planning intensity accordingly. Experiments show improved tracking and dynamic regret over matched static baselines, with the largest gains in oscillatory and switch-prone regimes.

摘要（中文）: 现实世界的强化学习通常是\emph{非平稳的}：奖励和动力学漂移、加速、振荡，并触发最优行为的突变。现有的理论通常使用粗粒度的模型来表示非平稳性，这些模型衡量的是环境变化了\emph{多少}，而不是\emph{如何}在局部变化 —— 即便加速和近平局驱动了追踪误差和策略抖动。我们通过将环境建模为可微同伦路径，并追踪最优贝尔曼不动点的诱导运动，从而对非平稳折扣马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）采取一种几何视角。这产生了一种内在复杂度的长度-曲率-扭结签名：累积漂移、加速/振荡和动作间隙诱导的非光滑性。我们证明了一个求解器无关的路径积分稳定性界，并推导出间隙安全的，切实可行的区域，用于验证远离切换区域的局部稳定性。基于这些结果，我们引入了\textit{同伦追踪强化学习 (HT-RL)} 和 \textit{HT-MCTS}，它们是轻量级的包装器，在线估计基于回放的长度、曲率和近平局近似值， 并相应地调整学习或规划强度。实验表明，相对于匹配的静态基线，在追踪和动态遗憾方面均有所改善，尤其是在振荡和易于切换的区域中 gains 提升最大。

TLDR: This paper introduces a geometric approach to nonstationary reinforcement learning, modeling environmental changes as a differentiable homotopy path and developing algorithms (HT-RL, HT-MCTS) that adapt learning intensity based on estimated path characteristics, showing improved tracking and dynamic regret.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种非平稳强化学习的几何方法，将环境变化建模为可微同伦路径，并开发了算法（HT-RL、HT-MCTS），该算法基于估计的路径特征调整学习强度，从而改善跟踪和动态后悔值。

Authors: Zuyuan Zhang, Mahdi Imani, Tian Lan

From Particles to Agents: Hallucination as a Metric for Cognitive Friction in Spatial Simulation

Abstract (English): Traditional architectural simulations (e.g. Computational Fluid Dynamics, evacuation, structural analysis) model elements as deterministic physics-based "particles" rather than cognitive "agents". To bridge this, we introduce \textbf{Agentic Environmental Simulations}, where Large Multimodal generative models actively predict the next state of spatial environments based on semantic expectation. Drawing on examples from accessibility-oriented AR pipelines and multimodal digital twins, we propose a shift from chronological time-steps to Episodic Spatial Reasoning, where simulations advance through meaningful, surprisal-triggered events. Within this framework we posit AI hallucinations as diagnostic tools. By formalizing the \textbf{Cognitive Friction} ($C_f$) it is possible to reveal "Phantom Affordances", i.e. semiotic ambiguities in built space. Finally, we challenge current HCI paradigms by treating environments as dynamic cognitive partners and propose a human-centered framework of cognitive orchestration for designing AI-driven simulations that preserve autonomy, affective clarity, and cognitive integrity.

摘要（中文）: 传统建筑模拟（例如计算流体动力学、疏散、结构分析）将元素建模为基于确定性物理的“粒子”，而非认知“主体”。为了弥合这一点，我们引入了\textbf{主体环境模拟}，其中大型多模态生成模型基于语义期望主动预测空间环境的下一状态。借鉴面向可访问性的AR流水线和多模态数字孪生的例子，我们提出从按时间顺序的时间步进过渡到情景空间推理，其中模拟通过有意义的、由惊奇触发的事件推进。在此框架内，我们将AI幻觉视为诊断工具。通过形式化\textbf{认知摩擦} ($C_f$)，可以揭示“幽灵可供性”，即建筑空间中的符号歧义。最后，我们通过将环境视为动态认知伙伴来挑战当前的人机交互范式，并提出一种以人为中心的认知编排框架，用于设计由AI驱动的模拟，以保持自主性、情感清晰度和认知完整性。

TLDR: This paper introduces Agentic Environmental Simulations, using AI hallucinations as a metric for 'Cognitive Friction' in spatial environments, promoting a shift to Episodic Spatial Reasoning and challenging current HCI paradigms.

Authors: Javier Argota Sánchez-Vaquerizo, Luis Borunda Monsivais

When does predictive inverse dynamics outperform behavior cloning?

Abstract (English): Behavior cloning (BC) is a practical offline imitation learning method, but it often fails when expert demonstrations are limited. Recent works have introduced a class of architectures named predictive inverse dynamics models (PIDM) that combine a future state predictor with an inverse dynamics model (IDM). While PIDM often outperforms BC, the reasons behind its benefits remain unclear. In this paper, we provide a theoretical explanation: PIDM introduces a bias-variance tradeoff. While predicting the future state introduces bias, conditioning the IDM on the prediction can significantly reduce variance. We establish conditions on the state predictor bias for PIDM to achieve lower prediction error and higher sample efficiency than BC, with the gap widening when additional data sources are available. We validate the theoretical insights empirically in 2D navigation tasks, where BC requires up to five times (three times on average) more demonstrations than PIDM to reach comparable performance; and in a complex 3D environment in a modern video game with high-dimensional visual inputs and stochastic transitions, where BC requires over 66\% more samples than PIDM.

摘要（中文）: 行为克隆（BC）是一种实用的离线模仿学习方法，但当专家演示数据有限时，它通常会失败。最近的研究引入了一种名为预测逆动力学模型（PIDM）的架构，该架构结合了未来状态预测器和逆动力学模型（IDM）。虽然PIDM通常优于BC，但其优势背后的原因尚不清楚。在本文中，我们给出一个理论解释：PIDM引入了偏差-方差权衡。虽然预测未来状态会引入偏差，但在预测上调节IDM可以显著降低方差。我们建立了PIDM实现比BC更低的预测误差和更高的样本效率的状态预测器偏差的条件，且当有额外的数据源可用时，差距会扩大。我们在二维导航任务中通过实验验证了这些理论见解，在该任务中，BC需要比PIDM多达五倍（平均三倍）的演示数据才能达到相当的性能；并且在一个具有高维视觉输入和随机转移的现代电子游戏中的复杂三维环境中，BC比PIDM需要多66%以上的样本。

TLDR: The paper theoretically and empirically analyzes why Predictive Inverse Dynamics Models (PIDM) can outperform Behavior Cloning (BC) in imitation learning, attributing the benefit to a bias-variance tradeoff introduced by the future state predictor.

简要说明: 本文从理论和实验上分析了预测逆动力学模型 (PIDM) 在模仿学习中优于行为克隆 (BC) 的原因，认为其优势在于未来状态预测器引入的偏差-方差权衡。

Authors: Lukas Schäfer, Pallavi Choudhury, Abdelhak Lemkhenter, Chris Lovett, Somjit Nath, Luis França, Matheus Ribeiro Furtado de Mendonça, Alex Lamb, Riashat Islam, Siddhartha Sen, John Langford, Katja Hofmann, Sergio Valcarcel Macua

Expected Return Causes Outcome-Level Mode Collapse in Reinforcement Learning and How to Fix It with Inverse Probability Scaling

Abstract (English): Many reinforcement learning (RL) problems admit multiple terminal solutions of comparable quality, where the goal is not to identify a single optimum but to represent a diverse set of high-quality outcomes. Nevertheless, policies trained by standard expected return maximization routinely collapse onto a small subset of outcomes, a phenomenon commonly attributed to insufficient exploration or weak regularization. We show that this explanation is incomplete: outcome level mode collapse is a structural consequence of the expected-return objective itself. Under idealized learning dynamics, the log-probability ratio between any two outcomes evolves linearly in their reward difference, implying exponential ratio divergence and inevitable collapse independent of the exploration strategy, entropy regularization, or optimization algorithm. We identify the source of this pathology as the probability multiplier inside the expectation and propose a minimal correction: inverse probability scaling, which removes outcome-frequency amplification from the learning signal, fundamentally changes the learning dynamics, and provably yields reward-proportional terminal distributions, preventing collapse in multimodal settings. We instantiate this principle in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) as a drop-in modification, IPS-GRPO, requiring no auxiliary models or architectural changes. Across different reasoning and molecular generation tasks, IPS-GRPO consistently reduces outcome-level mode collapse while matching or exceeding baseline performance, suggesting that correcting the objective rather than adding exploration heuristics is key to reliable multimodal policy optimization.

摘要（中文）: 许多强化学习（RL）问题允许存在多个质量相当的终端解，其目标不是识别单一最优解，而是表示一组多样化的优质结果。然而，通过标准期望回报最大化训练的策略通常会坍缩到一小部分结果上，这种现象通常归因于探索不足或正则化弱。我们表明这种解释是不完整的：结果层面的模式坍缩是期望回报目标本身的结构性结果。在理想化的学习动态下，任意两个结果之间的对数概率比与其奖励差异呈线性关系，这意味着指数级的比率差异，以及不可避免的坍缩，这与探索策略、熵正则化或优化算法无关。我们确定了这种病态现象的根源在于期望内部的概率乘数，并提出了一个最小的修正：逆概率缩放，它可以消除学习信号中的结果频率放大，从根本上改变学习动态，并可证明地产生奖励成比例的终端分布，从而防止多模态设置中的坍缩。我们将此原则实例化为组相对策略优化（GRPO）中的一个即插即用修改版，IPS-GRPO，无需辅助模型或架构更改。在不同的推理和分子生成任务中，IPS-GRPO始终可以减少结果层面的模式坍缩，同时达到或超过基线的性能，这表明纠正目标而不是添加探索启发式方法是可靠的多模态策略优化的关键。

TLDR: This paper identifies outcome-level mode collapse in RL as a structural consequence of the expected-return objective and proposes inverse probability scaling (IPS) to address it, demonstrating improved performance across reasoning and molecular generation tasks.

简要说明: 本文指出，强化学习中结果层面的模式崩溃是预期回报目标的结构性结果，并提出了逆概率缩放（IPS）来解决这个问题，并在推理和分子生成任务中展示了改进的性能。

Authors: Abhijeet Sinha, Sundari Elango, Dianbo Liu

Signal-Adaptive Trust Regions for Gradient-Free Optimization of Recurrent Spiking Neural Networks

Abstract (English): Recurrent spiking neural networks (RSNNs) are a promising substrate for energy-efficient control policies, but training them for high-dimensional, long-horizon reinforcement learning remains challenging. Population-based, gradient-free optimization circumvents backpropagation through non-differentiable spike dynamics by estimating gradients. However, with finite populations, high variance of these estimates can induce harmful and overly aggressive update steps. Inspired by trust-region methods in reinforcement learning that constrain policy updates in distribution space, we propose \textbf{Signal-Adaptive Trust Regions (SATR)}, a distributional update rule that constrains relative change by bounding KL divergence normalized by an estimated signal energy. SATR automatically expands the trust region under strong signals and contracts it when updates are noise-dominated. We instantiate SATR for Bernoulli connectivity distributions, which have shown strong empirical performance for RSNN optimization. Across a suite of high-dimensional continuous-control benchmarks, SATR improves stability under limited populations and reaches competitive returns against strong baselines including PPO-LSTM. In addition, to make SATR practical at scale, we introduce a bitset implementation for binary spiking and binary weights, substantially reducing wall-clock training time and enabling fast RSNN policy search.

摘要（中文）: 循环脉冲神经网络（RSNNs）是节能型控制策略的一种有前景的基础，但对其进行高维、长时程强化学习的训练仍然具有挑战性。基于群体的无梯度优化通过估计梯度绕过了通过不可微分脉冲动力学的反向传播。然而，在有限的群体规模下，这些估计值的高方差可能会导致有害和过于激进的更新步骤。受强化学习中信任域方法的启发，该方法限制了分布空间中的策略更新，我们提出了\textbf{信号自适应信任域（SATR）}，这是一种分布更新规则，通过约束由估计的信号能量归一化的KL散度来限制相对变化。SATR在强信号下自动扩展信任域，并在更新由噪声主导时缩小信任域。我们针对伯努利连接分布实例化了SATR，该分布已显示出RSNN优化方面的强大经验性能。在一套高维连续控制基准测试中，SATR在有限群体规模下提高了稳定性，并达到了与包括PPO-LSTM在内的强大基准线相比具有竞争力的回报。此外，为了使SATR能够大规模应用，我们引入了一种用于二进制脉冲和二进制权重的位集实现，从而大大减少了实际训练时间，并实现了快速的RSNN策略搜索。

TLDR: The paper proposes Signal-Adaptive Trust Regions (SATR), a gradient-free optimization method for recurrent spiking neural networks (RSNNs), improving stability and performance in high-dimensional, long-horizon reinforcement learning tasks, especially with limited data and binary implementations for scalability.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种信号自适应信任域（SATR）方法，用于循环脉冲神经网络（RSNNs）的无梯度优化，提高了在高维、长时程强化学习任务中的稳定性和性能，特别是当数据有限以及使用二进制实施以提高可扩展性时。

Authors: Jinhao Li, Yuhao Sun, Zhiyuan Ma, Hao He, Xinche Zhang, Xing Chen, Jin Li, Sen Song

KAPSO: A Knowledge-grounded framework for Autonomous Program Synthesis and Optimization

Abstract (English): We introduce KAPSO, a modular framework for autonomous program synthesis and optimization. Given a natural language goal and an evaluation method, KAPSO iteratively performs ideation, code synthesis and editing, execution, evaluation, and learning to improve a runnable artifact toward measurable objectives. Rather than treating synthesis as the endpoint, KAPSO uses synthesis as an operator within a long-horizon optimization loop, where progress is defined by evaluator outcomes. KAPSO targets long-horizon failures common in coding agents, including lost experimental state, brittle debugging, and weak reuse of domain expertise, by integrating three tightly coupled components. First, a git-native experimentation engine isolates each attempt as a branch, producing reproducible artifacts and preserving provenance across iterations. Second, a knowledge system ingests heterogeneous sources, including repositories, internal playbooks, and curated external resources such as documentation, scientific papers, and web search results, and organizes them into a structured representation that supports retrieval over workflows, implementations, and environment constraints. Third, a cognitive memory layer coordinates retrieval and maintains an episodic store of reusable lessons distilled from experiment traces (run logs, diffs, and evaluator feedback), reducing repeated error modes and accelerating convergence. We evaluated KAPSO on MLE-Bench (Kaggle-style ML competitions) and ALE-Bench (AtCoder heuristic optimization), and report end-to-end performance. Code Available at: https://github.com/Leeroo-AI/kapso

摘要（中文）: 我们介绍KAPSO，一个用于自主程序合成和优化的模块化框架。给定一个自然语言目标和一个评估方法，KAPSO迭代地执行构思、代码合成和编辑、执行、评估和学习，以改进一个可运行的工件，使其朝着可衡量的目标前进。KAPSO不是将合成作为终点，而是将合成用作长时程优化循环中的一个算子，其中进展由评估器的结果定义。 KAPSO着眼于编码代理中常见的长时程失败问题，包括实验状态丢失、脆弱的调试以及领域专业知识的弱复用，通过集成三个紧密耦合的组件来解决这些问题。首先，一个原生于git的实验引擎将每次尝试隔离为一个分支，产生可复现的工件，并在迭代中保留出处。其次，一个知识系统摄取异构来源的信息，包括仓库、内部手册和精心策划的外部资源（如文档、科学论文和网络搜索结果），并将它们组织成一个结构化的表示，以支持对工作流、实现和环境约束的检索。第三，一个认知记忆层协调检索，并维护一个情景存储，其中包含从实验跟踪（运行日志、差异和评估器反馈）中提取的可重用经验，从而减少重复的错误模式并加速收敛。 我们使用MLE-Bench（Kaggle风格的机器学习竞赛）和ALE-Bench（AtCoder启发式优化）对KAPSO进行了评估，并报告了端到端的性能。 代码可在以下位置获取：https://github.com/Leeroo-AI/kapso

TLDR: KAPSO is a new framework for autonomous program synthesis and optimization that leverages knowledge grounding, git-native experimentation, and cognitive memory to improve performance in long-horizon tasks, demonstrated on ML and heuristic optimization benchmarks.

简要说明: KAPSO是一个用于自主程序合成和优化的新框架，它利用知识基础、原生Git实验和认知记忆来提高长周期任务的性能，并在ML和启发式优化基准测试中得到了验证。

Authors: Alireza Nadaf, Alireza Mohammadshahi, Majid Yazdani

Mean-Field Control on Sparse Graphs: From Local Limits to GNNs via Neighborhood Distributions

Abstract (English): Mean-field control (MFC) offers a scalable solution to the curse of dimensionality in multi-agent systems but traditionally hinges on the restrictive assumption of exchangeability via dense, all-to-all interactions. In this work, we bridge the gap to real-world network structures by proposing a rigorous framework for MFC on large sparse graphs. We redefine the system state as a probability measure over decorated rooted neighborhoods, effectively capturing local heterogeneity. Our central contribution is a theoretical foundation for scalable reinforcement learning in this setting. We prove horizon-dependent locality: for finite-horizon problems, an agent's optimal policy at time t depends strictly on its (T-t)-hop neighborhood. This result renders the infinite-dimensional control problem tractable and underpins a novel Dynamic Programming Principle (DPP) on the lifted space of neighborhood distributions. Furthermore, we formally and experimentally justify the use of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) for actor-critic algorithms in this context. Our framework naturally recovers classical MFC as a degenerate case while enabling efficient, theoretically grounded control on complex sparse topologies.

摘要（中文）: 均场控制（MFC）为多智能体系统中维度灾难提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，但传统上依赖于密集、全连接交互所带来的可交换性的严格假设。本研究通过提出一个适用于大型稀疏图的MFC严格框架，弥合了与真实网络结构的差距。我们将系统状态重新定义为带装饰的根邻域上的概率测度，从而有效地捕捉了局部异质性。我们的核心贡献是为这种场景中的可扩展强化学习提供了一个理论基础。我们证明了依赖于时域范围的局部性：对于有限时域问题，智能体在时间t的最优策略严格依赖于其(T-t)跳邻域。这一结果使得无限维控制问题变得易于处理，并支撑着一个在邻域分布提升空间上的新型动态规划原则（DPP）。此外，我们从理论和实验上证明了在此背景下使用图神经网络（GNNs）进行actor-critic算法的合理性。我们的框架自然地将经典MFC作为一种退化情况恢复，同时实现了在复杂稀疏拓扑上高效且具有理论基础的控制。

TLDR: This paper provides a theoretical framework for scalable mean-field control on sparse graphs using neighborhood distributions and justifies the use of GNNs for actor-critic algorithms, bridging the gap between classical MFC and real-world network structures.

简要说明: 该论文为稀疏图上的可扩展平均场控制提供了一个理论框架，利用邻域分布并证明了使用 GNN 进行 actor-critic 算法的合理性，从而弥合了经典 MFC 与现实世界网络结构之间的差距。

Authors: Tobias Schmidt, Kai Cui

ScaleSim: Serving Large-Scale Multi-Agent Simulation with Invocation Distance-Based Memory Management

Abstract (English): LLM-based multi-agent simulations are increasingly adopted across application domains, but remain difficult to scale due to GPU memory pressure. Each agent maintains private GPU-resident states, including models, prefix caches, and adapters, which quickly exhaust device memory as the agent count grows. We identify two key properties of these workloads: sparse agent activation and an estimable agent invocation order. Based on an analysis of representative workload classes, we introduce invocation distance, a unified abstraction that estimates the relative order in which agents will issue future LLM requests. Leveraging this abstraction, we present ScaleSim, a memory-efficient LLM serving system for large-scale multi-agent simulations. ScaleSim enables proactive prefetching and priority-based eviction, supports diverse agent-specific memory through a modular interface, and achieves up to 1.74x speedup over SGLang on simulation benchmarks.

摘要（中文）: 基于LLM的多智能体仿真在各个应用领域中日益普及，但由于GPU内存压力，其规模化仍然面临挑战。每个智能体维护私有的驻留GPU状态，包括模型、前缀缓存和适配器，这会随着智能体数量的增长迅速耗尽设备内存。我们识别出这些工作负载的两个关键属性：稀疏智能体激活和可估计的智能体调用顺序。基于对代表性工作负载类型的分析，我们引入了调用距离，这是一个统一的抽象概念，用于估计智能体发出未来LLM请求的相对顺序。利用此抽象概念，我们提出了ScaleSim，一种用于大规模多智能体仿真的内存高效LLM服务系统。ScaleSim支持主动预取和基于优先级的驱逐，通过模块化接口支持多样化的智能体特定内存，并在仿真基准测试中实现了高达1.74倍于SGLang的加速。

TLDR: ScaleSim is a memory-efficient LLM serving system for large-scale multi-agent simulations, using invocation distance to optimize memory management via prefetching and eviction, demonstrating speedups over SGLang.

简要说明: ScaleSim是一个用于大规模多智能体模拟的内存高效LLM服务系统，它使用调用距离通过预取和驱逐来优化内存管理，并展示了相对于SGLang的加速。

Authors: Zaifeng Pan, Yipeng Shen, Zhengding Hu, Zhuang Wang, Aninda Manocha, Zheng Wang, Zhongkai Yu, Yue Guan, Yufei Ding

Heterogeneous Vertiport Selection Optimization for On-Demand Air Taxi Services: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach

Abstract (English): Urban Air Mobility (UAM) has emerged as a transformative solution to alleviate urban congestion by utilizing low-altitude airspace, thereby reducing pressure on ground transportation networks. To enable truly efficient and seamless door-to-door travel experiences, UAM requires close integration with existing ground transportation infrastructure. However, current research on optimal integrated routing strategies for passengers in air-ground mobility systems remains limited, with a lack of systematic exploration.To address this gap, we first propose a unified optimization model that integrates strategy selection for both air and ground transportation. This model captures the dynamic characteristics of multimodal transport networks and incorporates real-time traffic conditions alongside passenger decision-making behavior. Building on this model, we propose a Unified Air-Ground Mobility Coordination (UAGMC) framework, which leverages deep reinforcement learning (RL) and Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) communication to optimize vertiport selection and dynamically plan air taxi routes. Experimental results demonstrate that UAGMC achieves a 34\% reduction in average travel time compared to conventional proportional allocation methods, enhancing overall travel efficiency and providing novel insights into the integration and optimization of multimodal transportation systems. This work lays a solid foundation for advancing intelligent urban mobility solutions through the coordination of air and ground transportation modes. The related code can be found at https://github.com/Traffic-Alpha/UAGMC.

摘要（中文）: 城市空中交通（UAM）已经成为一种变革性的解决方案，它利用低空空域来缓解城市拥堵，从而减轻地面交通网络的压力。为了实现真正高效且无缝的门到门出行体验，UAM 需要与现有地面交通基础设施紧密集成。然而，目前关于空地一体化出行系统中乘客最优集成路径策略的研究仍然有限，缺乏系统性的探索。为了解决这一差距，我们首先提出了一个统一的优化模型，该模型集成了空中和地面交通的策略选择。该模型捕捉了多式联运网络的动态特性，并将实时交通状况与乘客决策行为相结合。在此模型的基础上，我们提出了一个统一的空地出行协调（UAGMC）框架，该框架利用深度强化学习（RL）和车联网（V2X）通信来优化垂直起降机场的选择，并动态规划空中出租车路线。实验结果表明，与传统的比例分配方法相比，UAGMC 可以将平均出行时间减少 34％，从而提高整体出行效率，并为空地多式联运系统的集成和优化提供新的见解。这项工作为通过协调空中和地面交通模式来推进智能城市出行解决方案奠定了坚实的基础。相关代码可在 https://github.com/Traffic-Alpha/UAGMC 找到。

TLDR: This paper presents a deep reinforcement learning framework (UAGMC) for optimizing vertiport selection and air taxi routing in a multimodal urban mobility system, achieving a 34% reduction in travel time compared to traditional methods.

Authors: Aoyu Pang, Maonan Wang, Zifan Sha, Wenwei Yue, Changle Li, Chung Shue Chen, Man-On Pun

Safety Generalization Under Distribution Shift in Safe Reinforcement Learning: A Diabetes Testbed

Abstract (English): Safe Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms are typically evaluated under fixed training conditions. We investigate whether training-time safety guarantees transfer to deployment under distribution shift, using diabetes management as a safety-critical testbed. We benchmark safe RL algorithms on a unified clinical simulator and reveal a safety generalization gap: policies satisfying constraints during training frequently violate safety requirements on unseen patients. We demonstrate that test-time shielding, which filters unsafe actions using learned dynamics models, effectively restores safety across algorithms and patient populations. Across eight safe RL algorithms, three diabetes types, and three age groups, shielding achieves Time-in-Range gains of 13--14\% for strong baselines such as PPO-Lag and CPO while reducing clinical risk index and glucose variability. Our simulator and benchmark provide a platform for studying safety under distribution shift in safety-critical control domains. Code is available at https://github.com/safe-autonomy-lab/GlucoSim and https://github.com/safe-autonomy-lab/GlucoAlg.

摘要（中文）: 安全强化学习（RL）算法通常在固定的训练条件下进行评估。我们研究了训练时的安全保证是否能迁移到分布偏移下的部署，并以糖尿病管理作为安全关键的测试平台。我们在统一的临床模拟器上对安全RL算法进行了基准测试，揭示了一个安全泛化差距：在训练期间满足约束的策略，在未见过的患者身上经常违反安全要求。我们证明了测试时屏蔽，即使用学习到的动力学模型来过滤不安全的行为，可以有效地恢复算法和患者群体的安全性。在八种安全RL算法、三种糖尿病类型和三个年龄组中，屏蔽技术为诸如PPO-Lag和CPO等强大的基线带来了13-14\%的血糖达标时间（Time-in-Range）增益，同时降低了临床风险指数和血糖变异性。我们的模拟器和基准测试提供了一个平台，用于研究安全关键控制领域中分布偏移下的安全性。代码可在https://github.com/safe-autonomy-lab/GlucoSim和https://github.com/safe-autonomy-lab/GlucoAlg上获取。

TLDR: This paper investigates the safety generalization gap in safe RL algorithms for diabetes management under distribution shift and proposes test-time shielding using learned dynamics models to improve safety and effectiveness.

Authors: Minjae Kwon, Josephine Lamp, Lu Feng

Solver-in-the-Loop: MDP-Based Benchmarks for Self-Correction and Behavioral Rationality in Operations Research

Abstract (English): Operations Research practitioners routinely debug infeasible models through an iterative process: analyzing Irreducible Infeasible Subsystems (\IIS{}), identifying constraint conflicts, and systematically repairing formulations until feasibility is achieved. Yet existing LLM benchmarks evaluate OR as one-shot translation -- given a problem description, generate solver code -- ignoring this diagnostic loop entirely. We introduce two benchmarks that place the \textbf{solver in the evaluation loop}. \textbf{\ORDebug{}} evaluates iterative self-correction through 5,000+ problems spanning 9 error types; each repair action triggers solver re-execution and \IIS{} recomputation, providing deterministic, verifiable feedback. \textbf{\ORBias{}} evaluates behavioral rationality through 2,000 newsvendor instances (1,000 ID + 1,000 OOD), measuring systematic deviations from closed-form optimal policies. Across 26 models and 12,000+ samples, we find that domain-specific RLVR training enables an 8B model to surpass frontier APIs: 95.3\% vs 86.2\% recovery rate (+9.1\%), 62.4\% vs 47.8\% diagnostic accuracy (+14.6\%), and 2.25 vs 3.78 steps to resolution (1.7$\times$ faster). On \ORBias{}, curriculum training achieves the only negative ID$\rightarrow$OOD bias drift among models evaluated (-9.6\%), reducing systematic bias by 48\% (from 20.0\% to 10.4\%). These results demonstrate that process-level evaluation with verifiable oracles enables targeted training that outperforms scale.

摘要（中文）: 运筹学从业者通常通过迭代过程调试不可行模型：分析不可约不可行子系统（IIS），识别约束冲突，并系统地修复公式直到实现可行性。 然而，现有的 LLM 基准测试将运筹学评估为一次性翻译——给定问题描述，生成求解器代码——完全忽略了这个诊断循环。 我们引入了两个将\textbf{求解器置于评估循环中}的基准测试。\textbf{\ORDebug{}} 通过涵盖 9 种错误类型的 5000 多个问题来评估迭代自纠正； 每个修复动作都会触发求解器的重新执行和 IIS 的重新计算，从而提供确定性、可验证的反馈。 \textbf{\ORBias{}} 通过 2,000 个报童问题实例（1,000 个 ID + 1,000 个 OOD）评估行为理性，衡量与闭式最优策略的系统性偏差。 在 26 个模型和 12,000 多个样本中，我们发现特定领域的 RLVR 训练使 8B 模型能够超越前沿 API：95.3% 对比 86.2% 的恢复率（+9.1%），62.4% 对比 47.8% 的诊断准确率（+14.6%），以及 2.25 对比 3.78 的解决步骤（快 1.7 倍）。 在 \ORBias{} 上，课程训练实现了评估模型中唯一的负 ID$\rightarrow$OOD 偏差漂移 (-9.6%)，将系统性偏差降低了 48% (从 20.0% 到 10.4%)。 这些结果表明，具有可验证 Oracle 的过程级评估能够进行有针对性的训练，从而优于规模提升。

TLDR: This paper introduces two new benchmarks, ORDebug and ORBias, for evaluating LLMs in operations research, focusing on iterative self-correction and behavioral rationality. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of domain-specific training and curriculum learning in improving model performance and reducing bias.

简要说明: 本文介绍了两个新的基准测试，ORDebug 和 ORBias，用于评估 LLM 在运筹学中的应用，重点关注迭代自我纠正和行为理性。结果表明，领域特定的训练和课程学习在提高模型性能和减少偏差方面非常有效。

Authors: Ruicheng Ao, David Simchi-Levi, Xinshang Wang

Beyond Global Alignment: Fine-Grained Motion-Language Retrieval via Pyramidal Shapley-Taylor Learning

Abstract (English): As a foundational task in human-centric cross-modal intelligence, motion-language retrieval aims to bridge the semantic gap between natural language and human motion, enabling intuitive motion analysis, yet existing approaches predominantly focus on aligning entire motion sequences with global textual representations. This global-centric paradigm overlooks fine-grained interactions between local motion segments and individual body joints and text tokens, inevitably leading to suboptimal retrieval performance. To address this limitation, we draw inspiration from the pyramidal process of human motion perception (from joint dynamics to segment coherence, and finally to holistic comprehension) and propose a novel Pyramidal Shapley-Taylor (PST) learning framework for fine-grained motion-language retrieval. Specifically, the framework decomposes human motion into temporal segments and spatial body joints, and learns cross-modal correspondences through progressive joint-wise and segment-wise alignment in a pyramidal fashion, effectively capturing both local semantic details and hierarchical structural relationships. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmark datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving precise alignment between motion segments and body joints and their corresponding text tokens. The code of this work will be released upon acceptance.

摘要（中文）: 作为以人为中心的跨模态智能中的一项基础任务，动作-语言检索旨在弥合自然语言和人体动作之间的语义鸿沟，从而实现直观的动作分析。然而，现有方法主要集中于将整个运动序列与全局文本表示对齐。这种以全局为中心的范式忽略了局部运动片段、个体身体关节与文本标记之间的细粒度交互，不可避免地导致次优的检索性能。为了解决这一局限性，我们从人类运动感知的金字塔式过程（从关节动态到片段连贯性，最终到整体理解）中汲取灵感，提出了一种用于细粒度动作-语言检索的新型金字塔 Shapley-Taylor (PST) 学习框架。具体而言，该框架将人体运动分解为时间片段和空间身体关节，并通过金字塔式的渐进式关节级和片段级对齐来学习跨模态对应关系，有效地捕捉局部语义细节和分层结构关系。在多个公共基准数据集上进行的大量实验表明，我们的方法显著优于最先进的方法，实现了运动片段、身体关节以及它们对应的文本标记之间的精确对齐。该工作的代码将在接收后发布。

TLDR: This paper introduces a Pyramidal Shapley-Taylor (PST) learning framework for fine-grained motion-language retrieval that aligns motion segments and body joints with corresponding text tokens, outperforming state-of-the-art methods on benchmark datasets.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种用于细粒度运动-语言检索的金字塔形 Shapley-Taylor (PST) 学习框架，该框架可以将运动片段和身体关节与相应的文本标记对齐，并在基准数据集上优于最先进的方法。

Authors: Hanmo Chen, Guangtao Lyu, Chenghao Xu, Jiexi Yan, Xu Yang, Cheng Deng

RSGround-R1: Rethinking Remote Sensing Visual Grounding through Spatial Reasoning

Abstract (English): Remote Sensing Visual Grounding (RSVG) aims to localize target objects in large-scale aerial imagery based on natural language descriptions. Owing to the vast spatial scale and high semantic ambiguity of remote sensing scenes, these descriptions often rely heavily on positional cues, posing unique challenges for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in spatial reasoning. To leverage this unique feature, we propose a reasoning-guided, position-aware post-training framework, dubbed \textbf{RSGround-R1}, to progressively enhance spatial understanding. Specifically, we first introduce Chain-of-Thought Supervised Fine-Tuning (CoT-SFT) using synthetically generated RSVG reasoning data to establish explicit position awareness. Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) is then applied, augmented by our newly designed positional reward that provides continuous and distance-aware guidance toward accurate localization. Moreover, to mitigate incoherent localization behaviors across rollouts, we introduce a spatial consistency guided optimization scheme that dynamically adjusts policy updates based on their spatial coherence, ensuring stable and robust convergence. Extensive experiments on RSVG benchmarks demonstrate superior performance and generalization of our model.

摘要（中文）: 遥感视觉定位（RSVG）旨在基于自然语言描述在大规模航空影像中定位目标物体。由于遥感场景的空间尺度巨大且语义歧义性较高，这些描述通常严重依赖于位置线索，这对多模态大型语言模型（MLLMs）的空间推理提出了独特的挑战。为了利用这一独特特征，我们提出了一种推理引导的、位置感知的后训练框架，命名为\textbf{RSGround-R1}，以逐步增强空间理解能力。具体来说，我们首先引入了思维链监督微调（CoT-SFT），利用合成生成的RSVG推理数据来建立显式的位置感知。然后，应用强化微调（RFT），并辅以我们新设计的位置奖励，该奖励为准确的定位提供连续且距离感知的指导。此外，为了缓解跨越多次推演的不连贯定位行为，我们引入了一种空间一致性引导的优化方案，该方案根据策略更新的空间一致性动态调整策略更新，确保稳定且鲁棒的收敛。在RSVG基准上的大量实验表明，我们的模型具有卓越的性能和泛化能力。

TLDR: The paper introduces RSGround-R1, a framework to improve spatial reasoning in Remote Sensing Visual Grounding (RSVG) using Chain-of-Thought Supervised Fine-Tuning and Reinforcement Fine-Tuning with positional and spatial consistency guidance.

简要说明: 本文提出了RSGround-R1，一个通过链式思考监督微调和强化微调来提高遥感视觉定位（RSVG）中空间推理能力的框架，该框架使用位置和空间一致性指导。

Authors: Shiqi Huang, Shuting He, Bihan Wen

Optimistic Transfer under Task Shift via Bellman Alignment

Abstract (English): We study online transfer reinforcement learning (RL) in episodic Markov decision processes, where experience from related source tasks is available during learning on a target task. A fundamental difficulty is that task similarity is typically defined in terms of rewards or transitions, whereas online RL algorithms operate on Bellman regression targets. As a result, naively reusing source Bellman updates introduces systematic bias and invalidates regret guarantees. We identify one-step Bellman alignment as the correct abstraction for transfer in online RL and propose re-weighted targeting (RWT), an operator-level correction that retargets continuation values and compensates for transition mismatch via a change of measure. RWT reduces task mismatch to a fixed one-step correction and enables statistically sound reuse of source data. This alignment yields a two-stage RWT $Q$-learning framework that separates variance reduction from bias correction. Under RKHS function approximation, we establish regret bounds that scale with the complexity of the task shift rather than the target MDP. Empirical results in both tabular and neural network settings demonstrate consistent improvements over single-task learning and naïve pooling, highlighting Bellman alignment as a model-agnostic transfer principle for online RL.

摘要（中文）: 我们研究情景马尔可夫决策过程中在线迁移强化学习（RL），在该过程中，可以在目标任务的学习过程中利用来自相关源任务的经验。一个根本性的困难是，任务相似性通常是根据奖励或转移定义的，而在线 RL 算法则基于贝尔曼回归目标运行。因此，简单地重用源贝尔曼更新会引入系统偏差并使后悔保证失效。 我们确定一步贝尔曼对齐是在线 RL 中迁移的正确抽象，并提出重加权目标（RWT），一种算子层面的校正方法，它通过度量改变来重新调整持续值并补偿转移不匹配。RWT 将任务不匹配简化为固定的单步校正，并能以统计学上合理的方式重用源数据。 这种对齐产生了一个两阶段 RWT $Q$ 学习框架，该框架将方差缩减与偏差校正分开。在 RKHS 函数逼近下，我们建立了与任务偏移的复杂度（而非目标 MDP）成比例的后悔界限。 在表格和神经网络设置中的经验结果表明，相对于单任务学习和朴素池化，一致的改进，突出了贝尔曼对齐作为在线 RL 的模型无关的迁移原则。

TLDR: This paper introduces a novel transfer reinforcement learning method called re-weighted targeting (RWT) that aligns Bellman updates across different tasks by correcting for transition mismatches, leading to improved regret bounds and empirical performance.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种新的迁移强化学习方法，称为重加权目标 (RWT)，它通过校正转移不匹配来对齐不同任务之间的贝尔曼更新，从而改善遗憾边界和经验性能。

Authors: Jinhang Chai, Enpei Zhang, Elynn Chen, Yujun Yan

READY: Reward Discovery for Meta-Black-Box Optimization

Abstract (English): Meta-Black-Box Optimization (MetaBBO) is an emerging avenue within Optimization community, where algorithm design policy could be meta-learned by reinforcement learning to enhance optimization performance. So far, the reward functions in existing MetaBBO works are designed by human experts, introducing certain design bias and risks of reward hacking. In this paper, we use Large Language Model~(LLM) as an automated reward discovery tool for MetaBBO. Specifically, we consider both effectiveness and efficiency sides. On effectiveness side, we borrow the idea of evolution of heuristics, introducing tailored evolution paradigm in the iterative LLM-based program search process, which ensures continuous improvement. On efficiency side, we additionally introduce multi-task evolution architecture to support parallel reward discovery for diverse MetaBBO approaches. Such parallel process also benefits from knowledge sharing across tasks to accelerate convergence. Empirical results demonstrate that the reward functions discovered by our approach could be helpful for boosting existing MetaBBO works, underscoring the importance of reward design in MetaBBO. We provide READY's project at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ICML_READY-747F.

摘要（中文）: 元黑盒优化 (MetaBBO) 是优化领域中一个新兴方向，它通过强化学习元学习算法设计策略，以提升优化性能。目前，现有MetaBBO工作中的奖励函数均由人类专家设计，这引入了一定的设计偏见和奖励入侵风险。在本文中，我们使用大型语言模型 (LLM) 作为 MetaBBO 的自动化奖励发现工具。具体而言，我们同时考虑了有效性和效率两方面。在有效性方面，我们借鉴了启发式算法进化的思想，在基于LLM的迭代程序搜索过程中引入定制的进化范式，从而确保持续改进。在效率方面，我们进一步引入多任务进化架构，以支持对不同 MetaBBO 方法进行并行奖励发现。这种并行过程也受益于跨任务的知识共享，从而加速收敛。实验结果表明，通过我们的方法发现的奖励函数有助于提升现有 MetaBBO 工作，突出了奖励设计在 MetaBBO 中的重要性。我们在此提供 READY 项目，网址为 https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ICML_READY-747F。

TLDR: The paper proposes READY, an approach using large language models to automatically discover reward functions for Meta-Black-Box Optimization (MetaBBO), improving upon existing methods that rely on human-designed rewards by introducing evolution paradigm and multi-task evolution architecture.

简要说明: 该论文提出了 READY，一种使用大型语言模型自动发现元黑盒优化 (MetaBBO) 奖励函数的方法，通过引入进化范式和多任务进化架构，改进了现有依赖人工设计的奖励的方法。

Authors: Zechuan Huang, Zhiguang Cao, Hongshu Guo, Yue-Jiao Gong, Zeyuan Ma

Error Amplification Limits ANN-to-SNN Conversion in Continuous Control

Abstract (English): Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) can achieve competitive performance by converting already existing well-trained Artificial Neural Networks (ANNs), avoiding further costly training. This property is particularly attractive in Reinforcement Learning (RL), where training through environment interaction is expensive and potentially unsafe. However, existing conversion methods perform poorly in continuous control, where suitable baselines are largely absent. We identify error amplification as the key cause: small action approximation errors become temporally correlated across decision steps, inducing cumulative state distribution shift and severe performance degradation. To address this issue, we propose Cross-Step Residual Potential Initialization (CRPI), a lightweight training-free mechanism that carries over residual membrane potentials across decision steps to suppress temporally correlated errors. Experiments on continuous control benchmarks with both vector and visual observations demonstrate that CRPI can be integrated into existing conversion pipelines and substantially recovers lost performance. Our results highlight continuous control as a critical and challenging benchmark for ANN-to-SNN conversion, where small errors can be strongly amplified and impact performance.

摘要（中文）: 脉冲神经网络 (SNNs) 能够通过转换已有的训练良好的[人工神经网络] (ANNs) 来实现具有竞争力的性能，避免进一步昂贵的训练。这种特性在[强化学习] (RL) 中尤其具有吸引力，因为通过环境交互进行训练既昂贵又具有潜在的危险。然而，现有的转换方法在[连续控制] 领域表现不佳，而该领域中合适的基线在很大程度上是缺失的。我们发现[误差放大] 是主要原因：微小的动作近似误差会在决策步骤中进行时间相关性的累积，从而导致累积的状态分布偏移和严重的性能下降。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种轻量级的、免训练的机制——[跨步残差势初始化] (CRPI)，它将残余膜电位置于决策步骤中，以抑制时间相关的误差。在具有向量和视觉观测的[连续控制] 基准上的实验表明，CRPI 可以集成到现有的转换流程中，并大幅恢复损失的性能。我们的结果突出表明，[连续控制] 是 ANN 到 SNN 转换的一个关键且具有挑战性的基准，其中微小的误差可能会被强烈放大并影响性能。

TLDR: This paper identifies error amplification in ANN-to-SNN conversion for continuous control as a key challenge and proposes a Cross-Step Residual Potential Initialization (CRPI) method to mitigate this issue, demonstrating performance recovery on continuous control benchmarks.

简要说明: 该论文指出，在连续控制中，ANN到SNN转换中的误差放大是一个关键挑战，并提出了一种跨步残差势初始化(CRPI)方法来缓解这个问题，并在连续控制基准测试中证明了性能恢复。

Authors: Zijie Xu, Zihan Huang, Yiting Dong, Kang Chen, Wenxuan Liu, Zhaofei Yu

LLM4Fluid: Large Language Models as Generalizable Neural Solvers for Fluid Dynamics

Abstract (English): Deep learning has emerged as a promising paradigm for spatio-temporal modeling of fluid dynamics. However, existing approaches often suffer from limited generalization to unseen flow conditions and typically require retraining when applied to new scenarios. In this paper, we present LLM4Fluid, a spatio-temporal prediction framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) as generalizable neural solvers for fluid dynamics. The framework first compresses high-dimensional flow fields into a compact latent space via reduced-order modeling enhanced with a physics-informed disentanglement mechanism, effectively mitigating spatial feature entanglement while preserving essential flow structures. A pretrained LLM then serves as a temporal processor, autoregressively predicting the dynamics of physical sequences with time series prompts. To bridge the modality gap between prompts and physical sequences, which can otherwise degrade prediction accuracy, we propose a dedicated modality alignment strategy that resolves representational mismatch and stabilizes long-term prediction. Extensive experiments across diverse flow scenarios demonstrate that LLM4Fluid functions as a robust and generalizable neural solver without retraining, achieving state-of-the-art accuracy while exhibiting powerful zero-shot and in-context learning capabilities. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/qisongxiao/LLM4Fluid.

摘要（中文）: 深度学习已成为流体动力学时空建模的一种有前景的范例。然而，现有方法通常存在针对未见流动条件的泛化能力有限的问题，并且应用于新场景时通常需要重新训练。在本文中，我们提出了 LLM4Fluid，一种时空预测框架，利用大型语言模型 (LLM) 作为可泛化的流体动力学神经求解器。该框架首先通过降阶建模（增强了物理信息解耦机制）将高维流场压缩成紧凑的潜在空间，有效地缓解了空间特征纠缠，同时保留了必要的流动结构。然后，预训练的 LLM 充当时间处理器，通过时间序列提示自回归地预测物理序列的动力学。为了弥合提示和物理序列之间的模态差距（否则会降低预测精度），我们提出了一种专门的模态对齐策略，该策略解决了表征不匹配问题并稳定了长期预测。在各种流动场景下进行的大量实验表明，LLM4Fluid 可作为一个稳健且可泛化的神经求解器，无需重新训练，实现了最先进的精度，同时展现出强大的零样本和上下文学习能力。代码和数据集可在 https://github.com/qisongxiao/LLM4Fluid 上公开获取。

TLDR: LLM4Fluid leverages pre-trained LLMs coupled with reduced-order modeling and modality alignment to achieve generalizable and accurate spatio-temporal prediction of fluid dynamics without retraining across diverse flow scenarios.

简要说明: LLM4Fluid利用预训练的大型语言模型，结合降阶建模和模态对齐，实现了流体动力学时空预测的泛化性和准确性，且无需在不同流动场景中重新训练。

Authors: Qisong Xiao, Xinhai Chen, Qinglin Wang, Xiaowei Guo, Binglin Wang, Weifeng Chen, Zhichao Wang, Yunfei Liu, Rui Xia, Hang Zou, Gencheng Liu, Shuai Li, Jie Liu

Explicit Credit Assignment through Local Rewards and Dependence Graphs in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): To promote cooperation in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, the reward signals of all agents can be aggregated together, forming global rewards that are commonly known as the fully cooperative setting. However, global rewards are usually noisy because they contain the contributions of all agents, which have to be resolved in the credit assignment process. On the other hand, using local reward benefits from faster learning due to the separation of agents' contributions, but can be suboptimal as agents myopically optimize their own reward while disregarding the global optimality. In this work, we propose a method that combines the merits of both approaches. By using a graph of interaction between agents, our method discerns the individual agent contribution in a more fine-grained manner than a global reward, while alleviating the cooperation problem with agents' local reward. We also introduce a practical approach for approximating such a graph. Our experiments demonstrate the flexibility of the approach, enabling improvements over the traditional local and global reward settings.

摘要（中文）: 为了促进多智能体强化学习中的合作，可以将所有智能体的奖励信号聚合在一起，形成全局奖励，这也被称为完全合作设置。然而，全局奖励通常是嘈杂的，因为它们包含了所有智能体的贡献，这些贡献必须在信用分配过程中解决。另一方面，由于智能体贡献的分离，使用局部奖励有利于更快的学习，但可能存在次优性，因为智能体会目光短浅地优化自身奖励，而忽略了全局最优性。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种结合两种方法优点的方案。通过使用智能体之间的交互图，我们的方法能够比全局奖励更精细地辨别单个智能体的贡献，同时缓解了智能体局部奖励带来的合作问题。我们还介绍了一种用于近似这种图的实用方法。我们的实验证明了该方法的灵活性，使其能够优于传统的局部和全局奖励设置。

TLDR: This paper presents a multi-agent reinforcement learning method that combines global and local rewards using an agent interaction graph for more fine-grained credit assignment, aiming to improve cooperation and performance.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种多智能体强化学习方法，该方法结合了全局和局部奖励，并使用智能体交互图进行更细粒度的信用分配，旨在提高协作和性能。

Authors: Bang Giang Le, Viet Cuong Ta

ETS: Energy-Guided Test-Time Scaling for Training-Free RL Alignment

Abstract (English): Reinforcement Learning (RL) post-training alignment for language models is effective, but also costly and unstable in practice, owing to its complicated training process. To address this, we propose a training-free inference method to sample directly from the optimal RL policy. The transition probability applied to Masked Language Modeling (MLM) consists of a reference policy model and an energy term. Based on this, our algorithm, Energy-Guided Test-Time Scaling (ETS), estimates the key energy term via online Monte Carlo, with a provable convergence rate. Moreover, to ensure practical efficiency, ETS leverages modern acceleration frameworks alongside tailored importance sampling estimators, substantially reducing inference latency while provably preserving sampling quality. Experiments on MLM (including autoregressive models and diffusion language models) across reasoning, coding, and science benchmarks show that our ETS consistently improves generation quality, validating its effectiveness and design.

摘要（中文）: 强化学习（RL）语言模型后训练对齐是有效的，但由于其复杂的训练过程，在实践中也成本高昂且不稳定。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种免训练的推理方法，可以直接从最优RL策略中采样。应用于Masked Language Modeling（MLM）的转移概率由一个参考策略模型和一个能量项组成。基于此，我们的算法，能量引导测试时缩放（ETS），通过在线蒙特卡洛估计关键的能量项，并具有可证明的收敛速度。此外，为了确保实际效率，ETS利用现代加速框架以及定制的 Importance Sampling 估计器，在可证明地保持采样质量的同时，大幅降低了推理延迟。在推理、编码和科学基准测试中，对MLM（包括自回归模型和扩散语言模型）的实验表明，我们的ETS持续提高了生成质量，验证了其有效性和设计。

TLDR: This paper introduces Energy-Guided Test-Time Scaling (ETS), a training-free inference method for aligning language models with RL policies, improving generation quality across various benchmarks.

Authors: Xiuyu Li, Jinkai Zhang, Mingyang Yi, Yu Li, Longqiang Wang, Yue Wang, Ju Fan

Best Arm Identification with LLM Judges and Limited Human

Abstract (English): We study fixed-confidence best-arm identification (BAI) where a cheap but potentially biased proxy (e.g., LLM judge) is available for every sample, while an expensive ground-truth label can only be acquired selectively when using a human for auditing. Unlike classical multi-fidelity BAI, the proxy is biased (arm- and context-dependent) and ground truth is selectively observed. Consequently, standard multi-fidelity methods can mis-select the best arm, and uniform auditing, though accurate, wastes scarce resources and is inefficient. We prove that without bias correction and propensity adjustment, mis-selection probability may not vanish (even with unlimited proxy data). We then develop an estimator for the mean of each arm that combines proxy scores with inverse-propensity-weighted residuals and form anytime-valid confidence sequences for that estimator. Based on the estimator and confidence sequence, we propose an algorithm that adaptively selects and audits arms. The algorithm concentrates audits on unreliable contexts and close arms and we prove that a plug-in Neyman rule achieves near-oracle audit efficiency. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical guarantees and demonstrate the superior empirical performance of the proposed algorithm.

摘要（中文）: 我们研究固定置信度最优臂识别(BAI)问题，其中每个样本都存在一个廉价但可能存在偏差的代理（例如，LLM评判），而只有在使用人工进行审计时才能选择性地获取昂贵的真实标签。与经典的多精度BAI不同，该代理存在偏差（与臂和上下文相关），并且ground truth是选择性观察的。因此，标准的多精度方法可能会错误地选择最优臂，而均匀审计虽然准确，但会浪费稀缺资源且效率低下。我们证明，如果没有偏差校正和倾向调整，错误选择概率可能不会消失（即使有无限的代理数据）。然后，我们开发了一种针对每个臂的均值的估计器，该估计器将代理得分与逆倾向加权残差相结合，并形成该估计器的随时有效的置信序列。基于该估计器和置信序列，我们提出了一种自适应选择和审计臂的算法。该算法将审计集中在不可靠的上下文和接近的臂上，并且我们证明了一个插入式Neyman规则实现了接近最优的审计效率。数值实验证实了理论保证，并证明了所提出的算法具有优越的经验性能。

TLDR: This paper addresses the problem of best-arm identification when using biased LLM judges as proxies for expensive ground truth labels, proposing an adaptive auditing algorithm with theoretical guarantees and empirical validation.

简要说明: 本文研究了在使用有偏的LLM作为ground truth的代理时，如何进行最佳臂识别的问题。提出了一种自适应审计算法，并提供了理论保证和实验验证。

Authors: Ruicheng Ao, Hongyu Chen, Siyang Gao, Hanwei Li, David Simchi-Levi

Few-Shot Learning for Dynamic Operations of Automated Electric Taxi Fleets under Evolving Charging Infrastructure: A Meta-Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach

Abstract (English): With the rapid expansion of electric vehicles (EVs) and charging infrastructure, the effective management of Autonomous Electric Taxi (AET) fleets faces a critical challenge in environments with dynamic and uncertain charging availability. While most existing research assumes a static charging network, this simplification creates a significant gap between theoretical models and real-world operations. To bridge this gap, we propose GAT-PEARL, a novel meta-reinforcement learning framework that learns an adaptive operational policy. Our approach integrates a graph attention network (GAT) to effectively extract robust spatial representations under infrastructure layouts and model the complex spatiotemporal relationships of the urban environment, and employs probabilistic embeddings for actor-critic reinforcement learning (PEARL) to enable rapid, inference-based adaptation to changes in charging network layouts without retraining. Through extensive simulations on real-world data in Chengdu, China, we demonstrate that GAT-PEARL significantly outperforms conventional reinforcement learning baselines, showing superior generalization to unseen infrastructure layouts and achieving higher overall operational efficiency in dynamic settings.

摘要（中文）: 随着电动汽车和充电基础设施的快速扩张，在充电可用性动态且不确定的环境中，有效管理自动驾驶电动出租车 (AET) 车队面临着严峻挑战。 尽管大多数现有研究都假设静态充电网络，但这种简化在理论模型和实际运营之间造成了巨大差距。 为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了一种新颖的元强化学习框架 GAT-PEARL，用于学习自适应运营策略。 我们的方法集成了一个图注意力网络 (GAT)，以有效地提取基础设施布局下的鲁棒空间表示，并对城市环境的复杂时空关系进行建模；并采用演员-评论家强化学习的概率嵌入 (PEARL)，从而无需重新训练即可实现基于推理的快速适应充电网络布局的改变。 通过在中国成都的真实世界数据上进行的大量仿真，我们证明了 GAT-PEARL 显著优于传统的强化学习基线，在未见的基础设施布局中展现出更强的泛化能力，并在动态环境中实现了更高的整体运营效率。

Authors: Xiaozhuang Li, Xindi Tang, Fang He

Achieving $\varepsilon^{-2}$ Dependence for Average-Reward Q-Learning with a New Contraction Principle

Abstract (English): We present the convergence rates of synchronous and asynchronous Q-learning for average-reward Markov decision processes, where the absence of contraction poses a fundamental challenge. Existing non-asymptotic results overcome this challenge by either imposing strong assumptions to enforce seminorm contraction or relying on discounted or episodic Markov decision processes as successive approximations, which either require unknown parameters or result in suboptimal sample complexity. In this work, under a reachability assumption, we establish optimal $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ sample complexity guarantees (up to logarithmic factors) for a simple variant of synchronous and asynchronous Q-learning that samples from the lazified dynamics, where the system remains in the current state with some fixed probability. At the core of our analysis is the construction of an instance-dependent seminorm and showing that, after a lazy transformation of the Markov decision process, the Bellman operator becomes one-step contractive under this seminorm.

摘要（中文）: 我们提出了针对平均奖励马尔可夫决策过程的同步和异步Q学习的收敛速度，其中缺乏收缩性构成了一个根本性的挑战。现有的非渐近结果通过施加强假设以强制执行半范数收缩，或者依赖于折扣或情景马尔可夫决策过程作为逐次逼近来克服这一挑战，但这两种方法要么需要未知的参数，要么导致次优的样本复杂度。在这项工作中，在可达性假设下，我们为同步和异步Q学习的一个简单变体建立了最优的$\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$样本复杂度保证（直到对数因子），该变体从懒化动力学中进行采样，其中系统以某个固定的概率保持在当前状态。我们分析的核心是构建一个与实例相关的半范数，并证明在对马尔可夫决策过程进行懒化变换后，贝尔曼算子在这个半范数下变成单步收缩的。

TLDR: This paper presents a modified Q-learning algorithm for average-reward Markov Decision Processes achieving optimal sample complexity of $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ by introducing a lazy transformation and a new contraction principle based on an instance-dependent seminorm.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种改进的平均奖励马尔可夫决策过程的 Q-learning 算法，通过引入惰性变换和基于实例相关半范数的新收缩原理，实现了 \$\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ 的最优样本复杂度。

Authors: Zijun Chen, Zaiwei Chen, Nian Si, Shengbo Wang

Model-Free Neural State Estimation in Nonlinear Dynamical Systems: A Comparative Study of Neural Architectures and Classical Filters

Abstract (English): Neural network models are increasingly used for state estimation in control and decision-making problems, yet it remains unclear to what extent they behave as principled filters in nonlinear dynamical systems. Unlike classical filters, which rely on explicit knowledge of system dynamics and noise models, neural estimators can be trained purely from data without access to the underlying system equations. In this work, we present a systematic empirical comparison between such model-free neural network models and classical filtering methods across multiple nonlinear scenarios. Our study evaluates Transformer-based models, state-space neural networks, and recurrent architectures alongside particle filters and nonlinear Kalman filters. The results show that neural models (in particular, state-space models (SSMs)) achieve state estimation performance that approaches strong nonlinear Kalman filters in nonlinear scenarios and outperform weaker classical baselines despite lacking access to system models, while also attaining substantially higher inference throughput.

摘要（中文）: 神经网络模型越来越多地应用于控制和决策问题中的状态估计，但它们在多大程度上表现得像非线性动力系统中的有原则的滤波器，仍不清楚。与依赖系统动力学和噪声模型的显式知识的经典滤波器不同，神经估计器仅可通过数据进行训练，无需访问底层系统方程。在这项工作中，我们在多种非线性场景中，对这种无模型的神经网络模型和经典滤波方法进行了系统的实证比较。我们的研究评估了基于 Transformer 的模型、状态空间神经网络、循环架构以及粒子滤波器和非线性卡尔曼滤波器。结果表明，神经网络模型（特别是状态空间模型（SSM））在非线性场景中实现了接近强大的非线性卡尔曼滤波器的状态估计性能，并且在缺乏对系统模型的访问权限的情况下，优于较弱的经典基线，同时还获得了显著更高的推理吞吐量。

TLDR: This paper compares the performance of various neural network architectures for state estimation in nonlinear dynamical systems against classical filtering methods, demonstrating that state-space neural networks (SSMs) can achieve comparable or better performance with higher throughput without requiring explicit system models.

简要说明: 本文比较了各种神经网络架构在非线性动力系统中的状态估计性能与经典滤波方法，结果表明，状态空间神经网络（SSM）可以在不需要显式系统模型的情况下，实现可比甚至更好的性能，并且具有更高的吞吐量。

Authors: Zhuochen Liu, Hans Walker, Rahul Jain

Distributional Active Inference

Abstract (English): Optimal control of complex environments with robotic systems faces two complementary and intertwined challenges: efficient organization of sensory state information and far-sighted action planning. Because the reinforcement learning framework addresses only the latter, it tends to deliver sample-inefficient solutions. Active inference is the state-of-the-art process theory that explains how biological brains handle this dual problem. However, its applications to artificial intelligence have thus far been limited to extensions of existing model-based approaches. We present a formal abstraction of reinforcement learning algorithms that spans model-based, distributional, and model-free approaches. This abstraction seamlessly integrates active inference into the distributional reinforcement learning framework, making its performance advantages accessible without transition dynamics modeling.

摘要（中文）: 利用机器人系统对复杂环境进行最优控制面临着两个互补且相互交织的挑战：高效地组织感觉状态信息和具有远见性的动作规划。 由于强化学习框架仅解决后者，因此往往会产生样本效率低下的解决方案。主动推断是目前最先进的过程理论，它解释了生物大脑如何处理这一双重问题。 然而，它在人工智能方面的应用迄今为止仅限于现有基于模型方法的扩展。 我们提出了一种强化学习算法的形式化抽象，涵盖了基于模型、分布和无模型的方法。 这种抽象将主动推断无缝集成到分布强化学习框架中，使其性能优势在无需转换动态建模的情况下即可实现。

TLDR: This paper integrates active inference, a biologically inspired process for efficient state representation and planning, into distributional reinforcement learning to improve sample efficiency without relying on transition dynamics modeling.

简要说明: 本文将主动推断（一种受生物学启发的，用于高效状态表示和规划的过程）集成到分布强化学习中，以提高样本效率，且不依赖于转移动态建模。

Authors: Abdullah Akgül, Gulcin Baykal, Manuel Haußmann, Mustafa Mert Çelikok, Melih Kandemir

4D-CAAL: 4D Radar-Camera Calibration and Auto-Labeling for Autonomous Driving

Abstract (English): 4D radar has emerged as a critical sensor for autonomous driving, primarily due to its enhanced capabilities in elevation measurement and higher resolution compared to traditional 3D radar. Effective integration of 4D radar with cameras requires accurate extrinsic calibration, and the development of radar-based perception algorithms demands large-scale annotated datasets. However, existing calibration methods often employ separate targets optimized for either visual or radar modalities, complicating correspondence establishment. Furthermore, manually labeling sparse radar data is labor-intensive and unreliable. To address these challenges, we propose 4D-CAAL, a unified framework for 4D radar-camera calibration and auto-labeling. Our approach introduces a novel dual-purpose calibration target design, integrating a checkerboard pattern on the front surface for camera detection and a corner reflector at the center of the back surface for radar detection. We develop a robust correspondence matching algorithm that aligns the checkerboard center with the strongest radar reflection point, enabling accurate extrinsic calibration. Subsequently, we present an auto-labeling pipeline that leverages the calibrated sensor relationship to transfer annotations from camera-based segmentations to radar point clouds through geometric projection and multi-feature optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves high calibration accuracy while significantly reducing manual annotation effort, thereby accelerating the development of robust multi-modal perception systems for autonomous driving.

摘要（中文）: 4D雷达已成为自动驾驶的关键传感器，这主要是由于其相比传统3D雷达在仰角测量和更高分辨率方面的增强能力。将4D雷达与相机有效集成需要精确的外参标定，而雷达感知算法的开发则需要大规模的标注数据集。然而，现有的标定方法通常采用为视觉或雷达模态分别优化的独立目标，这使得对应关系建立变得复杂。此外，手动标注稀疏雷达数据既费力又不可靠。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了4D-CAAL，一个用于4D雷达-相机标定和自动标注的统一框架。我们的方法引入了一种新型双用途标定目标设计，即在正面集成用于相机检测的棋盘格图案，并在背面中心集成用于雷达检测的角反射器。我们开发了一种稳健的对应关系匹配算法，该算法将棋盘格中心与最强的雷达反射点对齐，从而实现精确的外参标定。随后，我们提出了一个自动标注流程，该流程利用标定后的传感器关系，通过几何投影和多特征优化，将基于相机的分割标注迁移到雷达点云。大量实验表明，我们的方法实现了高标定精度，同时显著减少了手动标注工作量，从而加速了用于自动驾驶的稳健多模态感知系统的开发。

TLDR: The paper introduces 4D-CAAL, a unified framework for 4D radar-camera calibration and auto-labeling using a novel dual-purpose calibration target, aimed at improving multi-modal perception for autonomous driving.

Authors: Shanliang Yao, Zhuoxiao Li, Runwei Guan, Kebin Cao, Meng Xia, Fuping Hu, Sen Xu, Yong Yue, Xiaohui Zhu, Weiping Ding, Ryan Wen Liu

How do Visual Attributes Influence Web Agents? A Comprehensive Evaluation of User Interface Design Factors

Abstract (English): Web agents have demonstrated strong performance on a wide range of web-based tasks. However, existing research on the effect of environmental variation has mostly focused on robustness to adversarial attacks, with less attention to agents' preferences in benign scenarios. Although early studies have examined how textual attributes influence agent behavior, a systematic understanding of how visual attributes shape agent decision-making remains limited. To address this, we introduce VAF, a controlled evaluation pipeline for quantifying how webpage Visual Attribute Factors influence web-agent decision-making. Specifically, VAF consists of three stages: (i) variant generation, which ensures the variants share identical semantics as the original item while only differ in visual attributes; (ii) browsing interaction, where agents navigate the page via scrolling and clicking the interested item, mirroring how human users browse online; (iii) validating through both click action and reasoning from agents, which we use the Target Click Rate and Target Mention Rate to jointly evaluate the effect of visual attributes. By quantitatively measuring the decision-making difference between the original and variant, we identify which visual attributes influence agents' behavior most. Extensive experiments, across 8 variant families (48 variants total), 5 real-world websites (including shopping, travel, and news browsing), and 4 representative web agents, show that background color contrast, item size, position, and card clarity have a strong influence on agents' actions, whereas font styling, text color, and item image clarity exhibit minor effects.

摘要（中文）: 网络代理已经在各种基于网络的任务中展现出强大的性能。然而，现有关于环境变化影响的研究主要集中于对对抗性攻击的鲁棒性，而较少关注代理在良性场景中的偏好。尽管早期的研究已经考察了文本属性如何影响代理行为，但对于视觉属性如何塑造代理决策过程的系统性理解仍然有限。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了VAF，一个受控的评估流程，用于量化网页视觉属性因素如何影响网络代理的决策过程。具体来说，VAF由三个阶段组成：（i）变体生成，确保变体与原始项目具有相同的语义，仅在视觉属性上有所不同；（ii）浏览交互，代理通过滚动和点击感兴趣的项目来导航页面，模拟人类用户在线浏览的方式；（iii）通过点击行为和代理推理进行验证，我们使用目标点击率和目标提及率来共同评估视觉属性的影响。通过定量地衡量原始项目和变体之间的决策差异，我们识别出哪些视觉属性对代理的行为影响最大。广泛的实验，跨越8个变体族（总共48个变体），5个真实世界的网站（包括购物、旅游和新闻浏览）和4个具有代表性的网络代理，表明背景颜色对比度、项目大小、位置和卡片清晰度对代理的行为有很强的影响，而字体样式、文本颜色和项目图像清晰度则表现出较小的影响。

TLDR: This paper introduces VAF, a pipeline to systematically evaluate how visual attributes of webpages influence web agent decision-making, finding that background contrast, size, position, and card clarity are most influential.

Authors: Kuai Yu, Naicheng Yu, Han Wang, Rui Yang, Huan Zhang

Task-free Adaptive Meta Black-box Optimization

Abstract (English): Handcrafted optimizers become prohibitively inefficient for complex black-box optimization (BBO) tasks. MetaBBO addresses this challenge by meta-learning to automatically configure optimizers for low-level BBO tasks, thereby eliminating heuristic dependencies. However, existing methods typically require extensive handcrafted training tasks to learn meta-strategies that generalize to target tasks, which poses a critical limitation for realistic applications with unknown task distributions. To overcome the issue, we propose the Adaptive meta Black-box Optimization Model (ABOM), which performs online parameter adaptation using solely optimization data from the target task, obviating the need for predefined task distributions. Unlike conventional metaBBO frameworks that decouple meta-training and optimization phases, ABOM introduces a closed-loop adaptive parameter learning mechanism, where parameterized evolutionary operators continuously self-update by leveraging generated populations during optimization. This paradigm shift enables zero-shot optimization: ABOM achieves competitive performance on synthetic BBO benchmarks and realistic unmanned aerial vehicle path planning problems without any handcrafted training tasks. Visualization studies reveal that parameterized evolutionary operators exhibit statistically significant search patterns, including natural selection and genetic recombination.

摘要（中文）: 手工设计的优化器在处理复杂的黑盒优化（BBO）任务时变得异常低效。元黑盒优化（MetaBBO）通过元学习自动配置低级BBO任务的优化器来应对这一挑战，从而消除了启发式依赖。然而，现有方法通常需要大量手工设计的训练任务来学习泛化到目标任务的元策略，这对具有未知任务分布的实际应用构成了一个关键限制。为了克服这个问题，我们提出了一种自适应元黑盒优化模型（ABOM），该模型仅使用来自目标任务的优化数据执行在线参数自适应，从而无需预定义的任务分布。与将元训练和优化阶段解耦的传统MetaBBO框架不同，ABOM引入了一种闭环自适应参数学习机制，其中参数化的进化算子通过利用优化过程中生成的种群不断地自我更新。这种范式转变实现了零样本优化：ABOM在合成BBO基准和真实的无人机路径规划问题上取得了具有竞争力的性能，而无需任何手工设计的训练任务。可视化研究表明，参数化的进化算子表现出统计上显著的搜索模式，包括自然选择和基因重组。

TLDR: The paper introduces Adaptive meta Black-box Optimization Model (ABOM), a novel meta-learning approach that performs task-free, online parameter adaptation for black-box optimization, achieving competitive results without prior training tasks on both synthetic benchmarks and UAV path planning.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种自适应元黑盒优化模型 (ABOM)，这是一种新型的元学习方法，可对黑盒优化执行无任务的在线参数自适应，在合成基准和无人机路径规划方面无需事先训练任务即可获得具有竞争力的结果。

Authors: Chao Wang, Licheng Jiao, Lingling Li, Jiaxuan Zhao, Guanchun Wang, Fang Liu, Shuyuan Yang

BEAP-Agent: Backtrackable Execution and Adaptive Planning for GUI Agents

Abstract (English): GUI agents are designed to automate repetitive tasks and enhance productivity. However, existing GUI agents struggle to recover once they follow an incorrect exploration path, often leading to task failure. In this work, we model GUI task execution as a DFS process and propose BEAP-Agent, a DFS-based framework that supports long-range, multi-level state backtracking with dynamic task tracking and updating. The framework consists of three collaborative components: Planner, Executor, and Tracker. Together, they enable effective task exploration and execution. BEAP-Agent fills the gap in systematic backtracking mechanisms for GUI agents, offering a systematic solution for long-horizon task exploration. We conducted a systematic evaluation on the OSWorld benchmark, where BEAP-Agent achieved an accuracy of 28.2%, validating the effectiveness of the proposed method.

摘要（中文）: 图形用户界面（GUI）代理旨在自动化重复性任务并提高生产力。然而，现有的GUI代理一旦沿着错误的探索路径，就难以恢复，通常导致任务失败。本文将GUI任务执行建模为深度优先搜索（DFS）过程，并提出BEAP-Agent，一种基于DFS的框架，支持长程、多层状态回溯，同时进行动态任务跟踪和更新。该框架由三个协同组件组成：规划器（Planner）、执行器（Executor）和跟踪器（Tracker）。它们共同实现有效的任务探索和执行。BEAP-Agent填补了GUI代理系统性回溯机制的空白，为长时程任务探索提供了一个系统性的解决方案。我们在OSWorld基准测试上进行了系统评估，BEAP-Agent取得了28.2%的准确率，验证了所提出方法的有效性。

TLDR: BEAP-Agent is a DFS-based framework for GUI task automation that supports backtracking and dynamic task updating, improving accuracy on the OSWorld benchmark compared to existing methods.

Authors: Ziyu Lu, Tengjin Weng, Yiying Yang, Yuhang Zhao, Xinxin Huang, Wenhao Jiang

BLO-Inst: Bi-Level Optimization Based Alignment of YOLO and SAM for Robust Instance Segmentation

Abstract (English): The Segment Anything Model has revolutionized image segmentation with its zero-shot capabilities, yet its reliance on manual prompts hinders fully automated deployment. While integrating object detectors as prompt generators offers a pathway to automation, existing pipelines suffer from two fundamental limitations: objective mismatch, where detectors optimized for geometric localization do not correspond to the optimal prompting context required by SAM, and alignment overfitting in standard joint training, where the detector simply memorizes specific prompt adjustments for training samples rather than learning a generalizable policy. To bridge this gap, we introduce BLO-Inst, a unified framework that aligns detection and segmentation objectives by bi-level optimization. We formulate the alignment as a nested optimization problem over disjoint data splits. In the lower level, the SAM is fine-tuned to maximize segmentation fidelity given the current detection proposals on a subset ($D_1$). In the upper level, the detector is updated to generate bounding boxes that explicitly minimize the validation loss of the fine-tuned SAM on a separate subset ($D_2$). This effectively transforms the detector into a segmentation-aware prompt generator, optimizing the bounding boxes not just for localization accuracy, but for downstream mask quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BLO-Inst achieves superior performance, outperforming standard baselines on tasks in general and biomedical domains.

摘要（中文）: 分割一切模型凭借其零样本能力彻底改变了图像分割领域，但其对人工提示的依赖阻碍了完全自动化的部署。 将目标检测器集成作为提示生成器为自动化提供了一条路径，但现有的流程存在两个根本性限制：目标不匹配，即为几何定位优化的检测器与SAM所需的最佳提示上下文不符；以及标准联合训练中的对齐过拟合，即检测器简单地记忆训练样本的特定提示调整，而不是学习可泛化的策略。 为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了BLO-Inst，一个通过双层优化对齐检测和分割目标的统一框架。 我们将对齐表述为一个关于不相交数据分割的嵌套优化问题。 在底层，SAM被微调，以在给定子集($D_1$)上当前检测建议的情况下最大限度地提高分割保真度。 在顶层，检测器被更新，以生成显式最小化在单独子集($D_2$)上微调SAM验证损失的边界框。 这有效地将检测器转换为分割感知的提示生成器，优化边界框不仅为了定位精度，还为了下游掩模质量。 广泛的实验表明，BLO-Inst 实现了卓越的性能，优于通用和生物医学领域任务的标准基线。

TLDR: The paper introduces BLO-Inst, a bi-level optimization framework to align object detection and SAM segmentation objectives, improving automated instance segmentation by training the detector to be a segmentation-aware prompt generator.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了BLO-Inst，一个双层优化框架，旨在对齐目标检测和SAM分割目标，通过训练检测器成为一个分割感知的提示生成器，从而提高自动实例分割的性能。

Authors: Li Zhang, Pengtao Xie

PLANING: A Loosely Coupled Triangle-Gaussian Framework for Streaming 3D Reconstruction

Abstract (English): Streaming reconstruction from monocular image sequences remains challenging, as existing methods typically favor either high-quality rendering or accurate geometry, but rarely both. We present PLANING, an efficient on-the-fly reconstruction framework built on a hybrid representation that loosely couples explicit geometric primitives with neural Gaussians, enabling geometry and appearance to be modeled in a decoupled manner. This decoupling supports an online initialization and optimization strategy that separates geometry and appearance updates, yielding stable streaming reconstruction with substantially reduced structural redundancy. PLANING improves dense mesh Chamfer-L2 by 18.52% over PGSR, surpasses ARTDECO by 1.31 dB PSNR, and reconstructs ScanNetV2 scenes in under 100 seconds, over 5x faster than 2D Gaussian Splatting, while matching the quality of offline per-scene optimization. Beyond reconstruction quality, the structural clarity and computational efficiency of \modelname~make it well suited for a broad range of downstream applications, such as enabling large-scale scene modeling and simulation-ready environments for embodied AI. Project page: https://city-super.github.io/PLANING/ .

摘要（中文）: 从单目图像序列进行流式重建仍然具有挑战性，因为现有方法通常倾向于高质量渲染或精确几何，但很少两者兼顾。我们提出了PLANING，一个高效的即时重建框架，它建立在混合表示之上，该混合表示将显式几何图元与神经高斯松散耦合，从而能够以解耦的方式对几何和外观进行建模。这种解耦支持一种在线初始化和优化策略，它将几何和外观更新分开，从而产生具有显著减少的结构冗余的稳定流式重建。PLANING的密集网格Chamfer-L2比PGSR提高了18.52%，PSNR超过ARTDECO 1.31 dB，并在100秒内重建ScanNetV2场景，比2D高斯溅射快5倍以上，同时达到离线单场景优化的质量。除了重建质量之外，PLANING的结构清晰度和计算效率使其非常适合广泛的下游应用，例如支持大规模场景建模和用于具身AI的模拟就绪环境。项目主页：https://city-super.github.io/PLANING/ 。

TLDR: The paper presents PLANING, a novel streaming 3D reconstruction framework that uses a hybrid triangle-Gaussian representation to achieve both high-quality rendering and accurate geometry, exceeding existing methods in speed and performance.

Authors: Changjian Jiang, Kerui Ren, Xudong Li, Kaiwen Song, Linning Xu, Tao Lu, Junting Dong, Yu Zhang, Bo Dai, Mulin Yu

BadDet+: Robust Backdoor Attacks for Object Detection

Abstract (English): Backdoor attacks pose a severe threat to deep learning, yet their impact on object detection remains poorly understood compared to image classification. While attacks have been proposed, we identify critical weaknesses in existing detection-based methods, specifically their reliance on unrealistic assumptions and a lack of physical validation. To bridge this gap, we introduce BadDet+, a penalty-based framework that unifies Region Misclassification Attacks (RMA) and Object Disappearance Attacks (ODA). The core mechanism utilizes a log-barrier penalty to suppress true-class predictions for triggered inputs, resulting in (i) position and scale invariance, and (ii) enhanced physical robustness. On real-world benchmarks, BadDet+ achieves superior synthetic-to-physical transfer compared to existing RMA and ODA baselines while preserving clean performance. Theoretical analysis confirms the proposed penalty acts within a trigger-specific feature subspace, reliably inducing attacks without degrading standard inference. These results highlight significant vulnerabilities in object detection and the necessity for specialized defenses.

摘要（中文）: 后门攻击对深度学习构成严重威胁，但与图像分类相比，其对目标检测的影响仍未得到充分理解。虽然已经提出了一些攻击方法，但我们发现现有基于检测的方法存在关键缺陷，特别是它们依赖于不切实际的假设，并且缺乏物理验证。为了弥补这一差距，我们引入了 BadDet+，一个基于惩罚的框架，它统一了区域误分类攻击（RMA）和目标消失攻击（ODA）。其核心机制是利用对数障碍惩罚来抑制触发输入的真实类别预测，从而实现（i）位置和尺度的不变性，以及（ii）增强的物理鲁棒性。在真实世界的基准测试中，与现有的 RMA 和 ODA 基线相比，BadDet+ 实现了卓越的合成到物理迁移，同时保持了干净的性能。理论分析证实，所提出的惩罚作用于特定于触发器的特征子空间内，可靠地诱导攻击，而不会降低标准推理性能。这些结果突出了目标检测的重大漏洞以及对专门防御的需求。

TLDR: The paper introduces BadDet+, a novel framework for backdoor attacks on object detection models that overcomes limitations of existing methods by using a log-barrier penalty to achieve position/scale invariance and enhanced physical robustness, demonstrating significant vulnerabilities in object detection.

Authors: Kealan Dunnett, Reza Arablouei, Dimity Miller, Volkan Dedeoglu, Raja Jurdak

EGAM: Extended Graph Attention Model for Solving Routing Problems

Abstract (English): Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) solvers, implemented with graph neural networks (GNNs), have introduced new approaches for solving routing problems. Trained with reinforcement learning (RL), the state-of-the-art graph attention model (GAM) achieves near-optimal solutions without requiring expert knowledge or labeled data. In this work, we generalize the existing graph attention mechanism and propose the extended graph attention model (EGAM). Our model utilizes multi-head dot-product attention to update both node and edge embeddings, addressing the limitations of the conventional GAM, which considers only node features. We employ an autoregressive encoder-decoder architecture and train it with policy gradient algorithms that incorporate a specially designed baseline. Experiments show that EGAM matches or outperforms existing methods across various routing problems. Notably, the proposed model demonstrates exceptional performance on highly constrained problems, highlighting its efficiency in handling complex graph structures.

摘要（中文）: 使用图神经网络(GNNs)实现的神经组合优化(NCO)求解器，为解决路径规划问题引入了新的方法。通过强化学习(RL)训练，最先进的图注意力模型(GAM)可以在不需要专家知识或标注数据的情况下获得接近最优的解。在这项工作中，我们推广了现有的图注意力机制，并提出了扩展图注意力模型(EGAM)。我们的模型利用多头点积注意力来更新节点和边的嵌入，解决了传统GAM仅考虑节点特征的局限性。我们采用自回归编码器-解码器架构，并使用包含专门设计的基线的策略梯度算法对其进行训练。实验表明，EGAM在各种路径规划问题上与现有方法相匹配或超越了现有方法。值得注意的是，所提出的模型在高度约束的问题上表现出卓越的性能，突显了其处理复杂图结构的能力。

TLDR: The paper introduces EGAM, an extended graph attention model for solving routing problems using reinforcement learning. EGAM improves upon existing graph attention models by incorporating edge embeddings, leading to better performance, especially on highly constrained problems.

Authors: Licheng Wang, Yuzi Yan, Mingtao Huang, Yuan Shen

TRACE: Trajectory Recovery for Continuous Mechanism Evolution in Causal Representation Learning

Abstract (English): Temporal causal representation learning methods assume that causal mechanisms switch instantaneously between discrete domains, yet real-world systems often exhibit continuous mechanism transitions. For example, a vehicle's dynamics evolve gradually through a turning maneuver, and human gait shifts smoothly from walking to running. We formalize this setting by modeling transitional mechanisms as convex combinations of finitely many atomic mechanisms, governed by time-varying mixing coefficients. Our theoretical contributions establish that both the latent causal variables and the continuous mixing trajectory are jointly identifiable. We further propose TRACE, a Mixture-of-Experts framework where each expert learns one atomic mechanism during training, enabling recovery of mechanism trajectories at test time. This formulation generalizes to intermediate mechanism states never observed during training. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data demonstrate that TRACE recovers mixing trajectories with up to 0.99 correlation, substantially outperforming discrete-switching baselines.

摘要（中文）: 时间因果表征学习方法假设因果机制在离散域之间瞬时切换，然而真实世界系统通常表现出连续的机制转换。例如，车辆的动力学在转弯操作过程中逐渐演变，并且人类步态从行走平滑地过渡到跑步。我们通过将过渡机制建模为有限个原子机制的凸组合，并由时变混合系数控制，来形式化这个设置。我们的理论贡献证明了潜在因果变量和连续混合轨迹是联合可识别的。我们进一步提出了TRACE，一个混合专家框架，其中每个专家在训练期间学习一个原子机制，从而能够在测试时恢复机制轨迹。该公式推广到训练期间从未观察到的中间机制状态。在合成数据和真实世界数据上的实验表明，TRACE 恢复的混合轨迹相关性高达 0.99，显著优于离散切换基线。

TLDR: The paper introduces TRACE, a method for temporal causal representation learning that models continuous mechanism transitions as convex combinations of atomic mechanisms, demonstrating its ability to recover mixing trajectories with high accuracy. It outperforms discrete-switching baselines on synthetic and real-world data.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了TRACE，一种时间因果表示学习方法，它将连续机制转换建模为原子机制的凸组合，并展示了其高精度恢复混合轨迹的能力。在合成数据和真实数据上，它的表现优于离散切换基线。

Authors: Shicheng Fan, Kun Zhang, Lu Cheng

MADE: Benchmark Environments for Closed-Loop Materials Discovery

Abstract (English): Existing benchmarks for computational materials discovery primarily evaluate static predictive tasks or isolated computational sub-tasks. While valuable, these evaluations neglect the inherently iterative and adaptive nature of scientific discovery. We introduce MAterials Discovery Environments (MADE), a novel framework for benchmarking end-to-end autonomous materials discovery pipelines. MADE simulates closed-loop discovery campaigns in which an agent or algorithm proposes, evaluates, and refines candidate materials under a constrained oracle budget, capturing the sequential and resource-limited nature of real discovery workflows. We formalize discovery as a search for thermodynamically stable compounds relative to a given convex hull, and evaluate efficacy and efficiency via comparison to baseline algorithms. The framework is flexible; users can compose discovery agents from interchangeable components such as generative models, filters, and planners, enabling the study of arbitrary workflows ranging from fixed pipelines to fully agentic systems with tool use and adaptive decision making. We demonstrate this by conducting systematic experiments across a family of systems, enabling ablation of components in discovery pipelines, and comparison of how methods scale with system complexity.

摘要（中文）: 现有计算材料发现的基准测试主要评估静态预测任务或孤立的计算子任务。这些评估虽然有价值，但忽略了科学发现固有的迭代和自适应性质。我们引入了材料发现环境（MADE），这是一个用于基准测试端到端自主材料发现流程的新框架。MADE模拟了闭环发现活动，其中代理或算法在受限的预言机预算下提出、评估和改进候选材料，从而捕捉了真实发现工作流程的顺序性和资源限制性。我们将发现形式化为相对于给定凸包的热力学稳定化合物的搜索，并通过与基线算法的比较来评估其有效性和效率。该框架具有灵活性；用户可以从可互换的组件（如生成模型、过滤器和规划器）组成发现代理，从而能够研究从固定流程到具有工具使用和自适应决策的全代理系统的任意工作流程。我们通过对一系列系统进行系统实验来证明这一点，实现了对发现流程中组件的消融研究，并比较了方法如何随系统复杂性的变化而扩展。

TLDR: The paper introduces MADE, a benchmark environment for closed-loop materials discovery, allowing researchers to test end-to-end autonomous discovery pipelines by simulating iterative refinement of materials under resource constraints.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了MADE，一个用于闭环材料发现的基准环境，允许研究人员通过模拟在资源约束下迭代改进材料，来测试端到端的自主发现流程。
